## Berkas: AI_WORKSPACE_RENDERING_FIX_REPORT.md
*Lokasi Asli:* `D:\NEXUS\Nexus-CSharp\docs\AI_WORKSPACE_RENDERING_FIX_REPORT.md`

# NEXUS AI WORKSPACE — RENDERING FIX

Tanggal: 2026-10-03. Scope: rendering Chat AI Workspace.

## FINAL STATUS: PARTIAL

Fix fungsional yang diimplementasikan lulus build dan **225/225 test relevan**.
Rendering diverifikasi pada WPF window aktual dengan fixture deterministik,
termasuk resize dan command sidebar. Status keseluruhan tetap PARTIAL karena
code block native FlowDocument Table masih bersudut persegi, dukungan markdown
existing belum setara seluruh CommonMark, dan streaming LLM live tidak diuji.

## RENDERING ISSUES FOUND

- P0: composer dibatasi 760 DIP sementara reading frame chat 860 DIP;
  scrollbar memakai viewport berbeda. Greeting tidak wrap pada viewport sempit.
- P0/P2: compact composer mengurangi lebar input berdasarkan rekonstruksi chrome
  dengan konstanta 28, sehingga input/placeholder dapat terdesak toolbar.
- P1: Document dan FallbackText mengganti seluruh RichTextBox/FlowDocument;
  selection dan blok yang sudah selesai tidak memiliki identitas stabil.
- P1: fenced code baru dikenali setelah closing fence; selama streaming tampil
  sebagai paragraph, lalu berubah bentuk saat fence selesai.
- P1: setiap perubahan token memaksa scroll ke bawah meskipun pengguna membaca
  bagian lama. Callback scroll juga tidak dikoaleskan.
- P1: sidebar selalu memulai animasi dari 0/260, bukan lebar animasi aktual;
  pembalikan animasi dapat melompat.
- P2: code copy mengganti label Salin menjadi Tersalin lalu Salin kode, mengubah
  panjang konten header. Indentasi kode pertama dibuang oleh Trim().
- P2: nested bold/italic menampilkan marker literal; strike dan escape belum
  direpresentasikan dengan benar; heading H4–H6 disamakan dengan H3.
- P2: indent nested list dibuang; ordered list selalu mulai dari 1.
- P2: alignment separator tabel dibuang. Pengukuran user table menggunakan
  cell terlebar alih-alih total kolom; indent list/quote juga tidak dihitung tepat.
- P2: ChoiceBlock hanya menampilkan pertanyaan dalam UI container non-selectable;
  opsi tidak terlihat di pesan setelah composer choice selesai. Copy pesan juga
  menghilangkan choice dan menyisakan marker nested emphasis.
- P2: border tabel/code/choice light theme terlalu samar pada snapshot.
  Hyperlink memakai warna bawaan WPF alih-alih accent NEXUS.

## ISSUES PREVIOUS AUDIT MISSED

Audit sebelumnya tidak dilampirkan sebagai daftar temuan yang dapat dibandingkan.
Tidak ada klaim bahwa suatu temuan pasti terlewat. Temuan independen tambahan:
copy feedback mengubah panjang header, hilangnya opsi choice pada pesan/copy,
lebar scrollbar NEXUS berbeda dari scrollbar sistem, ordered start dan table
alignment hilang, serta animasi sidebar menggunakan endpoint tetap.

## ISSUES VERIFIED FROM GEMINI

Tidak dapat diatribusikan: laporan audit Gemini spesifik tidak tersedia dalam
permintaan. Seluruh temuan di atas berasal dari source aktual dan/atau runtime WPF.

## CURRENT VS TARGET

Current di bawah berarti keadaan source sebelum perubahan sesi ini.

| Area | Current behavior | Problem | Target native behavior | Fix |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Paragraph | Native Paragraph dalam RichTextBox baru setiap update | Selection/tree churn | Paragraph selectable dengan identitas stabil | Reuse document dan update inline yang berubah |
| Heading | Native Paragraph; level di-clamp ke 3 | H4–H6 kehilangan hierarki | Level 1–6 memakai typography resource | Pertahankan level, mapping token H4–H6 |
| List | Native List flat; nomor mulai 1 | Nested indent dan ordered start hilang | List bertingkat dan indent native | Simpan indent/start, bangun child List pada ListItem |
| Quote | Native Section, left border 3, padding 10 | Tidak ditemukan clipping pada fixture; width probe mengabaikan indent | Native multiline wrapping dan selection | Tetap Section; width probe menyertakan block chrome |
| Code | Native Table selectable; header copy; fence harus lengkap | Late transition, trim indent, copy label bergeser, code size bukan token code | Header stabil, monospace, native wrapping, theme-aware | Open fence, preserve indent, token code size, separator header, feedback glyph/tooltip |
| Table | Native Auto columns, header weight, border 0.5 | Alignment hilang, size probe salah, light border samar | Native table wrapping dan alignment | Alignment pada Paragraph, total column width, NexusContentBorder |
| Inline code | Native Run, code font dan surface background | Style dapat bocor saat jenis inline berubah pada update | Baseline native, style hanya untuk inline code | Ganti inline bila jenis berubah |
| Bold | Bold membungkus Run mentah | Nested marker literal | Nested Bold/Italic spans | Render isi emphasis secara rekursif |
| Italic | Italic membungkus Run mentah | Nested delimiter salah | Native Italic dan nested Bold | Closing delimiter dan recursive spans |
| Strike | Plain text dengan ~~ | Tidak dirender | Native TextDecorations.Strikethrough | StrikeInline dan Span |
| Choice | UI card question-only | Opsi/selection/copy hilang | Pertanyaan dan opsi tetap terbaca/selectable | Native Section dan opsi; plain-text copy lengkap |
| User bubble | Right aligned, max 680, selectable | Width probe approximate untuk mixed fonts/table/list | Short bubble shrink, long bubble wrap | WPF TextBlock shaping dan per-block chrome; batas 680 tetap |
| Assistant content | Transparent bubble, one document | Entire document diganti | Content-oriented selectable document | Reuse RichTextBox/FlowDocument; chrome transparan tetap |
| Streaming | Rebuild tree, forced tail scroll | Churn, selection reset, scroll melawan pengguna | Update blok yang berubah, follow tail opt-in by position | Incremental updates, coalesced layout-time scroll |
| Composer | Single host; 760 vs chat 860; FormattedText dan chrome subtraction | Boundary mismatch, narrow input clipping | Shared reading token/gutter, native column width | Shared max width, actual scroll viewport, native TextBlock measure, narrow two-row layout |

## FIXES IMPLEMENTED

Path source relatif terhadap `src/03_App/Nexus.App/`, kecuali dinyatakan lain.

### 1. Reading boundary dan empty state
- **File:** `Views/ConversationView.xaml`, `Views/ConversationView.xaml.cs`.
- **Element:** AIComposerHost, AIInputBorder, AIChatReadingFrame, AIWelcomeTitle.
- **Observed problem:** max width 760/860 berbeda; greeting tidak wrap; scrollbar
  mengurangi ruang chat saja.
- **Root cause:** dua batas lebar independen dan area layout yang berbeda.
- **Change:** kedua host memakai NexusAIWorkspaceReadingFrameMaxWidth dan responsive
  gutter existing. Composer memakai selisih ActualWidth–ViewportWidth scroll yang
  dihitung WPF. Scrollbar mempunyai reserved lane stabil. Greeting wrap; composer
  tetap satu instance dan greeting tetap dalam host yang sama.
- **Why minimal:** binding/resource dan event layout existing; tanpa layout service
  atau composer duplikat. Teks welcome tetap persis sama.
- **Verification:** kedua tepi frame/composer cocok dalam toleransi 1 DIP pada
  1440, 1200, 900, 780, 600, Light/Dark, sidebar expand/collapse dan resize.

### 2. Composer native measurement
- **File:** `Views/ConversationView.xaml`, `Views/ConversationView.xaml.cs`.
- **Element:** AIComposerLayout, TxtAIInput dan placeholder.
- **Observed problem:** lebar compact direkonstruksi dari padding/action chrome
  dengan angka 28; placeholder terpotong ketika ruang input terlalu kecil.
- **Root cause:** approximation tidak mengikuti kolom Grid aktual.
- **Change:** ukur text dengan TextBlock WPF; bandingkan dengan actual star column
  dan margin child. Empty input memakai lebar placeholder sebagai kebutuhan minimum.
- **Why minimal:** pertahankan layout dua mode dan IsComposerExpanded existing.
- **Verification:** existing composer expansion/clear test; snapshot 600/sidebar
  menunjukkan input full-width di atas action row.

### 3. Stable streaming visual tree
- **File:** `AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`.
- **Element:** RichTextBox, FlowDocument, Paragraph/Run dan code Table.
- **Observed problem:** setiap callback membuat ulang semua kontrol/block.
- **Root cause:** RebuildContent selalu mengganti Content, termasuk fallback token
  yang sudah mempunyai parsed Document.
- **Change:** simpan satu document/control; abaikan fallback saat Document tersedia;
  bandingkan block content; retain blok identik, update growing plain Run dan code
  Run, replace hanya blok/inline yang perlu. Code copy menyimpan source terbaru.
- **Why minimal:** logika berada dalam presenter existing, tanpa renderer/state
  service baru dan tanpa mengubah scheduler/runtime.
- **Verification:** document/control/completed block/growing Run identity; selection
  completed paragraph bertahan; code Table dan hyperlink bertahan selama fence selesai.

### 4. Code fidelity dan copy header
- **File:** `AIWorkspace/Markdown/NexusMessageParser.cs`,
  `AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`.
- **Element:** fenced code, language/copy header, code paragraph.
- **Observed problem:** open fence belum code; Trim membuang indent; copy feedback
  mengubah lebar teks header; code font size mewarisi body.
- **Root cause:** regex mensyaratkan closing fence, trim seluruh whitespace, label
  feedback berbeda panjang, font size code tidak diset.
- **Change:** recognize open fence sampai EOF; preserve horizontal whitespace;
  monospace/code-size token; native header divider; feedback memakai glyph/tooltip
  dengan label tetap; native Auto columns.
- **Why minimal:** perubahan lokal pada regex/trim dan builder native existing.
- **Verification:** existing code copy/ASCII/selection tests dan updated-code copy;
  label bahasa satu visual line pada seluruh fixture viewport.
- **Remaining:** container native Table masih persegi. Mengganti dengan UI island
  rounded akan membutuhkan desain selection lintas blok yang berbeda.

### 5. Inline, heading dan list fidelity
- **File:** `AIWorkspace/Markdown/NexusMessageParser.cs`,
  `AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`.
- **Element:** inline builders, headings, native List/ListItem.
- **Observed problem:** nested markers literal, strike/escape mentah, H4–H6 sama,
  nested list flat dan ordered numbering mulai 1.
- **Root cause:** emphasis dibungkus Run mentah; metadata level/indent/start dibuang.
- **Change:** recursive native spans; minimal delimiter/escape/strike handling;
  simpan level, indent dan ordered start; token heading 1–6 dan child native List.
- **Why minimal:** memakai parser/model dan builders existing; tanpa dependency
  markdown atau parser architecture baru.
- **Verification:** nested Bold/Italic/Strike/escape test, tiga tingkat list,
  ordered start test, existing parser suite, snapshots.
- **Remaining:** mixed ordered/unordered nesting dan multiline list continuation
  masih keterbatasan parser existing; tidak mengklaim dukungan semua CommonMark.

### 6. Tables, theme dan user width
- **File:** `AIWorkspace/Markdown/NexusMessageParser.cs`,
  `AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`.
- **Element:** TableCell Paragraph alignment, renderer borders, hyperlink, MeasureOverride.
- **Observed problem:** table alignment hilang, cell-max width bukan total columns,
  mixed typography memakai manual per-run FormattedText; light border terlalu samar.
- **Root cause:** metadata alignment dibuang dan probe layout menduplikasi sebagian
  perhitungan text engine/chrome, memakai subtle legacy border.
- **Change:** preserve alignment; native Paragraph.TextAlignment; native TextBlock
  shaping mengganti FormattedText; total kolom dan block padding/border dipakai
  sekali, hapus recursive global chrome collector; NexusContentBorder dan accent
  hyperlink existing dipakai melalui DynamicResource.
- **Why minimal:** native Table tetap; tidak ada table engine/global token change.
- **Verification:** alignment/theme-resource tests, user short/long wrapping,
  ASCII width regressions, Light/Dark snapshots.
- **Necessary measurement:** SizeToContent tetap dibutuhkan karena RichTextBox
  mengisi constraint width. Allowance formatter 24 DIP tetap: eksperimen menghapus
  allowance gagal pada word Halo dan ketiga ASCII regression tests. Ini bukan
  padding dekoratif; removal ditolak berdasarkan runtime evidence.

### 7. Choice dan copy message
- **File:** `AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`,
  `AIWorkspace/Markdown/MarkdownPlainText.cs`.
- **Element:** choice in message dan copy plain text.
- **Observed problem:** opsi tidak dirender; UI question tidak selectable;
  copy menghilangkan choice dan menyisakan nested formatting markers.
- **Root cause:** question-only BlockUIContainer dan flatten inline.Text mentah.
- **Change:** native selectable Section memuat question/options; copy memuat opsi,
  recursive emphasis text, indent list dan start numbering.
- **Why minimal:** composer choice interaction/commands tetap memakai implementasi
  existing; hanya visual content dan copy formatting diperbaiki.
- **Verification:** options ada di selection dan plain-text copy, snapshots.

### 8. Scroll dan sidebar visual behavior
- **File:** `Views/ConversationView.xaml`, `Views/ConversationView.xaml.cs`.
- **Element:** AIChatScroll dan AIConversationPanel animation.
- **Observed problem:** setiap Content token scroll ke bottom; callback berulang;
  sidebar reversal mulai dari endpoint tetap.
- **Root cause:** unconditional dispatch, tidak mengikuti scroll intent/current width.
- **Change:** follow tail hanya saat berada di bottom; update pada parsed document
  dan extent change; coalesce dispatch pada Loaded/layout priority; user send/reset
  mengembalikan tail following. Sidebar mulai dari current actual width.
- **Why minimal:** local view fields/events dan animation existing; runtime tidak berubah.
- **Verification:** long-response test untuk tail, scroll-up, appended tokens dan
  kembali bottom; sidebar command expand/collapse dan window resize pada fixture.

## ISSUES REJECTED

- Menghapus seluruh SizeToContent measurement: RichTextBox native tidak shrink
  untuk short bubble; regression test membuktikan allowance formatter masih perlu.
- Mengganti FlowDocument dengan ItemsControl/per-block UI controls demi rounded
  code card: akan menghilangkan native selection lintas paragraph/list/quote/code/table.
- Mengubah quote, action-row hover geometry atau assistant transparent chrome:
  existing source/fixture tidak membuktikan masalah; hover row memakai opacity dan
  mempertahankan layout slot.
- Mengubah global theme token: existing NexusContentBorder sudah mencukupi.
- Menambah markdown/editor dependency, virtualization atau layout service: tidak diperlukan.

## REGRESSION

Tidak ditemukan regression pada 225 test relevan. Cross-block native selection
tetap ada; tidak ada migration message content ke ItemsControl. Eksperimen
penghapusan formatter allowance dan Grid star column ditolak/dikoreksi setelah
runtime tests/snapshot menemukan wrap/header artifacts. Final fixtures lulus.

Batas hasil: code/choice native containers persegi; long code memakai native
wrapping, bukan horizontal code scroller; parser existing bukan seluruh CommonMark
(contoh: mixed-kind nested lists dan escaped pipe dalam tabel); live LLM streaming
dan interaksi physical-mouse belum diverifikasi. Tidak mengklaim seluruh task PASS.

## NOT TOUCHED

Engine / llama.cpp / model selection logic / conversation runtime / prompt logic /
persistence / storage / database / history / agent architecture / AI Check /
image pipeline / engine services / network architecture / unrelated modules.
Subsystem terkait dapat ikut dikompilasi melalui ProjectReference, tetapi source-nya
tidak diedit oleh sesi rendering ini.

Components reused: all existing service/test paths. Production components added/refactored: none. Dependencies changed: none. New files: three requested documents plus execution logs/TRX/blame artifacts.

Build: **PASS**. Tests: **FAIL / ABORTED**, with subsets PASS. Regression Gate: **FAIL / incomplete**. Existing errors listed above are pre-Agent working-tree baseline errors; not automatically classified as regressions against earlier commits.

Performance impact: only verification/build/model loading workload; measured baseline figures above. Security impact: findings documented; no permissions/tool exposure added. Known issues: WPF host collision, governance failures, model fixture coupling, performance gates, unverified live E2E.

Checkpoint: audit branch created, working tree preserved, no commit. Rollback of documentation is scoped removal of authored documents/artifacts; no broad git reset/clean to remove user work. Native smoke closed its process through existing UI and confirmed cleanup.

Next recommended phase: review audit, fix baseline blockers surgically, rerun whole solution until complete, execute missing live E2E inventory. **No Phase 1 automatically started.**

<a id="final-implementation-plan-v10-ai-workspace-typography-markdown-renderermd"></a>
## Berkas: Final_Implementation_Plan_v1.0_AI_Workspace_Typography_Markdown_Renderer.md
*Lokasi Asli:* `D:\NEXUS\Nexus-CSharp\docs\Final_Implementation_Plan_v1.0_AI_Workspace_Typography_Markdown_Renderer.md`

# Final Implementation Plan v1.0 — AI Workspace Typography System & Markdown Renderer v1

## Executive Summary

**Tujuan:** Menyelesaikan Typography System AI Workspace dan Markdown Renderer v1 pada Nexus-CSharp.

**Scope:**
- Memusatkan seluruh pengaturan tipografi AI Workspace ke satu source of truth bersama Nexus.
- Memperluas parser dan renderer Markdown yang sudah ada, yaitu `NexusMessageParser` dan `MessageContentPresenter`.
- Menambahkan dukungan Heading, Horizontal Rule, dan Task List.
- Memperbarui warna teks Light Mode seluruh Nexus agar lebih hitam, kontras, dan nyaman dibaca.
- Tidak menyentuh modul di luar scope.

**Root cause:**
- Sistem tipografi terpecah antara `NexusFont*` dan `NexusTypography*` dengan nilai yang saling bertentangan.
- `ConversationView.xaml` dan `MessageContentPresenter.cs` masih memiliki ukuran font, font family, serta warna literal.
- Parser belum mengenali Heading, Horizontal Rule, dan Task List sehingga sintaks Markdown mentah masih tampil.
- Table dan Code Block sudah tersedia dan tidak boleh mengalami regresi.

**Source of truth final:**
- Sistem typography Nexus hanya memiliki satu canonical source of truth.
- Lokasi fisik dipilih Codex berdasarkan pendekatan paling aman dan paling sederhana.
- Warna menggunakan semantic color token Nexus.
- Theme switching tetap menggunakan `ThemeManager`.
- Tidak boleh membuat `ThemeManager`, `TypographyManager`, parser, atau renderer kedua.

---

## Final Architecture

```text
Nexus App
│
├─ ThemeManager
│  └─ Mengganti semantic dan compatibility theme dictionaries
│
├─ Typography Nexus
│  └─ Satu canonical source of truth
│     ├─ ukuran font
│     ├─ font family
│     └─ typography role
│
├─ ConversationView.xaml
│  └─ Mengonsumsi typography dan semantic color token bersama
│
├─ NexusMessageParser
│  └─ MessageDocument
│     ├─ Paragraph
│     ├─ Heading
│     ├─ HorizontalRule
│     ├─ List
│     ├─ Task List
│     ├─ Quote
│     ├─ Code
│     └─ Table
│
└─ MessageContentPresenter
   └─ Satu FlowDocument dalam satu RichTextBox read-only selectable
```

**Data flow:**

```text
Source teks
↓
NexusMessageParser
↓
MessageDocument
↓
MessageContentPresenter
↓
Satu RichTextBox selectable
```

---

## Phase 1 — Typography System

### Tujuan

Menyatukan typography Nexus tanpa memutus kompatibilitas resource dan tanpa membuat sistem baru.

### Kontrak

- Sistem typography Nexus harus mempunyai satu canonical source of truth.
- Codex bebas memilih lokasi fisik paling aman.
- Tidak wajib memindahkan seluruh key jika compatibility alias atau refactoring kecil lebih aman.
- Tidak boleh ada dua definisi typography yang saling bertentangan.
- `ResourceDictionary` dan urutan merge tidak boleh rusak.
- Key lama yang masih digunakan harus tetap dapat di-resolve.
- Jangan membuat `TypographyManager` atau `ThemeManager` baru.
- Jangan membuat token typography yang hanya dipakai AI Workspace.

### Rekonsiliasi typography

- Satukan `NexusFont*` dan `NexusTypography*` ke satu definisi nilai.
- Body, caption, title, heading, code, dan icon family harus berasal dari source of truth yang sama.
- `ConversationView.xaml` dan `MessageContentPresenter.cs` harus memakai token bersama.
- Jangan mempertahankan literal hanya untuk melewati governance.

### Light Mode

Keputusan final:

- Warna teks Light Mode seluruh Nexus boleh dibuat lebih hitam.
- Target visual adalah kontras dan kenyamanan baca setara ChatGPT Light Mode.
- Perubahan dilakukan melalui semantic color token dan compatibility brush Nexus.
- Jangan hardcode warna di renderer atau View.
- Dark Mode tidak boleh berubah atau mengalami regresi.

### ConversationView.xaml

- Pertahankan key `AIWorkspace*Style` yang sudah ada.
- Ganti `FontSize`, `FontFamily`, `FontWeight`, dan `Foreground` literal dengan resource bersama.
- Ganti `Foreground="White"` pada tombol send/stop dengan semantic token yang sesuai.
- Jangan membuat style system kedua.

### MessageContentPresenter.cs

- Hapus ukuran font literal.
- Hapus `FontFamily` yang dibuat langsung.
- Hapus `Brushes.Black` atau `Brushes.White` untuk kebutuhan semantic.
- `Brushes.Transparent` boleh dipertahankan untuk background transparan yang tidak mempunyai arti semantic.
- Gunakan resource reference yang mengikuti perubahan theme.
- Jangan memecah renderer menjadi beberapa selectable control.

### Definition of Done Phase 1

- Typography Nexus memiliki satu canonical source of truth.
- Tidak ada definisi ukuran atau family yang saling bertentangan.
- Tidak ada hardcode typography atau semantic color baru.
- Light Mode lebih hitam dan nyaman dibaca.
- Dark Mode tetap benar.
- Build berhasil.

---

## Phase 2 — Markdown Renderer v1

### Kontrak utama

- Tetap gunakan `NexusMessageParser`.
- Tetap gunakan `MessageContentPresenter`.
- Jangan membuat parser atau renderer kedua.
- Parser harus deterministik.
- Plain text tidak boleh salah dianggap Markdown.
- Table dan Code Block tidak boleh berubah atau regresi.
- Selection harus tetap satu dokumen.

### Block model

Tambahkan seperlunya:

- `HeadingBlock`
- `HorizontalRuleBlock`
- Status `IsChecked` pada item list untuk Task List

Jangan membuat struktur baru jika model list yang ada dapat diperluas secara aman.

### Heading

Dukung:

```text
# Heading 1
## Heading 2
### Heading 3
```

Kontrak:

- Karakter `#` tidak ikut dirender.
- H1, H2, dan H3 memiliki hierarchy yang jelas.
- H4 sampai H6 boleh memakai style H3.
- Typography heading berasal dari source of truth Nexus.
- Heuristic code tidak boleh menganggap heading sebagai blok kode PowerShell.

### Horizontal Rule

Dukung:

```text
---
***
___
```

Kontrak:

- Hanya dikenali pada baris standalone yang valid.
- Tidak boleh salah mengenali isi code fence.
- Tidak boleh merusak separator Markdown table.
- Dirender sebagai garis tipis mengikuti semantic stroke token.
- Tetap berada di dalam FlowDocument yang sama.

### Task List

Input:

```text
- [ ] Belum selesai
- [x] Sudah selesai
```

Output:

```text
☐ Belum selesai
☑ Sudah selesai
```

Dilarang menghasilkan:

```text
• ☐ Belum selesai
• ☑ Sudah selesai
```

Kontrak:

- Task List menggantikan bullet marker.
- Bullet List normal tetap memakai bullet.
- Ordered List tetap memakai nomor.
- Checkbox hanya bersifat visual read-only pada v1.

### Block yang wajib tetap bekerja

- Paragraph
- Bullet List
- Ordered List
- Block Quote
- Code Fence
- Inline Code
- Bold
- Italic
- Table
- Selection lintas seluruh isi satu pesan

### Definition of Done Phase 2

- Heading dirender tanpa karakter mentah.
- Horizontal Rule menjadi garis.
- Task List memakai `☐` dan `☑` tanpa bullet ganda.
- Table dan Code Block tidak regresi.
- Selection tetap satu dokumen.
- Build berhasil.

---

## Phase 3 — Verification

### Workflow wajib

```text
Implementasi
↓
Build
↓
Jalankan Nexus
↓
Verifikasi visual langsung
↓
Perbaiki jika masih salah
↓
Build ulang
↓
Jalankan ulang
↓
Verifikasi ulang
↓
Baru tambahkan regression test minimal
```

### Material verifikasi

Gunakan model apa pun yang tersedia pada AI Workspace. Renderer tidak boleh bergantung pada model tertentu.

Verifikasi satu respons yang memuat:

- `#`, `##`, `###`
- `---`, `***`, `___`
- Bullet List
- Ordered List
- Task List checked dan unchecked
- Block Quote
- Code Fence
- Inline Code
- Bold
- Italic
- Table
- Beberapa paragraf untuk menguji selection lintas blok

### Regression test

Regression test dibuat hanya setelah implementasi dan verifikasi visual berhasil.

Cukup tambahkan test minimum untuk:

- Heading
- Horizontal Rule
- Task List

Jangan membuat banyak test baru yang tidak dibutuhkan.

---

## Acceptance Criteria

Status **PASS** hanya jika seluruh poin berikut terpenuhi:

- [ ] Typography Nexus memiliki satu canonical source of truth.
- [ ] Tidak ada definisi ukuran atau font family yang duplikat dan bertentangan.
- [ ] Tidak ada `FontSize`, `FontFamily`, `Foreground`, `Background`, `BorderBrush`, atau semantic brush hardcoded baru.
- [ ] AI Workspace memakai typography dan semantic color token bersama.
- [ ] Heading dirender tanpa karakter `#`.
- [ ] Horizontal Rule dirender sebagai garis.
- [ ] Task List dirender dengan `☐` dan `☑` tanpa bullet ganda.
- [ ] Bullet List normal tetap memakai bullet.
- [ ] Ordered List tetap memakai nomor.
- [ ] Table tidak regresi.
- [ ] Code Block tidak regresi.
- [ ] Selection tetap menggunakan satu RichTextBox/FlowDocument.
- [ ] Plain text tidak salah diklasifikasikan sebagai Markdown.
- [ ] Light Mode seluruh Nexus memakai warna teks yang lebih hitam dan nyaman dibaca.
- [ ] Dark Mode tetap benar.
- [ ] Build berhasil.
- [ ] Nexus berhasil dijalankan.
- [ ] Verifikasi visual langsung berhasil.

Jangan menyatakan **COMPLETE** berdasarkan build atau test saja.

---

## Out of Scope

Jangan mengubah:

- AI Engine
- Llama Runtime
- Conversation Storage
- PDF Tools
- Image Tools
- Home
- Clean Downloads
- Rapikan File
- Settings

Tidak termasuk:

- HTML Markdown
- Image Markdown
- Mermaid
- Math
- LaTeX
- Footnote
- Emoji parser

---

## Implementation Philosophy

- Perubahan minimum.
- Pilih solusi paling sederhana yang memenuhi seluruh kontrak.
- Reuse komponen yang sudah ada.
- Jangan membuat sistem kedua.
- Jangan menambah abstraction tanpa kebutuhan nyata.
- Jangan memperluas scope.
- Jangan mempertahankan kompleksitas hanya karena disebut dalam rencana awal.
- Berhenti dan laporkan jika ditemukan konflik arsitektur kritis.

---

## Primary Files Expected To Change

Daftar ini bersifat indikatif, bukan absolut. Codex boleh menyentuh file lain jika benar-benar diperlukan untuk memenuhi kontrak, selama perubahan tetap minimum dan berada dalam scope.

### Implementasi utama

- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/Tokens/SharedMetrics.xaml`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/SharedStyles.xaml`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/Semantic/LightSemanticTheme.xaml`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/LightTheme.xaml`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/ConversationView.xaml`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/AIWorkspace/Markdown/NexusMessageParser.cs`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.App/AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`

### Governance bila diperlukan

- `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/DesignSystemSourceAudit.cs`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/DesignSystemGovernanceTests.cs`

### Regression test setelah verifikasi visual

- `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/AIWorkspaceMessageParserTests.cs`
- `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/AIWorkspaceRenderingTests.cs`

---

## Governance

- Governance hanya boleh diubah jika benar-benar diperlukan.
- Jangan menambah pengecualian untuk menyembunyikan hardcode.
- Setiap perubahan governance harus dijelaskan beserta alasan teknisnya.
- `AllowedModuleStyleKeys` tidak perlu berubah jika style key lama dipertahankan.
- `AllowedHexValues` tidak boleh ditambah hanya untuk mengakomodasi literal baru.
- Perubahan governance harus mengurangi debt, bukan memindahkannya.

---

## Notes for Codex

- Audit arsitektur sudah selesai. Jangan mengaudit ulang seluruh aplikasi.
- Gunakan dokumen ini sebagai kontrak implementasi.
- Jangan membuat plan baru kecuali ditemukan blocker kritis.
- Jangan membuat parser kedua.
- Jangan membuat renderer kedua.
- Jangan membuat `ThemeManager` atau `TypographyManager` baru.
- Jangan membuat token typography khusus AI Workspace.
- Jangan memutus ResourceDictionary dan compatibility key.
- Jangan membuat unit test sebelum implementasi dan perilaku visual benar.
- Urutan wajib: implementasi → build → jalankan Nexus → verifikasi visual → perbaiki → ulangi → baru regression test.
- Jika sesi tool tidak dapat melihat window WPF, jangan mengklaim visual PASS. Serahkan instance kepada pengguna untuk pemeriksaan langsung.
- Jika solusi yang lebih sederhana ditemukan dan tetap memenuhi kontrak, gunakan solusi yang lebih sederhana.
- Berhenti dan laporkan jika perubahan mulai menyentuh modul di luar scope.

# Nexus — Gate A Baseline Repair Report

Tanggal: **2026-10-01**. Status: **Gate A PASS untuk solution aktif dan fixtures yang tercatat**, dengan exception cold-start PDF yang disetujui secara eksplisit. **Phase 1 / Gate B belum diotorisasi dan tidak dimulai.**

Evidence awal tetap di [ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md](ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md) dan bagian awal [BASELINE.md](BASELINE.md). Dokumen audit history tidak diedit. Semua path source di sini relatif terhadap `Nexus-CSharp/`.

## 1. Acceptance summary

|---|---|---|
| Restore | PASS | `docs/gate-a-restore.log` |

| WPF host ownership | PASS | satu Application/STA Dispatcher, scoped resources; integration COMPLETE |
| Repeated integration completion | PASS | `full-5-live`: 597/597; `full-6-live`: 597/597; tanpa abort |
| Governance blockers | RESOLVED | architecture/design/resource/startup tests dalam full run |
| Representative live E2E | VERIFIED | dua live facts dieksekusi (bukan skipped), real HTTP/ONNX/PDF/WPF |
| Shared LLM/runtime | VERIFIED | one live PID per stage, same-model reuse, model identity, switch/cleanup |
| Compatibility | PASS pada inventory representatif di §6 | fixtures + hasil native + standalone startup/exit |

Durasi test seluruh solution: **538.0027499 s** wall, sekitar 8 menit 58 detik. Integration project sendiri sekitar 7 menit 20 detik. Ini workload native/benchmark/live, bukan UI startup SLA.

## 3. Problems fixed — evidence and decisions

### A. Application / Dispatcher ownership dan state isolation

`WpfAppHost` sebelumnya membuat App sendiri sementara tiga custom helpers juga membuat App pada short-lived STA threads. Helpers CleanDownloads/FileManagement/Rapikan sekarang memarshal ke host yang sama. Initialization error diteruskan melalui TaskCompletionSource dengan timeout; stack exception dipertahankan.

Integration collections serial karena Application resources/theme/clipboard merupakan process-global state. Resource dictionaries dibuat ulang dari canonical compiled sources per scoped UI action, dengan BeginInit/EndInit restoration; ini **bukan** membuat Application kedua. Test async yang mengubah grouped WPF history memakai host Dispatcher selama construct/await/assert. Tidak mengaktifkan parallel writes atau production synchronization framework.

Production crash-dialog handler dilepas **hanya pada test host**. Exception Dispatcher dicatat untuk menjadi assertion failure pada hosted action, sehingga dialog tidak menyembunyikan failure atau membuat run menunggu interaksi manusia. Production exception handling tidak diganti.

### B. Reflection governance

Governance test memilih `OnnxModelManager.GenerateMetadataAsync(string, string, AICapability?)` secara eksplisit. Overload dua argumen adalah forwarding compatibility method; parser/write/parse contract yang ingin diuji ada pada overload tiga argumen. Production API dipertahankan; assertion tentang metadata parser tidak dihapus.

### C. Physical model path boundary

Caller nyata adalah fallback discovery `RemoveBackgroundPipeline.TryResolveMvanetFallback`: membaca descriptor.ModelPath, mencari sibling metadata, lalu fallback ke path developer hardcoded. Itu discovery/model selection, bukan tensor pipeline responsibility.

Discovery dipindahkan ke **existing `ImageApplicationModelSelectionPolicy`**, menggunakan package sibling dan configured models root. Pipeline menerima optional descriptor resolver dan tetap menjalankan single PhotosSeg → MVANet fallback. Rule governance `get_ModelPath` tetap aktif; normalisasi/tensor/output semantics tidak diganti. Existing fallback test tetap menguji execution dengan descriptor fixture yang eksplisit.

### D. OCR availability vs shell startup

PpOcrEngine tetap DI-owned singleton. AiCheckViewModel menerima deferred factory dari production registration, sehingga native engine baru di-resolve dalam OCR background operation. Missing/corrupt OCR tidak perlu menggagalkan shell/Chat construction. Existing injected-engine constructor behavior tetap didukung.

Tests membuat **explicit temporary model/config/reference roots** untuk missing dan corrupt cases; shell/Chat resolve, inspection berakhir dengan `Pemeriksaan gagal`, bukan sukses palsu. Available case dibuktikan dengan real model root pada live E2E. Tidak mengganti prompts, G3/R3D thresholds, candidate identity/order, batching, atau judgment parser.

### E. Design/resource governance

Selisih 133 → 137 bukan empat token tambahan saja:

| Direct module references | Change sebelum repair |
|---|---:|
| Conversation focus `NexusStrokeStrong` | +1 |
| Conversation font family | +1 |
| Conversation default border references | +5 |
| Image standalone glyph font references | +4 |
| Previously removed radius/body/caption/code/small-icon references | -7 |
| **Net primitive-pattern debt change** | **+4** |

Sebelas direct references baru dimigrasikan melalui semantic content border/font/glyph roles atau component styles. Allowlist direduksi untuk tujuh references yang memang sudah hilang; strict expected debt menjadi **126**, bukan menaikkan angka ke 137. Dua pill styles dan dua round action templates dipusatkan pada existing Buttons dictionary. Geometry/commands tetap sama.

Sembilan literals module baru dipromosikan ke semantic/component resources. Lima primitive ARGB values merupakan **nilai existing yang dipindahkan**, bukan palette baru. Dark/Light mempertahankan nilai existing; HighContrast memakai SystemColors untuk roles baru. Existing baseline hardcoded debt kembali **137**, module-named styles **7**, common control templates **1**. Detail ada pada migration-baseline addendum.

Compiled-BAML regression menangkap alias FontFamily yang menghilangkan legacy key selama implementasi. Final resources memakai distinct keyed FontFamily instances dengan Source string yang sama dan tetap mempertahankan canonical keys. Regression membuktikan legacy glyph key dan semantic glyph key resolve dengan nilai identik. Font issue intermediate tidak dianggap PASS dan diperbaiki sebelum final run.

### F. Failures exposed after full completion

- WorkspaceTabStyle test loader memerlukan BaseUri ketika membuka source XAML yang mempunyai relative merged dictionaries; loader diperbaiki tanpa stripping dependency.
- ZzCausal probes mengharapkan NRE pada renderer tanpa App resources. Inputs dipertahankan sebagai regression pada real WPF host: assignment/render setup tidak throw. Renderer production tidak diberi dummy fallback untuk meloloskan probe.
- Clipboard assertion reads mempunyai bounded retry hanya untuk CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN, sesuai failure Win32 transient yang teramati; assertions tetap memeriksa copied text yang benar.
- Scanner visual matrix memilih Dark palette secara eksplisit karena calibrated blue-channel/luminance assertion bukan palette-independent. Threshold tidak diturunkan.
- Window chrome hit-test diagnostic menunjukkan native window dengan uninitialized VM. DataContext dilepas untuk probe chrome-only agar Loaded/Closing tidak memakai SettingsVM yang tidak ada.
- IntegrationSmoke memakai isolated config file, sehingga default-theme assertion tidak bergantung pada config yang ditulis test/user sebelumnya.

### G. PDF InfoBar resource crash loop

Full run mengungkap XamlParseException berulang saat PDF template mencoba `/Controls/InfoBar/InfoBar.xaml`. Pada testhost sebagai entry assembly, resource itu tidak ada. Production view sekarang memakai `/Nexus.App;component/Controls/InfoBar/InfoBar.xaml`; ini benar untuk desktop executable dan assembly-hosted WPF.

TRX `full-1` serta Windows AppHang evidence menunjukkan resource exception loop, bukan bukti ONNX threading crash walaupun benchmark sedang aktif. URI qualified, rendered PDF navigation, dan test-host exception reporting menutup blocker tersebut.

### H. First-send history sidebar

Real live E2E menemukan first send otomatis membuat dan menyimpan conversation, tetapi current sidebar belum menerima row tersebut. Setelah successful persistence, VM sekarang memperbarui **metadata summary saja** pada Dispatcher (id/title/time/count/selection). Tidak menjalankan history reload yang mengganti message tree, tidak menulis database baru, dan tidak menambah inference call.

Regression memeriksa row muncul, dua messages tetap utuh, dan tidak memanggil OpenConversationAsync. Enam async UI/history tests yang sebelumnya membuat grouped CollectionView pada MTA dipindahkan ke existing STA host; assertions edit/regenerate/attachments tidak dilemahkan.

### I. RadioButton grouping stack overflow

Repeated suite membuka stack overflow pada `RadioButton.UpdateRadioButtonGroup` → TwoWay `IsChatModeActive`. Global GroupName `WorkspaceModeGroup` menggabungkan beberapa detached ConversationView instances.

Kedua buttons adalah siblings dalam UniformGrid, sehingga group native berbasis logical parent cukup. Dua GroupName attributes dihapus; exclusivity tetap scoped ke pair pada view yang sama. Regression dua workspace membuktikan mode masing-masing independen; label/binding test tetap memeriksa casing, TwoWay binding, dan sibling grouping.

## 5. Full test result and repeated completion

Final command (fixture environment in §8):

```powershell
& "C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Microsoft\dotnet\dotnet.exe" test "src\Nexus.sln" --no-build --no-restore -m:1 --logger trx --results-directory "docs\gate-a-results\full-6-live" --blame-hang-timeout 5m --blame-hang-dump-type none --verbosity minimal
```

`-m:1` prevents test projects with native CPU workloads from contending with each other. WPF integration collections are serial; empirical CPU/PDF timing collections also exclude simultaneous collections within their process. This does not change production concurrency.

## 7. Performance findings and agreed baseline policy

Hardware observed: Intel Core i5-10400, **6 physical / 12 logical cores**, approximately 32 GB RAM, CPU inference. Native startup, tiered JIT, page initialization, disk/OS cache and unrelated native test CPU load affect cold timing. No production PDF optimization or engine/library replacement was performed.

### Repeated original test measurements (before policy changes)

| Workload | n | Median ms | Mean ms | Min / max ms | Sample std deviation ms |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|
| PDF dense10 | 8 processes | 89.5 | 93.25 | 82 / 117 | 11.17 |
| PDF dense50 | 8 processes | 320.5 | 323.38 | 300 / 357 | 16.58 |
| Triage | 8 processes | 28 | 28.75 | 27 / 33 | 1.91 |
| OCR Pilkades | 3 processes | 4,313 | 4,396.33 | 4,228 / 4,648 | 222.05 |
| OCR Panin | 3 processes | 3,323 | 3,327.67 | 3,311 / 3,349 | 19.43 |

Raw evidence: pdf-original-1..8.trx, triage-original-1..8.trx, ocr-timing-1..3.trx. P95 is not inferred from these small cold-run samples.

### Dense PDF stage profile, 3 processes × 20 warm samples

| Workload | Median | P95 | Min / max | Sample std |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| dense10 warm | 25.323 ms | 27.471 ms | 24.473 / 28.569 | 0.991 ms |
| dense50 warm | 125.773 ms | 130.205 ms | 122.049 / 136.147 | 2.729 ms |

Warm page-reader initialization dominates: dense10 mean page-reader 23.969 ms, document open 0.177 ms, GetText 0.305 ms; dense50 118.944 / 0.199 / 1.414 ms. Six of 60 dense10 samples and 24 of 60 dense50 samples crossed managed GC collection counters. No forced collection in the measured loop. Text equality/end-page content assertions hold across reads; raw data: pdf-profile-1..3.trx.

Cold profile samples: dense10 median 70.717 ms/min70.249/max71.174; dense50 median314.212/min313.451/max673.414. Different harness/JIT/cache histories mean these are not interchangeable with original single-shot benchmark distributions.

### Explicit agreement

User selected **“Pisahkan cold dan warm”** after reviewing profile and the remaining original dense10 117 ms failure. Agreed policy: report cold-start separately as a reproduced/profiled/classified baseline exception; measure steady-state with explicit warm-up and 20 samples; **warm P95 limits remain <100 ms dense10 / <500 ms dense50**. This is not a claim cold-start always meets the old single-shot limit, and not an unapproved threshold increase.

The same cold/warm distinction was applied transparently to the real-world PDF timing after the full run exposed a 208 ms cold spike; warm limit remains 100 ms. Triage already had JIT warm-up; it now records 20 samples and keeps P95 <100 ms. OCR timing retains its original single native inference limit; isolation fixes contention instead of granting an exception.

Final full-run warm measurements:

| Workload | n | Median ms | Mean ms | P95 ms | Min / max | Sample std ms | Gate |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| dense10 | 20 | 26.441 | 26.853 | 28.369 | 25.772 / 30.084 | 1.088 | PASS <100 |
| dense50 | 20 | 132.712 | 133.596 | 139.823 | 130.405 / 142.042 | 2.918 | PASS <500 |
| real-world 3 pages | 20 | 24.630 | 24.861 | 26.594 | 24.232 / 26.598 | 0.667 | PASS <100 |
| triage | 20 | 21.430 | 23.682 | 30.373 | 20.598 / 32.944 | 4.082 | PASS <100 |

Statistics: mean Σx/n; sample deviation `sqrt(Σ(x-mean)^2/(n-1))`; median mean of middle two sorted samples for even n; P95 nearest-rank `sorted[ceil(.95*n)-1]`. These are measured test workloads, not guaranteed production P95 across hardware/model/data.

## 3. Existing runtime reused / integration boundary

Production composition root `App.xaml.cs` already owns:

```text
Chat -> ConversationRuntimeWorkflow -> LlamaConversationPipeline
                                         -> shared HttpClient -> localhost:8080
                 -> GenericLlamaEngine -> shared ILlamaServerManager

AI CHECK -> AiCheckJudgeService -> shared LlamaClientService
                                -> shared HttpClient -> same localhost:8080

Agent -> SharedRuntimeAgent -> same ConversationRuntimeWorkflow/pipeline
                          -> same owned model/PID/endpoint
```

ModelCatalogService and ImageApplicationModelSelectionPolicy remain existing authoritative model selection. Agent neither starts nor switches nor stops a server, and does not create a model catalog or HttpClient. The production Chat and AI CHECK transport implementations are unchanged; the raw function-calling probe operates **in tests** on the already-owned DI runtime/HttpClient.

`ConversationRuntimeWorkflow` now has a concrete/interface singleton alias so the Agent and Chat can share that exact lifecycle owner. This is logical separation inside Nexus.App, no new assembly.

### Lifetime policy — Option A

Agent run is allowed only while the current workspace runtime is ready. A single bounded additional-consumer lease captures immutable catalog/model/PID/endpoint identity. Lease is an in-memory admission/cancellation/completion handle, **not an exclusive model owner or lifetime pin**; Chat remains free to issue requests.

Navigation stop, a different/unhealthy model switch, or non-reuse startup revokes additional-consumer admission, cancels its linked request and waits for its completion before existing engine stop/start. Same healthy model reuse leaves the lease/run alive and preserves PID. Application exit uses the same workflow stop path, so it also drains the consumer before owned-server shutdown. No global LlamaClientService.CancelStreaming call is used by Agent.

No deferred queue or scheduler: second concurrent Agent proof is rejected immediately, while a Chat request may coexist on the existing server. Navigation does not silently keep a model alive for Agent. A future background/pinned lifetime requirement would require a separate reviewed policy.

## 4. Agent contracts added / files changed

New production files:

```text
src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/Agent/AgentContracts.cs
src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/Agent/SharedRuntimeAgent.cs
src/03_App/Nexus.App/Services/RuntimeConsumerLease.cs
```

Contracts:

- `INexusAgent.ExecuteAsync(AgentRequest, CancellationToken)`.
- AgentRequest: SessionId, Prompt, explicit optional RelevantContext, MaxOutputTokens, Timeout.
- AgentRunContext: new RunId, supplied SessionId, UTC start/deadline, used output-token limit, immutable AgentModelSnapshot.
- AgentModelSnapshot: selected ID/path, owned PID, endpoint, in-process runtime identity.
- AgentResult: outcome, text, finish reason, deterministic error category, elapsed duration; rejected/inactive run never starts a model.

Actual execution bounds: **one model call, no retries or loop**, one additional Agent run at a time, 8,192 combined input characters, 16,384 result characters, 1–256 requested output tokens (default64), timeout default60 s/max120 s. Caller, deadline and runtime-revocation cancellation are linked per run. SSE must produce content and finish_reason `stop`; length/incomplete/malformed/identity-change failures do not become successful results.

No access/dependency on ConversationManager in Agent implementation. Relevant context is supplied by value; no implicit current history or memory loading. Logger records RunId/SessionId/model/PID/start/end/duration/outcome/error metadata; prompt/context/answer are not logged by the Agent proof.

Existing production files changed: `App.xaml.cs` (shared workflow alias, Agent registration, coordinated exit stop); `Services/ConversationRuntimeWorkflow.cs` (admission/revocation/drain around its existing lifecycle). LlamaServerManager, GenericLlamaEngine, ModelCatalogService, selection policy, transport helper/client/pipeline, persistence and ViewModels are otherwise reused unchanged.

New tests:

```text
src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/GateBToolCapabilityTests.cs
src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/SharedRuntimeAgentTests.cs
src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/GateBSharedRuntimeLiveTests.cs
```

Evidence/documents: GATE_B_REPORT.md and `docs/gate-b-evidence/`. JSON/raw protocol files are diagnostic artifacts, **not Agent persistence**. Test Chat config/history roots are isolated fixtures reusing existing storage, not new production stores. No csproj/package changes.

## 6. Session / cancellation / lifecycle results

| Test | Result |
|---|---|
| Inactive run | rejected, zero inference requests, no server start |
| Same-model reuse during Agent | Agent stays active, no engine stop/start, PID preserved |
| Second Agent run while busy | rejected; no hidden queue/concurrency expansion |
| Cancel Agent | CallerCancelled, queued/concurrent Chat completes, PID remains Ready |
| Cancel Chat | Chat token throws cancellation, Agent completes; no shared/global cancellation |
| Agent sessions | distinct RunIds and supplied SessionIds; no retained context transferred to next run |
| Chat session isolation | same serialized ActiveConversation and byte-identical stored detail after Agent runs |
| Navigation stop | WorkspaceRuntimeDeactivated; cancel/drain then owned model stop; no alive PID |
| Model switch during Agent | run cancelled against old immutable snapshot; switch proceeds after drain; new run uses new current model |
| Deadline/failure/oversize | deterministic category, lease released, next valid run succeeds |
| App startup/exit | standalone compiled Nexus window responds, native close exits, no leftover process |

Real cancellation proof uses at most one Agent and one Chat request, not high concurrency. Single-slot server scheduling may queue one request; isolation is independent request tokens and outcomes, not a claim both infer simultaneously.

## 7. Native tool-call capability and raw protocol

**Supported on the active Gemma E4B / b11057 combination tested.** No model/server change was made to obtain support. No native tool claim is made for Qwen or arbitrary models; Qwen appears only in lifecycle-switch proof.

Single declaration: `get_test_value`, object JSON args `{ "key": "gate-b" }`, enum whitelist, no extra properties. Automatic tool choice (not forced required choice) produces native `message.tool_calls`, type=function, function name, JSON argument string, nonempty id, finish_reason=tool_calls. Dummy evaluator is a fixed test string `{ "value":42 }` only; it performs no filesystem/process/theme/PDF/image action.

Captured sequence:

1. Single tool declaration + user request.
2. Native tool call with valid JSON and tool_call_id.
3. Assistant tool_calls message preserved + role=tool with matching tool_call_id and fixed result.
4. Second model response containing42, with no further tool call.
5. Streamed declaration yields **9 native delta fragments** (index0), reassembled id/name/arguments, `[DONE]`, then a matching tool-result follow-up/second model response.

Non-stream round trip repeated twice per probe. Initial, targeted and both full-run probes succeeded. Final probe directory **12731a0ae9eb4c9fb6228fe45dd494af**, PID19528, errorCategory null/cleanup true; includes:

```text
identity.json / props.response.json / result.json
nonstream-{1,2}.request.json / .response.txt / .http.json
followup-{1,2}.request.json / .response.txt / .http.json
stream.request.json / stream.response.txt / stream.http.json
stream-followup.request.json / .response.txt / .http.json
```

Malformed JSON, array arguments, wrong enum and additional args are rejected. Plain-text `get_test_value(...)` response is explicitly **NativeToolCallingUnsupported**, not parsed as a fallback/hack. Probe errors preserve raw response/status and propagate failure; stop rule remains applicable if future active-model probe fails. Argument/fragment parsing here is diagnostic test-only, not a production tool protocol executor or registry.

## 10. Known limitations / recommendation for Gate C / stop

- Gate B consumer supports one reasoning call only and has no production tool execution, Agent loop/planner/UI/memory/skills/recovery store.
- Option A cancels Agent on workspace deactivation; no background pin or durability/resume guarantee.
- Coordination applies to production workflow stop/switch/exit; calling the low-level manager directly bypasses it. Existing app consumers should continue using their owned workflow; a broader runtime-admission redesign is not claimed.
- Deprecated global client CancelStreaming remains in compatibility code. Agent uses pipeline/request tokens and never calls it; arbitrary external legacy consumer calls are outside cancellation-isolation proof.
- Native tool support is verified for this active model/build/protocol fixture, not a general model certification. Production transport still maps ordinary text/reasoning; future Gate C protocol extension must preserve Chat/Judge semantics and use validated typed calls.
- Limited contention samples and one cold startup failure remain documented. No performance exception/threshold adjustment newly granted here; Gate A benchmark policy unchanged.

Recommendation: after review, Gate C may begin with a small read-only allowlisted registry/policy/input validation/result bounds on the verified shared runtime, retaining side-effect-free initial capabilities. No MAF dependency or shell/write authority is justified by this proof.

# Nexus — Gate C / Phase 2 Report

Date: **2026-10-03**. **Gate C PASS** for the bounded read-only tool path and certified fixture below. Full certification: **1,124 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, no abort**. **STOP before Gate D.**

## Scope

Explicit read-only registry, closed argument validation, Nexus-owned default-deny policy and a native protocol round-trip on the existing shared runtime. `AgentRequest.EnableReadOnlyToolCall` defaults to false; the existing no-tool streaming path remains available. Enabled runs allow at most **one tool execution and one model follow-up**, without retries or an autonomous loop.

Gate B admission, immutable run/model identity, one additional-consumer lease, per-request cancellation and Option A cancel/drain on navigation/model switch are retained. Existing Chat/Judge transport, runtime manager, model catalog and persistence are reused. No filesystem/shell/process/write tool, approval UI, MAF/Hermes runtime, ToolHost, Agent persistence, planner or visual polish was introduced.

## Tool Registry

`ToolRegistry` accepts explicit `INexusTool` instances from composition-root registration. No reflection, assembly scan, dynamic invocation or automatic exposure of existing services. Exact ordinal names match `\A[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,63}\z`; case, trailing whitespace, dotted aliases and Unicode/lookalike spellings are not normalized. Unknown names yield `ToolNotFound` and zero execution.

Registry snapshots descriptors and clones JSON schemas, rejects duplicate names, invalid metadata/risk/limits and unsupported schema constructs. Descriptors exposed as a read-only collection; the policy allowlist is independently copied into a private set.

## Registered Tools

Exactly one production tool: **`nexus_get_model_snapshot`**. Empty arguments `{}`; risk `ReadOnly`, readOnly true, timeout **2 seconds**, result-envelope cap **2,048 UTF-8 bytes**. Returns logical model ID from the immutable run snapshot and state from the existing `ILlamaServerManager`. It does not start, stop or switch the runtime.

Live data: `{"modelId":"gemma-4-e4b","runtimeState":"Ready"}`. Complete serialized status/data/reasonCode envelope: **94 bytes**. No model path, PID, endpoint, configuration dump or credential is exposed by this tool.

## Tool Descriptor Schema

`ToolDescriptor`: Name, Description, Risk, ReadOnly, Timeout, MaxResultSize and ArgumentSchema. Registration bounds: description <=512 characters; timeout >0 and <=5 seconds; result cap 256–16,384 bytes; risk/readOnly consistency required.

`ToolExecutionContext` carries the existing `AgentRunContext`. Typed result outcomes: `Success`, `Denied`, `InvalidArguments`, `ToolNotFound`, `Timeout`, `Cancelled`, `ExecutionFailure`, `ResultTooLarge`. `ToolDispatchResult` includes exact call ID/name, validation, policy decision, typed result and serialized length.

## Argument Validation

Closed JSON schema dialect: object root, <=8 scalar properties, required keys and `additionalProperties:false`. Supports string (required maxLength 1–256, optional bounded enum), integer (Int64 range) and boolean. Unknown keywords, nested object/array types and invalid ranges fail registration; this is intentionally not a general JSON Schema implementation.

Raw arguments <=**4,096 UTF-8 bytes**, JSON depth <=4. Rejects malformed JSON, non-object roots, duplicate supplied properties, missing required keys, wrong type/enum/range, excessive strings/depth and extra properties before policy/execution. Attempts to supply `risk`, `allowWrite` or `requiresApproval` are extra properties, not authority.

## Policy Decisions

Nexus owns an explicit exact-name allowlist. ReadOnly must be true and Risk must be ReadOnly; writes/process/system-critical operations are denied even if their names are allowlisted. Valid RunId/session, model snapshot, unexpired run deadline and object arguments are required. Default-deny reasons include `UnknownTool`, `InvalidArguments`, `WriteOrProcessNotAllowed`, `PolicyDenied`, `InvalidRunContext`; successful admission is `AllowedReadOnlyTool`.

Prompt/context/tool-result text does not modify permission, descriptor risk, allowlist or execution bounds. Denied/invalid results may be represented as bounded tool data for the single follow-up; the overall Agent result cannot report Completed when its tool outcome is non-success.

## Tool Protocol

Optional dedicated **non-stream** native adapter uses the existing DI shared HttpClient and immutable shared endpoint. Ordinary Chat and Judge parsing is unchanged. Native `assistant.tool_calls` only: exact function name, argument JSON string and nonempty ID. Plain-text pseudo-calls remain text and are never executed.

Strict checks: one choice, assistant role, finish_reason, call type/shape, ID syntax, duplicate response properties/IDs, argument size and single-call count. Requests/responses capped at **65,536 UTF-8 bytes**, response JSON depth12; assistant text <=16,384 characters. The model receives the preserved assistant call plus `role:tool`, with **assistant ID = dispatch ID = tool_call_id**. Mismatches fail. A second native request on the follow-up fails `ToolCallLimitReached` without a second execution. No retries/text repair/automatic model replacement.

Capability is explicitly certified only for model `gemma-4-e4b`, `gemma-4-E4B-it-Q4_K_M.gguf` and llama-server directory/build **b11057**, with matching fingerprints:

```text
Model SHA-256: 519b9793ed6ce0ff530f1b7c96e848e08e49e7af4d57bb97f76215963a54146d
Engine SHA-256: C72F76994F8D680D7B423FF68662DE33857D2A2B001465DE55952873D4EF3DF3
```

Other identities/hashes return Unknown and fail `NativeToolCapabilityUnknown` before sending tools. File fingerprinting is trusted runtime capability verification within the run deadline, not a model-callable file-reading tool. Support cache uses full paths, lengths and last-write timestamps.

## Cancellation / Timeout

Caller, run deadline and Gate B lease revocation tokens remain linked across capability verification, native request, tool and follow-up. Run timeout defaults60 seconds/max120 seconds; output tokens1–256 per model request. Tool deadline is the smaller of descriptor timeout and remaining run deadline. Oversized results become `ResultTooLarge` with no original data forwarded. Exceptions become deterministic `ToolExecutionFailure` without exception payload/path forwarding.

Controlled tests verify cooperative tool cancellation and typed Timeout/Cancelled outcomes. `WaitAsync` bounds asynchronous completion but cannot preempt a blocking synchronous body or terminate a non-cooperative API; the sole production operation is a fast in-memory snapshot. Agent does not use global `LlamaClientService.CancelStreaming()`.

## Session Isolation

Actual live proof asserts Chat Messages/SelectedConversation/ActiveConversation unchanged, stored detail byte-identical, and fixture config/model-index byte-identical after tool-enabled runs. Context is explicitly supplied by value; registry/tool does not load Chat history. No production persistence was added.

Both cancellation directions use the real shared Chat transport: cancel Agent while Chat completes; cancel Chat while tool-enabled Agent completes. Owned PID remains the same and Ready during coexistence; cleanup stops the owned runtime with no alive leftover. Gate B navigation/reuse/switch/drain facts also execute in full certification.

## Security Tests

Adversarial coverage includes exact/Unicode/lookalike names, duplicate registration, invalid schema/metadata, malformed/non-object JSON, wrong integer type/fraction, enum/range/string/depth/size, required/extra/duplicate arguments and attempted authority overrides. Denied/unknown cases assert zero tool executions.

Tests also cover result caps, timeout/cancellation, sanitized execution failure/logs, native call IDs/mismatches/duplicates/missing fields/multiple calls, unexpected risk metadata, pseudo-tool text, unknown capability and repeated follow-up requests. A prompt asking to ignore policy cannot admit a denied tool. Logging records RunId/call ID/name/validation/policy/outcome/duration/result length/error category only; arguments, result data and raw exception text are not logged by the tool executor.

## Known Limitations

- Native capability certified only for the fingerprinted Gemma E4B/b11057 fixture. Other model/build combinations remain Unknown; no automatic substitution.
- Gate C native adapter is non-stream; Gate B's streamed diagnostic capability proof is retained, but streamed tool execution is deferred.
- Capability fingerprinting has a first-run IO/latency cost. Cache uses path/length/mtime, not a tamper-resistant identity service.
- Validator supports a deliberately small schema dialect. Trusted registered code must remain cooperative and return bounded data; executor is not a process sandbox for arbitrary extensions.
- Option A cancels/drains Agent when workspace runtime deactivates. No background pin, durable resume, scheduler, autonomous loop or heavy-concurrency guarantee.
- Tool dispatch metadata on the Agent result is available after a completed round-trip; protocol/transport/cancellation failure before return may omit it. Executor metadata logs still record dispatches that reached execution.

## Recommendation for Gate D

Gate C is ready for review on this explicit read-only scope. A separately authorized Gate D should preserve default-deny, exact native identities, bounded execution, session isolation and shared-runtime lifetime ownership. Expand authority or model support only with explicit contracts and evidence.

**STOP after Gate C. Gate D has not started.**

# Nexus Gate D Report

Date: **2026-10-03**. **Gate D / Phase 3 PASS** for the bounded read-only orchestration and certified model/runtime below. Final whole-solution certification: **1,194 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, no abort**. **Gate E HOLD — STOP after Gate D.**

## 1. Scope

Nexus-native finite Agent orchestration, sequential native tools, deterministic budgeting/retry classification and C# verification on the existing shared runtime. The fast path remains `EnableReadOnlyToolCall=false`: one streaming reasoning request, structural verification, no tools/capability hashing. The enabled path uses explicit known fact requirements, validated native requests and run-local execution facts.

No write, shell, arbitrary process, general filesystem read, persistence/database, memory, planner graph, Agent UI, second model, new runtime/project/assembly, MAF, Hermes runtime or ToolHost. Chat and AI CHECK retain their existing transports/features; server tuning is unchanged.

| Write / shell capability | **NO / NO** |
| Tool concurrency | **1**, sequential |

## 3. Existing Gate C boundaries retained

- Explicit `ToolRegistry` remains the metadata source of truth; no service/assembly scanning or reflection exposure.
- Exact ordinal native names/IDs; closed schema validation, byte/depth limits and typed tool results retained.
- Nexus-owned immutable policy allowlist, default-deny, ReadOnly/Risk checks; model/prompt/result text cannot change permissions.
- Original model snapshot tool remains **2-second timeout / 2,048-byte result-envelope cap**, empty closed arguments; no tool behavior change.
- Native parser still rejects simultaneous multiple calls, duplicate JSON properties/IDs, malformed authority metadata and plain-text pseudo-tool execution.
- Matching assistant native ID / dispatch ID / role=tool ID retained on every follow-up; duplicate IDs across turns additionally rejected.
- Gate B run admission, shared instance ownership, one Agent lease, immutable model snapshot, request-scoped cancellation and Option A cancel/drain remain.
- Fingerprinted **Gemma E4B + b11057** capability required; Unknown deterministically fails without inference/model replacement/download.

## 4. Orchestrator

`AgentOrchestrator` replaces the Gate C one-tool `AgentToolRoundTrip`; the old helper is removed rather than retaining a separate unverified executor. Existing `SharedRuntimeAgent` owns admission/linked cancellation/results; orchestrator owns finite native turns and sequential dispatches.

Flow: capability check once per run → `for(turn < MaxModelTurns)` → model response → native request/validation/policy/tool/result → next turn, or deterministic final verification. There is no model-controlled `while(true)`, autonomous retry loop, scheduler or second model.

Explicit verification profiles are `ModelSnapshot` (default tool path) and `ModelAndRuntime` (two independent sources). Pending fact declarations are selected from Registry descriptors already allowed by Policy. Native `tool_choice=required` is used while required facts are missing; after facts are obtained, final turn has no tool declarations, `tool_choice=none`, and a native JSON schema for the final shape. Schema constrains types/keys, **not factual values**. Unexpected native requests are still parsed, counted and checked against registry/policy/budgets; declaration/request constraints are not relied on as the security boundary.

This is a small explicit fact workflow, not a complexity router or general planner. No tool schema is independently copied into an orchestrator catalog. The added runtime tool reads current workflow readiness/owned-runtime agreement, whereas model identity comes from the immutable run snapshot.

## 5. State machine

Implemented states only: `Idle`, `Analyzing`, `ExecutingTool`, `Verifying`, `Completed`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`. Inactive/busy/invalid requests retain existing Rejected outcome with terminal Failed state; deadlines retain TimedOut outcome with terminal Failed state. No approval/recovery/pause/resume state.

`AgentRunState` is separate from `ToolOutcome`. A denied/timed-out tool remains a typed dispatch fact; it does not become the Agent state. SharedRuntimeAgent assigns the terminal state after deterministic verification/failure/cancellation. No Partial state was needed.

## 6. Run limits

Immutable `AgentRunLimits` effective snapshot is attached to `AgentRunContext`. Trusted caller may lower limits; C# rejects ceilings above these bounds or invalid values before inference:

| Limit | Default / hard ceiling |
|---|---:|
| MaxModelTurns |4 |
| MaxToolCalls |3 |
| MaxToolFailures |2 |
| MaxRetries |1 |
| MaxRunDuration |60s default,120s maximum |
| MaxInputContext |32,768 UTF-8 bytes, aggregate serialized message context |
| MaxToolResultBytes |6,144 UTF-8 bytes, aggregate envelopes |
| MaxFinalOutput |16,384 characters |

Existing initial prompt+explicit RelevantContext remains <=8,192 characters. Output tokens remain1–256 per model request. Request Timeout, if supplied, becomes the effective immutable duration; it never resets between turns. Native adapter retains request/response cap65,536 UTF-8 bytes and parser depth12. No new token estimator.

`AgentRunProgress` owns C# counters/state/dispatches/verification and Stopwatch. Mutators are internal, limits immutable, cancellation token excluded from serialization. RunId, SessionId, UTC logs, immutable model snapshot and effective limits remain on context. Stopwatch is stopped at terminal completion, so returned execution summary is stable.

## 7. Tool-call budgeting

Each accepted native tool request consumes one slot **before** argument validation, policy or execution. Unknown/denied/invalid/timed-out/failed requests cannot get unlimited free attempts. At the ceiling, next request fails `ToolCallLimitReached` without executing another operation. Malformed native responses terminate rather than being repaired/retried.

Synthetic infinite-request proof: exactly **4 model requests,3 accepted tool requests,3 executions**, then ToolCallLimitReached; no fourth execution. Every inference/retry reserves a model-turn slot. Last-turn tool response without room for final verification fails ModelTurnLimitReached, with no extra inference. One native request per response, sequential awaits, no parallel executor.

## 8. Context/result budgeting

Accounts actual UTF-8 serialized messages: initial prompt/context/system envelope, retained assistant native responses/call metadata, tool-result envelopes and final response. Checks before sending another request and before verification. Historical call/result messages are appended once, not repeatedly duplicated. Initial user/context is preserved; Nexus may replace its own system final-format instruction after fact collection.

Aggregate `TotalToolResultBytes` includes every dispatch envelope, including typed failures. Per-tool cap remains independent. Crossing6,144 bytes fails ToolResultTooLarge; aggregate message context crossing32,768 bytes fails ContextBudgetExceeded; original oversize result payload is not forwarded. Tests lower aggregate limits to prove enforcement before further requests.

## 9. Failure classification

Typed `AgentFailureCategory` extends existing ErrorCategory compatibility rather than changing existing Gate B string expectations: Cancelled, DeadlineExceeded, ModelTransportFailure, NativeToolUnsupported, ToolNotFound, InvalidToolArguments, ToolDenied, ToolTimeout, ToolExecutionFailure, ToolResultTooLarge, ToolCallLimitReached, ModelTurnLimitReached, FailureBudgetExceeded, MalformedModelResponse, VerificationFailed, RuntimeDeactivated, ModelIdentityChanged, ContextBudgetExceeded, FinalOutputLimitReached, Unknown.

Counters include failed tool dispatches and failed model attempts (transport/protocol); verification failure is terminal and counted. At the failure ceiling no further turn is admitted. Typed dispatch facts survive cancellation, protocol/transport failure and failed final verification. No failure text/path/exception payload is turned into a successful result.

## 10. Retry policy

Default **no retry**. Only explicitly classified model HTTP408/429/502/503/504 may retry, max1, provided failure/model-turn/deadline budgets remain. Same logical request retried, sequentially, without deadline reset. Unknown/network errors without certified status,400/401/500, malformed response/metadata, policy/argument/identity/cancellation/budget/capability failures do not retry.

No automatic tool retry: current tools have no certified transient failure class. A timeout may be sent as bounded typed data for a model follow-up. If model asks for another operation, it consumes a new tool/model slot and failure budget; it is not a free executor retry. Any original non-success tool dispatch remains unresolved, so later success/prose cannot yield Completed.

RetryScheduled event records original ModelTransportFailure, attempt number and HTTP reason. Classifier table tests and transport tests prove one retry, no retry when disabled, no extra model slot and failure ceiling. No exponential framework/delay policy introduced.

## 11. Verification

`AgentResultVerifier` is deterministic C#, no LLM/Judge/critic. Fast path verifies nonempty bounded structure and finish_reason=stop; this is not a semantic truth guarantee for arbitrary no-tool prose.

Tool profiles require successful required executions, no non-success dispatch, and **raw JSON final** with exact unique keys/types and no extra fields. ModelSnapshot compares modelId/runtimeState to tool data and immutable model identity. ModelAndRuntime additionally requires the runtime snapshot and compares ready/current state against that independent output. Conflicting/missing facts, duplicate/extra keys, prose, fenced JSON or guessed correct-looking output without tool execution fail VerificationFailed.

Completed requires final model response, passed verification, no unresolved tool failures, and unchanged ready runtime identity. Failed tool outcomes remain in ToolDispatches; compatibility Tool references the last dispatch, never substitutes for the full fact list. No hidden reasoning/chain-of-thought transcript is retained.

Initial live failures were preserved: model returned fenced JSON (FinalStructureInvalid); stronger prompt alone then produced placeholders without execution (RequiredModelToolMissing). Neither was accepted. Native required-fact declarations plus final shape schema resolved formatting/required-execution reliability **without weakening verifier, increasing budgets or changing model/server**. Records: `orchestration-live.trx`, `live-diagnostic.trx`, `orchestration-live-final.trx` and associated live directories. Initial first failure lacked detailed category evidence; the diagnostic run captured fenced response and verifier facts.

## 12. Cancellation/deadline

One run-local Stopwatch budget and linked CTS cover admission, fingerprinting, model turns, dispatch/result handling, retry and verification. UTC Deadline is diagnostic/compatibility; active run calculations use monotonic remaining duration. Boundaries check cancellation and runtime identity, plus explicit monotonic expiration even if the timer callback has not fired yet. Tool CTS uses min(remaining run duration, tool timeout).

Tests cancel during initial request, tool execution and follow-up; verification checks cancellation; no later execution after cancel. Cancellation retains typed dispatched cancellation where present, releases lease and next run verifies. Two delayed model turns prove the deadline is not restarted. UTC Deadline moved into the past in a monotonic-context test does not change active elapsed authority.

Non-cooperative synchronous arbitrary extensions are not sandboxed: WaitAsync cannot preempt them. Both production tools are fast in-memory read-only snapshots. No global CancelStreaming call added.

## 13. Session/runtime isolation

Existing runtime/model/HttpClient reused; no starts/switches/stops initiated by Agent. SharedRuntimeAgent acquires the existing single additional-consumer lease and binds every turn to its immutable model path/PID/endpoint/instance snapshot. Identity changes fail ModelIdentityChanged; navigation/switch revocation cancels against the old snapshot, drains before stop/switch and never silently resumes on the new model.

Live proof confirms unchanged Chat Messages/SelectedConversation/ActiveConversation, byte-identical stored conversation detail/config/model index and unchanged design fixture hash. Themes/Views unchanged relative to pre-edit checkpoint. No implicit Chat history access; context is supplied by value. Gate B existing navigation/reuse/model-switch/live Chat and Gate A AI CHECK tests all execute in final full certification.

Real shared-transport cancellation passes both directions: Agent cancelled while Chat completes; Chat cancelled while tool-enabled Agent verifies. One Agent run at a time, same PID retained during coexistence, no orphan after cleanup. Server remains existing single-slot tuning; concurrency here is independent request lifetimes, not a claim of simultaneous inference.

Reused IDs across turns rejected; existing simultaneous-call/parser/schema/name attacks remain covered.

Verifier tests reject final prose, wrong model/state, extra authority fields, missing required execution, missing second source and pseudo-tool text. Timeout→success→final still fails with retained original timeout. Cancellation/lifecycle/identity/monotonic tests, transient/non-transient retry table and hash-cache invalidation tests are included.

Structured events implemented: RunStarted, StateChanged, ModelTurnStarted/Completed, ToolRequested/Validated/PolicyDecision/Started/Completed, VerificationStarted/Completed, RetryScheduled, RunCompleted/Failed/Cancelled. Metadata includes RunId/SessionId/state/turn/call numbers/call ID/name/outcome/category/elapsed/modelId/remaining budgets. Tool events correlate RunId+ToolCallId. ## 6. Build-graph/sealed-fixture prerequisite

Engineering provisions and restores the fixture before Agent admission. The catalog accepts only the explicit Product Alpha/Beta/Gamma compile mapping and one Tests project with three required facts, net8.0, SDK 8.0.424, fixed package versions, disabled Directory.Build imports/shared compilation, and no custom targets/Exec/linked compile items. ## 7. Source inspection

The host supplies exact existing targets and before hashes. Existing guarded UTF-8/BOM/path checks are reused. Source preparation requires one exact original literal occurrence per file and a real change; no fuzzy matching or inferred already-desired acceptance. The model sees bounded literal arguments, not writable root, path configuration, build commands, or approval controls.

## 8. SourceEditPlan

`SourceEditPlan` binds PlanId, RunId, workspace identity, verification profile id/digest, ordered immutable proposals, operation/call identities, before/desired/diff hashes and diff text, and caps. Max files three, total diff 2,048 UTF-8 bytes, total changed lines six. The digest is recomputed during approval matching. Two or three constituent writes consume one separate source-plan budget; ordinary `.txt` MaxWriteOperations remains one.

## 9. Plan approval

`IWriteApprovalProvider.RequestPlanAsync` defaults to deny. One approval binds exact plan/workspace/run/profile/digest. After validation C# derives per-file Gate E grants with the same approval id; there are no per-file approval prompts. Model arguments cannot create grants. Production DI uses an empty `SourcePlanCatalog` and existing default-deny approval. Test approvals are explicit synthetic user decisions.

## 10. All-file preflight

Every approved before hash is refreshed after approval and before first commit. A stale file fails with zero commits. Profile integrity is checked before admission and each file. Each constituent safe write also refreshes its own guarded precondition. A later conflict triggers recovery of already committed earlier files; newer external contents are preserved.

## 11. Sequential safe apply

Existing Windows local fixed NTFS TxF/KTM commit path is reused with no unlocked replacement fallback. Source writes are strictly sequential. Operation records retain commit-reached, approval, backup, actual hash, verification, and recovery facts. Files are not claimed as cross-file atomic: a transient partial state exists between commits, followed by finite reverse recovery on failure.

## 12. Verification runner

`SourceVerification` is an internal fixed-profile runner, not a model-callable process API. Absolute SDK executable: `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Microsoft\dotnet\dotnet.exe`. Shell execution is disabled, arguments use ArgumentList, working directory is the reviewed fixture, environment is restricted, output/error are drained concurrently. Retained combined diagnostics: 4,096 UTF-8 bytes; structured TRX cap: 32,768 bytes. Owned process-tree termination and bounded wait/drain run on cancellation and exceptions before recovery returns.

## 13. Build verification

Exactly one invocation:

```text
build Tests/Tests.csproj --no-restore --disable-build-servers -t:Rebuild -m:1 -nr:false -p:UseSharedCompilation=false -p:ImportDirectoryBuildProps=false -p:ImportDirectoryBuildTargets=false --verbosity quiet
```
