## 14. Targeted-test verification

Exactly one invocation:

```text
test Tests/Tests.csproj --no-build --no-restore --filter FullyQualifiedName~SourceFixtureTests.RequiredFacts --logger trx;LogFileName=required.trx --results-directory <unique-owned-directory> --verbosity quiet
```

The 15-second test cap shares the run deadline. Before tests, receipt hashes/MVIDs must match. After tests, graph and assembly hashes must still match. A unique bounded TRX must have exactly AlphaDesired, BetaDesired and GammaBounded, all passed, total/executed/passed three and no failure/abort/nonexecution categories. Prose or exit code alone cannot establish success.

## 15. Recovery

Apply/build/test/final-state failure recovers committed files in reverse order through extracted Gate E guarded recovery. Original backups and approved desired-current hashes are required. Recovery conflicts preserve newer external bytes. Cancellation does not abandon a reached durable commit. Successful coordinator results retain an internal nonserialized approved-plan receipt so final-model/protocol/identity failure also invokes the same recovery path. Model final facts with a changed digest are rejected and both files are restored in the Agent regression test.

## 16. Cancellation/deadline

One monotonic run clock, default 60 seconds / maximum 120 seconds; no fresh per-stage deadline. Existing bounds remain four model turns, three tool calls, two tool failures and one retry. Source-plan requests disable post-mutation model transport retries. Runtime identity and cancellation are rechecked at source-plan boundaries, including between constituent writes. Existing executor fully awaits mutation, process cleanup and recovery. Finite local cleanup may complete beyond the admission deadline to preserve durable state facts.

## 17. Security/trust boundary

`nexus_apply_source_plan` permits only a_old/a_new/b_old/b_new and optional c_old/c_new, equal to trusted intent literals. Catalog mapping, hashes, commands, profile, approval and limits originate in C#. Source/diagnostics/tool text remains data. C# final verification checks actual desired files, untouched source files, graph and result records plus exact planId/planDigest/verified JSON. Compiled fixture/tests execute with current-user authority; this proof assumes the reviewed host SDK/package installation and fixture, rather than containment of hostile executable source.

# Nexus Gate E Report

Implementation date: **2026-10-03**; blocker repair/certification: **2026-10-04**. **Gate E PASS** on the scoped Windows NTFS safe-write capability and certified runtime. Final whole solution: **1,261 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 0 aborted**. The two earlier **1,259 pass / 1 fail / 0 skip** runs are preserved in §21 and the **Certification Blocker Resolution** section below. No assertion/timeout weakening. **STOP before Gate E.1 / Phase 5.**

## 1. Scope

One capability: `nexus_apply_text_patch`, one literal replacement in one existing small UTF-8 `.txt` file under an explicit Nexus-host workspace. Nexus prepares the exact proposal/diff, independent provider approves, C# rechecks expected state, retains backup, stages/flushes temp, commits atomically and verifies. Recovery is conditional on the committed state still being present.

No general WriteFile/ReadFile, create/delete/move capability, multi-file editing, source editing/build/test/Git tool, shell/arbitrary process, Agent dashboard, memory/skills, DB, second model/runtime, MAF/Hermes/ToolHost. Temp/backup artifacts are internal mutation responsibilities, not model-callable file operations.

### Phase summary

| Requirement | Evidence/status |
|---|---|
| Registered write tools | `nexus_apply_text_patch` only |
| Max write operations / retries | **1 / 0** |
| Approved roots | Explicit immutable `ApprovedTextWorkspace` supplied by trusted C# host; **production default disabled/null**; proof uses isolated `live-*/approved-workspace` and OS Temp roots |
| Allowed targets | Existing regular single-link `.txt`, UTF-8 BOM/no BOM, <=4,096 bytes, Windows local fixed NTFS |
| Expected hash / stale conflict | PASS |
| Sibling-prefix / traversal | PASS |
| Reparse/junction escape | PASS on actual junction; universal reparse rejection; symlink creation privilege unavailable on host, see §5 |

## 3. Existing Gate D boundaries retained

Finite existing AgentOrchestrator retained: MaxModelTurns4, MaxToolCalls3, MaxRetries1, MaxToolFailures2, one monotonic default60s/max120s deadline, one Agent lease, sequential tools. Exact registry, closed validator/native parser, immutable model snapshot, request-scoped cancellation, Option A drain and C# verifier remain.

Original `nexus_get_model_snapshot` stays2-second timeout/2,048-byte cap with unchanged implementation. Native capability hashes/build/model checks and cache semantics unchanged; ordinary Chat/Judge transport/runtime workflow/server tuning unchanged. No new Agent engine or second verifier model.

## 4. Safe-write capability

Closed arguments: target (relative <=64 characters), expected_sha256 (exact64 hex characters checked by C#), old_text/new_text (<=256 characters each). Literal replacement uses EditingLite's existing unique-match ProposalService. Combined literal UTF-8 input <=1,024 bytes; target/desired file <=4,096 bytes. Initial caller intent is bounded before serialization/inference.

Risk SafeWrite, ReadOnly=false. Descriptor timeout120s is capped by remaining run deadline; result envelope cap2,048 bytes. Write-capable execution is fully awaited, never detached on cancellation. Non-success outcomes are typed. An already-present intended literal or unchanged request can return NoOp, with approval and exact-state verification but no commit/backup requirement.

## 5. Path containment

EditingLite `FileSnapshotService.ResolvePath` now delegates to canonical, separator-aware `SafePath.ResolveRelative`, replacing unsafe `StartsWith(root)` matching. Requires a fully qualified trusted root and relative target; rejects absolute/UNC/device targets, traversal/dot components, mixed-separator escapes, ADS/colon, trailing dot/space, repeated separators, DOS device/short aliases and excessive depth/length. Windows case handling is ordinal-ignore-case with final native-path equality.

Agent scope additionally requires existing local fixed NTFS root/target, `.txt`, normal/archived regular attributes, single hard link, no read-only/compressed/system/reparse target. Root and every existing ancestor/target reparse boundary is rejected. Native handles inspect attributes/link count/final resolved path; directory handles use real read access and no delete sharing, preventing ordinary rename/deletion while allowing required child-file transaction writes. Anchors rechecked at read/staging/commit boundaries; backup pinned through verification/recovery.

Actual junction escape, sibling prefix, traversal, alternate aliases, hard-link target and pinned-directory rename tests pass. Host cannot create a new file symlink (Win32 privilege1314); initial fixture attempt was retained as failed evidence. Final test explicitly proves unavailable target fail-closed without elevation/skip; an actual junction proves the same tag-independent reparse check. **Successful creation/testing of a file-symlink fixture is not claimed.**

## 6. Mutation proposal

Audited existing EditingLite primitives: snapshot, unique literal proposal, diff, syntax validator, temp writer and atomic helper. Gaps found: prefix containment, separately-read content/hash, deleted success backups and blanket legacy restore. Agent uses new safe methods on the **same partial EditingWorkflowService/FileWriterService**, not an AgentFileWriterV2.

Proposal binds OperationId/RunId/ToolCallId, workspace policy identity, logical target, exact original/desired hashes, exact escaped literal plus deterministic line diff, limits, timestamp and encoding. Before/desired text is available to approval provider in memory, excluded from serialization/logging. Escaped literals expose otherwise invisible CR/LF changes. UTF-8 BOM and unchanged line endings are preserved by encoding/replacement, not normalized. `.txt` proof does not claim source AST validation.

Legacy manual ApplyEditAsync is retained for existing compatibility and **not exposed as an Agent tool**; its blanket rollback is not the certified Agent path. Snapshot hash now comes from the same bytes read as content, not a second path read.

## 7. Approval

`IWriteApprovalProvider.RequestAsync(exactProposal, token)` is a real boundary after complete proposal. Decision binds approval/operation/policy/run/tool-call IDs, target, before/desired/diff hashes and effective file/input limits. Changed content, target, operation/run/call, workspace, diff or limits invalidates approval. Decision is not sourced from model arguments.

Production `DenyWriteApprovalProvider` and unconfigured workspace deliberately grant no writes. Proof provider explicitly approves only its synthetic exact target/hash/diff. Existing dialog service's fallback behavior is not reused; no silent automatic confirmation or dashboard. Provider receives exact physical snapshot path/owned workspace as well as logical target/content/diff, enabling a future specific review UI.

Rejected approval yields ApprovalDenied and no temp/commit. Cancellation while awaiting provider yields Cancelled and zero mutation; approval task cannot itself perform a hidden write. Late decision after cancelled wait is not applied.

## 8. Expected hash / conflict detection

Expected hash checked against one coherent original snapshot during preparation, then **re-read after approval**, and again inside protected transaction before commit. Changed hash returns Conflict, without adopting new state or applying stale patch. Tests preserve newer C bytes exactly and show no commit/approval retry. Record includes observed hash where recheck occurred. No write retry is permitted.

## 9. Backup / temporary write / atomic commit

Unique retained before-state artifact: `<target>.nexus-edit-<OperationId:N>.bak`, CreateNew, associated target/hash/timestamp in record. Copy from protected original reader, flush/close, verify original SHA, pin backup. No static overwrite; backup remains after success/failure for operator review.

Same-directory unique temp uses CreateNew, WriteThrough, cancellable bounded writes, flush-to-disk, closed encoding-aware content and SHA verification. Transaction rechecks both current target hash and temp desired hash, rejects unexpected attributes/links/security-descriptor differences, and never deletes/overwrites a pre-existing temp collision. Foreign altered temp is not committed and retained rather than blindly cleaned.

**Backend choice:** ordinary File.Replace with a no-write/no-delete target handle cannot commit on this Windows host; opening an unlock-and-replace gap would weaken the precondition. A POSIX rename probe also failed. Certified helper uses Windows **NTFS TxF/KTM**: exclusive transacted target/source handles, same-length private enlistment, transacted temp-name replacement and synchronous CommitTransaction. Tested external in-place write and replacement during the enlisted interval are blocked. On failure/cancellation before durable commit, private transaction rolls back. No unlocked fallback or automatic retry.

TxF is a deprecated/platform-specific Windows facility; availability was proven on this host, not all deployments. Unsupported/disabled/non-NTFS backend fails closed. No new app database or distributed workflow introduced.

## 10. Verification

Commit success alone is insufficient: guarded read-back SHA must equal DesiredHash. Operation records retain approval/backup/commit/actual hash/verification/recovery facts. Final C# verifier requires successful dispatch, exact operation facts/JSON claims, stable policy/intent binding and **fresh target plus backup hash check after model follow-up**. External change during final model turn fails verification without restoring over it.

NoOp requires verified unchanged before=desired state. Conflict, ApprovalDenied, verification failure, recovered failure or RecoveryConflict never becomes Completed through final prose. A committed write followed by model/transport/cancellation failure remains recorded as committed; failure does not imply nothing changed.

## 11. Recovery

Certified case: read-back verification fault while target still equals known committed B and backup still equals original A. Recovery prepares a separate bounded restore temp from immutable original snapshot, transactionally rechecks current B and restores A, then verifies A. Backup retained. RecoverySucceeded is a **non-success tool/run outcome**, not successful completion of the requested mutation.

If current target differs from committed B, or backup identity/hash cannot be trusted, no restore. RecoveryConflict preserves newer external C and original backup. Tests inject C after commit and at recovery boundary. Recovery also uses protected expected-hash transaction, so no unlocked recovery overwrite/retry. Read/restore inability fails safely; full crash/power-loss resume is not claimed.

## 12. Cancellation / commit point

Stages implemented: Prepared, Approved, PreconditionChecked, BackupCreated, TempWritten, CommitStarted, Committed, Verified, Recovering, Recovered. Agent AwaitingApproval/Recovering added only for real behaviors, separate from tool/recovery outcomes.

Commit point is successful synchronous CommitTransaction. Before it: cancellation rolls back/halts with no durable target change, owned temp cleanup where trustworthy. Explicit monotonic authorization check runs immediately before native commit, not merely timer-token inspection. Tests cancel before temp, halfway through temp, before commit and after private transaction staging.

After commit: finite verify/recovery ignores run cancellation so safe-state evaluation completes before lease drain; run still returns cancellation/deadline with truthful commit facts. Tests prove after-commit verification and cancellation during recovery. Kernel transaction timeout is5 seconds; post-commit safe-state completion can outlast the cancelled run deadline, without resetting the run or admitting new model/tool work.

## 13. Write budgets

Immutable MaxWriteOperations default/hard1; MaxWriteRetries fixed0. Accepted native request still consumes overall tool budget. Known write request reserves write slot before arguments/policy/approval, including rejected/invalid attempts. Second write request fails WriteOperationLimitReached, with exact one execution/approval/commit. No hidden write side channel. Recovery is internal remediation of the same operation, not another model-selected write.

Once any write workflow is requested, model transport follow-up retries are disabled as well; pre-write transient HTTP may follow Gate D classifier. Bounds cannot be raised by prompt, tool output or approval.

## 14. Policy

Existing default-deny policy augmented with separate explicit safe-write names and immutable host workspace. SafeWrite admission requires valid run/model/deadline, exact validated arguments matching trusted caller intent, approved existing target/type/size, one available/reserved write slot and no prior operation. Admission returns SafeWriteRequiresApproval, **not permission to commit**. Independent approval and all hash/path checks follow.

Readonly path unchanged; write descriptor not sent to model without configured workspace + explicit SafeTextChange intent/profile. Dangerous/SystemCritical/reversible/process/general write capabilities are not registered for production. Model `approved`, `allowedRoot`, `risk` or budget fields are additional/invalid arguments, not authority.

## 15. Security / adversarial tests

Covered: stale hash before proposal/after approval, approval reject/binding changes, path prefix/traversal/UNC/ADS/device names/mixed separators/reparse/junction/hard-link, missing/binary/invalid UTF-8/oversize target, temp collision/tamper, backup retention, post-temp fault, desired-state idempotence, BOM/CRLF and exact diff, external write/replace during transaction, cancellation on both sides of commit, read-back fault recovery, external C recovery conflict and fresh final-state conflict.

Agent tests cover self-approval/root/risk injection, trusted-intent mismatch, unregistered/second write attempt, disabled/raised budgets, untrusted file instructions, post-write HTTP no-retry, final prose rejection, approval cancellation/navigation/model switch and lease release with truthful committed facts. Native one-call/ID/parser/schema/default-deny adversarial tests retained.

Actual shared Gemma/b11057 proof uses exactly one owned PID; no startup/switch initiated by Agent and no orphan after cleanup. Original fingerprint cache remains once per fixture, not per turn/tool. Chat Messages/SelectedConversation/ActiveConversation and stored detail/config/model-index/design fixture hashes remain unchanged, except expressly approved `sample.txt` plus its backup in isolated workspace.

Real transport cancel-Agent/cancel-Chat isolation passes. Controlled approval waits cancelled/drained by navigation/model switch before runtime stop; model snapshot remains old run identity. Existing Chat/AI CHECK/Image/PDF/history tests execute in both full runs. Renderer/Views/Themes unchanged from checkpoint.

MutationProposed, ApprovalRequested/Granted/Denied, PreconditionChecked, BackupCreated, TempWritten, CommitStarted, Committed, WriteVerified, RecoveryStarted/Completed/Conflict. Correlation RunId/OperationId/ToolCallId, logical target, before/desired/actual hashes, policy/approval/backup identity, outcome and monotonic elapsed. No production full content/diff/prompt/context/secret logging or chain-of-thought capture.

Run-local immutable operation snapshots added to AgentRunProgress; no durable Agent journal/DB. Filesystem backups are retained operation artifacts. Synthetic fixture native responses/events are diagnostic evidence only.

## Certification Blocker Resolution

### Observed failure and frozen baseline

The October3 whole-solution runs recorded visual10/logical8, viewport502px and nominal required455.17px. Blocked checkpoint `87107a7dc3958d36172b151ca2a3072720bc19a4f6d00d5807c14dbe4af4a6f6` preserved unchanged. Initial diagnosis used **the same blocked App/test DLLs**, `--no-build --no-restore`, before any repository code edit. A separately built temporary state runner loaded these DLLs; no safe-write/Agent/runtime/approval/recovery code was changed during repair.

### Minimal reproduction and reduction evidence

- Fresh-process original ASCII class: **3/3 PASS** (`blocker-fresh-control.trx`). Fresh state: Consolas12 Normal/Normal/Normal,96DPI, Ideal/Auto/Auto, LeftToRight, id-ID/en-GB, Dark, viewport502, visual8.
- Actual TRX order identified40 predecessor classes. Second half + ASCII:346/346 PASS. First half produced exact ASCII failure before a later diagnostic run timeout; that incomplete run is diagnostic evidence only. Further reductions including143-case fast first-half subset,57-case subset and76-case subset passed. Actual reduced-run order mattered: selecting Gate A alone placed ASCII **before** its predecessor, so its apparent PASS was not treated as a valid negative causal control.
- Explicit same-process **`LiveChatHistoryAiCheckAndModelLifecycle_UseOneOwnedRuntime → original ASCII assertion` FAIL** (`blocker-explicit-chat-state.log`, `blocker-explicit-chat-width-chain.log`). Explicit image/PDF/theme/96+144DPI predecessor → ASCII PASS. Chat-view with two static messages → ASCII PASS.
- Further reduction removed model/network/OCR entirely: show a Display-mode RichTextBox in a Window, close/detach it, allow its PTS context to be returned by finalization/background dispatcher, then run **the original unchanged ASCII assertion first**. Exact10/8/502/455.17 failure recorded in `blocker-minimal-original-red.log`. GC is used only to reproduce context release deterministically, not as a production fix or retry.

Temporary runner setup failures (dependency resolution/native assets, including wrong ONNX binding/access violation) were classified as **probe-environment failures**, not evidence of Nexus corruption. Exact test-bin dependencies/native paths were used afterward. The one server left by the failed external probe was identified by PID/command line and cleaned before further runtime experiments. No native/harness timeout was increased to pass the actual assertion.

### Root cause

Same font identity,12pt, style/weight/stretch, theme, dictionary source order, culture, flow direction,96DPI, viewport502, paddings and rounding flags. **Actual character rectangles differed:** fresh glyph advance6.5966667 DIP/line height14.05078125; contaminated advance7 DIP/line height14. Nominal Ideal measured width remained455.17, while native flow layout was pixel-aligned Display. Public TextOptions getters could still report Ideal.

Read-only inspection of the blocked runtime's dispatcher PTS context pool showed the Ideal document borrowing an in-use formatter marked **Display**. WPF `PtsCache.AcquireContextCore` chooses a free context by optimal-paragraph flag but omits TextFormattingMode; formatter mode is set only at context creation. `StructuralCache.EnsurePtsContext` always requests the formatter-enabled PTS context. A recycled displayed Chat context can therefore carry Display metrics into a detached Ideal document. Resource/theme restoration cannot correct that hidden formatter pool.

Source corroboration: dotnet/wpf `PresentationFramework/MS/Internal/PtsHost/PtsCache.cs` and `StructuralCache.cs`; same internal pool/formatter fields were inspected on the installed runtime. This is a dispatcher-level framework cache dependency, not safe-write contamination or a leaked theme/font-name change. It can occur in actual application lifecycle as displayed documents are discarded/reused.

### Why isolated runs passed

Fresh/detached renderer tests create or use an Ideal context, so measuring and rendering agree. The long live Chat lifecycle displays messages under ConversationView's Display formatting and allows contexts to be released; the next detached document can borrow the Display formatter. Whether a context remains in use or has been finalized explains the order/lifetime dependency. A capture that creates an extra document can itself change pool availability; original assertions were therefore invoked before additional capture in the minimal red proof.

### Fix and ownership

Two explicit formatting assignments in `src/03_App/Nexus.App/AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs`: the message RichTextBox and temporary no-wrap measurement TextBlock both use **TextFormattingMode.Display**, the existing production Chat formatting contract. Rendering and measurement are now coherent pixel-aligned operations regardless of a prior Display PTS context. This is a component formatting-contract fix, not a renderer rewrite, changed font, new padding/tolerance, hardcoded viewport or private framework cache mutation.

Added `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/MessageTextFormattingIsolationTests.cs`: displayed document → close/detach → deterministic context release → **existing original ASCII assertions**. No existing assertion/expected count or timeout modified; no blanket resource/cache reset or parallelism change. User staged patch and all40 Themes/Views remain byte-identical. Original WpfAppHost behavior unchanged. All Gate E mutation/Agent code matches blocked checkpoint.

### Targeted and impacted evidence

- Minimal context-reuse regression + original ASCII class: **4 passed / 0 failed/skipped** (`blocker-root-fix-green.trx`).
- External minimal runner after fix: original assertion PASS (`blocker-minimal-fixed-state.log`). Explicit real Chat-live predecessor after fix: original assertion PASS (`blocker-live-chat-fixed-state.log`).
- Renderer/ASCII/layout/WPF resources/theme/Conversation/AI CHECK/write boundary relevant selection with visual capture enabled: **156 passed / 0 failed/skipped**,18.029s (`blocker-impacted-final.trx`). Earlier selection had155 pass plus one intentionally non-opted-in visual fact; final selection executes it.
- Frozen mutation/path/kernel tests: **46 passed / 0 failed/skipped** (`blocker-frozen-mutation.trx`). Original Gate E targeted132 and impacted655 evidence remains valid and retained; full final executes all those facts again.

### Final full certification

Exactly one full run after stable targeted/impacted fix: **1,261 passed,0 failed,0 skipped,0 aborted**, all11 projects, Integration**758/758**, wall **753.4018291 seconds**. All Gate A/B/C/D/E live flags and visual capture enabled,6-minute hang watchdog unchanged. Restore/build PASS. # Gate F — one bounded diagnostic-driven repair

**PASS for the reviewed pinned-model Windows/NTFS fixture, 2026-10-05.** Final full certification: **1,323 passed / 0 failed / 0 skipped / 0 aborted**. Production source/repair authority remains default-deny. **STOP after this gate.**

## 1. Scope

One recover-first repair after a recognized required-test equality assertion failure: A approval/apply/build/test, owned-process completion, reverse recovery, verified original baseline, shaped diagnostic observation, independent B proposal/approval/apply/build/test, deterministic final verification. Maximum two source-plan requests and one repair; no Plan C. Existing 2–3 C# files, exact ordered task targets, one unique literal replacement per file. Compiler-error repair is HOLD.

## 2. Starting E.1 checkpoint

Verified archive: `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\nexus-gate-e1-final-source-20261005-102832.zip`, SHA-256 `8db96e04e457adaf6481802d5564c399a43cb1f097bf341ead4cff9941bc03bb`. All 1,028 source files, complete source set, staged/unstaged binary patches and 118 separately archived generated files matched before edits. E.1 certification remains 1,285 PASS, zero fail/skip/abort.

Pre-edit Gate F archive: `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\nexus-gate-f-before-source-20261005-111218.zip`, SHA-256 `506441ae25a23c264c88fa3eefe2a6522377a181aead15aab15868adfff2123d`, 1,028 files; tracked generated outputs separately captured. HEAD remains `a530067973db2847d8c0028c6e17b0242e0ccabd`, branch `audit/nexus-agent-phase0-20261001`. Final review confirms the staged user patch and archived Themes/Views unchanged; no XAML/project/solution/props/targets change relative to E.1. No reset/clean/stash/restore/commit.

## 3. Open-source patterns adapted/rejected

- [Hermes file tools](https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/main/tools/file_tools.py), [security](https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/main/website/docs/user-guide/security.md): adapt freshness invalidation/fresh per-attempt approval; reject shell/runtime/persistence and reusable broad grants.
- [Aider base coder](https://github.com/Aider-AI/aider/blob/main/aider/coders/base_coder.py): adapt failure observation → bounded correction and explicit reflection count; reject shell/test-command configuration and automatic Git writes. Issues [1090](https://github.com/Aider-AI/aider/issues/1090) and [5254](https://github.com/Aider-AI/aider/issues/5254) informed loop/command-boundary rejection; historical issue reports were not treated as blanket claims about every current release.
- [SWE-agent](https://github.com/SWE-agent/SWE-agent/blob/main/sweagent/agent/agents.py): adapt attempt history, cumulative statistics and stop condition; reject terminal, hard-reset, autosubmission and persisted thought/action trajectories.
- [OpenHands versioned controller](https://github.com/OpenHands/OpenHands/blob/0.54.0/openhands/controller/agent_controller.py): adapt controller-owned transitions/action-observation separation; reject generic event bus/runtime/Docker/delegation infrastructure. Current main has changed repository architecture, so the audit used versioned controller evidence.

No extra dependency or foreign agent runtime.

## 4. Existing components reused

`SourceEditPlan`, `SourcePlanApproval`, `IWriteApprovalProvider.RequestPlanAsync`, guarded source snapshots, existing SafePath/SafeWrite/NTFS TxF, reverse recovery, fixed `SourceVerification`, immutable profile, Agent registry/schema validator/policy/executor/native adapter, existing finite orchestrator, deterministic verifier, monotonic progress clock and shared runtime lease. No second writer, approval system, recovery engine, coding framework, DB or DAG.

## 5. Diagnostic shaping

`RequiredAssertionFailure` projects only known required-test identities, logical source path and reviewed integer equality values. The permitted raw message is the exact bounded xUnit integer `Assert.Equal() Failure: Values differ / Expected: 2 / Actual: <single digit>` format; appended prose, injection, unrelated exception or foreign test is rejected. Model message is regenerated from typed integers, not copied from stderr/TRX. Raw output and stack trace are not model feedback.

Feedback fields include AttemptNumber, PlanId, FailureStage/Category, LogicalSourcePath, nullable CompilerCode/Line/Column (null in this slice), RequiredTestName, bounded message, failed-state digest, recovered-base digest, remaining-budget snapshot and compact failed-edit facts. Compiler diagnostic extraction remains disabled. Success receipts normalize empty ImmutableArray facts for existing E.1 JSON evidence compatibility.

## 6. Repair eligibility

Only `KnownRequiredTestAssertionFailure` in A: build PASS, owned test process exits with the recognized failure exit, fresh bounded unique TRX, all three known facts executed exactly once, consistent passed/failed counters, no abort/timeout/nonexecution, only recognized equality failures, failed source belongs to original task, successful recovery, entire baseline/profile intact, valid runtime/model identity and remaining budget. B failure is never eligible.

Approval/policy/path/capability/budget failures, compile errors, stale binary, changed graph/profile, malformed/missing TRX, test-host crash, hash mismatch, invalid final model facts, cancellation/deadline/identity changes and recovery conflicts are terminal. Diagnostic text cannot select a command, tool, profile, target or approval.

## 7. Recover-first transition

Existing runner finishes owned-process cleanup before returning. Existing coordinator recovers reached commits in reverse order. `VerifySourcePlanBaseline` then uses guarded reads to check every original task before hash, all untouched fixture sources and profile graph integrity. `BaselineVerified` and `RecoveredBaseDigest` are retained in the result. The same baseline is rechecked before B preparation and existing all-file preflight after B approval. External edits are preserved, never adopted as a new base.

The initial WPF live prerequisite exposed synchronous recovery waiting on a temp writer that captured the dispatcher context. Existing safe temp writer now uses context-free asynchronous writes/flush/disposal; the same writer and atomic algorithm remain. A dedicated synchronization-context regression and live recover-first proof pass. Early interrupted/hung live logs are retained.

## 8. Attempt history

Run-local `SourceAttemptFact` records RunId, attempt number, PlanId, ParentPlanId, PlanDigest, verification/failure/recovery outcomes and baseline digest. Exact plan/results are retained internally and excluded from ordinary JSON metadata to avoid source/diagnostic logging. Latest pointers coexist with history. A failure survives B success; latest finalization recovery updates B only. No random additional AttemptId, persistent trajectory, DB or crash-resume.

Plan slots are reserved for requests even when arguments are rejected. Prepared-plan history counts actual prepared plans; an invalid B proposal can therefore consume request slot two while history contains only prepared A. These are distinct truthful facts.

## 9. Attempt B proposal authority

The original task supplies fixed ordered targets, workspace/profile and before hashes. B may supply only bounded literal arguments in the existing closed schema. Its target order equals A; optional c arguments are rejected for a two-file task. Preparation retains one unique match, real change, UTF-8/path/file/input/diff/line bounds. For sources without an assertion failure, B must reapply the exact A old/new literals after recovery; only failing sources may propose changed literals. No paths/root/profile/commands/budgets/approval/retry fields.

Explicit fixed-test goals and restore-all/reapply semantics were required: an earlier read-only probe proposed a no-op and failed adequacy before any dispatch. That failed probe is retained in `repair-live-final.trx`; tests were not relaxed. The subsequent explicit-goal cold/warm/contention probes and live proof pass.

## 10. Fresh approval

B is newly prepared with new PlanId/digest, fresh before/desired/diff hashes and independent `RequestPlanAsync`. A approval cannot match B, even when literal edits happen to repeat. Approval replay/denial/cancellation tests pass with zero unauthorized B writes. Only the same approved B plan derives constituent Gate E grants. Production approval remains deny by default.

## 11. Cumulative budgets

Run-wide hard maxima: source plans 2, repairs 1, build-start reservations 2, test-start reservations 2, model turns 4, tool calls 3, failures 2, transient retries 1. Default source plan budget remains 1 with repair budget 0. Counters are not reset between A/B; a start reservation that fails to spawn remains consumed conservatively.

Per-plan caps remain 2,048 diff bytes/six changed lines. Cumulative forward approved-plan preparation caps: 4,096 bytes/12 lines, including denied prepared plans. No recovery/HTTP retry becomes a third source plan. Final verification checks cumulative facts. Repeat/duplicate calls and larger budgets are rejected.

## 12. Deadline probe

Each live Gate F harness first obtains real engineering A failure/recovery facts, then runs a separate read-only model probe. During the probe there is no tool executor dispatch, approval request, source write or build/test; baseline hashes and approval-call count remain unchanged. Engineering failure setup occurs before the probe and is not misrepresented as read-only.

Same pinned Gemma GGUF/native runtime/tokenizer settings and source/diagnostic packet factory as live repair; native requests disable thinking, temperature zero, output cap 256. Cold repair prompt, repeated warm prompt and concurrent Chat are measured; adequacy requires the exact four bounded repair arguments and desired literals, not merely a successful HTTP response. Host-only receipt binds profile digest/model identity/path and the maximum measured latency. Production catalog has no receipt/profile grant.

Measured full live repair: **106.757 seconds**, below existing hard maximum 120. Certification is restricted to explicit 120-second opt-in; default 60 is unchanged and cannot enable repair. Approval waits remain inside the same monotonic deadline. No pause/reset/increase. Human-delay/cold-start feasibility beyond this controlled fixture is not claimed.

Serialized UTF-8 caps: diagnostic packet 2,048 bytes, hash-bound source slices 1,024 bytes, per-attempt result 3,072 bytes, aggregate results 6,144 bytes, repair-context admission 8,192 bytes; existing total context cap 32,768 remains. `AgentSourceRepairTests` covers A→B success, B compile/test failure, changed final digest, A approval replay, approval denial, new/third target, foreign profile/budget fields, duplicate call id, repeated third repair, transport/malformed protocol, stale source during reasoning/approval, untouched source changes, identity/deactivation/cancellation, B owned-process cancellation, B recovery conflict, start-budget exhaustion, nonfailed-file no-op, disabled catalog/default deadline and higher caps rejection. `SourceFailureProjectionTests` covers injection, foreign test, bad counters/abort, unrelated exception/exit, safe projection, dispatcher-context safety and success-receipt serialization.

# Gate H acceptance — approved Option B / Workflow V2

Owner approval: 12 October 2026. Authoritative machine-readable selection contract:
[`gate-h-acceptance-manifest.json`](gate-h-acceptance-manifest.json).

## Old and new acceptance

Previously, the stochastic read-only P1 cold/warm/Chat generation-success probe was
mandatory and its safely rejected OutputLimit stopped certification. The owner now
approves two separately reported suites: **mandatory safety/functional certification**
and **P1 probabilistic reliability diagnostics**. Historical failures and reports are
preserved verbatim; this document changes future acceptance, not historical results.

Only this exact test is reclassified:

```text
Nexus.Integration.Tests.GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.CandidateArrays_ProductionAdapterAndDecoder_ReadOnlyColdWarmChat
```

Its original scenario, schema, 256 native-token cap, 64 Chat-token cap, 120-second
deadline, strict success assertions and validator remain. The test now has
`[Trait("Category","GateHP1Reliability")]`. Failure telemetry records the mode,
stage, exception/reason, available usage and zero-dispatch read-only boundary, then
**rethrows**. Raw requests/responses, xUnit failure and TRX remain visible. The
diagnostic remains independently executable; it is never deleted or silently skipped.

## Mandatory PASS remains unchanged

All discovered cases except the exact single P1 diagnostic are mandatory. This includes:
native protocol validation; malformed/truncated rejection; OutputLimit fail-closed;
zero unauthorized dispatch/mutation; approval A/B independence; SafeWrite/recovery;
multi-hunk correctness; genuine F assertion repair, G compiler repair and H 2+1 repair;
the Agent 120-second deadline; relevant A–H deterministic, WPF, OCR, ONNX and runtime
regression. A genuine repair that fails to generate a valid plan is a functional failure.
No other flaky-test exemption, blanket class exclusion or weakened security boundary.

Mandatory certification requires zero failures/skips/aborts/missing fixtures and safe
cleanup. Unauthorized mutation, invalid approval, acceptance of malformed output or
a deterministic host defect revealed by P1 is also a mandatory blocker.

## Explicit selection and inventory

```text
Mandatory:  Category!=GateHP1Reliability
Diagnostic: Category=GateHP1Reliability&FullyQualifiedName=Nexus.Integration.Tests.GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.CandidateArrays_ProductionAdapterAndDecoder_ReadOnlyColdWarmChat
```

These are reviewed filters, not undocumented exclusions. The manifest declares all
11 solution test projects and names all 13 required live A–H fixtures. Unclassified
tests default to mandatory. Discovery must find exactly one diagnostic-tagged case,
and it must be the named method. Additional tagged tests, missing assemblies/fixtures,
duplicate IDs and failed discovery are rejected by `tools/gate_h_acceptance.py`.

Implementation discovery (fresh VSTest discovery only; no model calls):

| Assembly | Discovered | Mandatory | Diagnostic |
|---|---:|---:|---:|

| Nexus.Foundation.Tests | 35 | 35 | 0 |
| Nexus.AI.EditingLite.Tests | 150 | 150 | 0 |
| Nexus.AI.Llama.Tests | 177 | 177 | 0 |

`nexus-workflow-v2/test-inventory.json`; summary is `discovery-proof.json`. Freeze a

## Future execution commands — not authorized by this implementation approval

Pinned host on the present machine:

```powershell
$dotnet = "C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Microsoft\dotnet\dotnet.exe"
$settings = "C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\environment-dotnet.runsettings"
```

Actual certification must execute these commands through the existing verified Job
wrapper, rather than directly launching unowned process trees. Keep all manifest
`required_live_flags=1`, validated model/engine/fixture paths and evidence folders.
An unset fixture flag is not an acceptable skipped certification test. Test source
fixture requirements remain unchanged.

Updated external tooling paths, already used by Nexus certification:

- `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate_h_approved_preflight.py`
- `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate_h_instrumented_full.py`
- Unchanged `gate_h_certification_job.py` (verified anonymous Job ownership).

For a **new explicitly approved** freeze, set `NEXUS_GATE_H_FREEZE` to the new freeze
directory and `NEXUS_GATE_H_SUITE` to `mandatory` or `diagnostic`. Run preflight then
the wrapper once per suite. Each suite uses a separate evidence directory and exclusive
`run-started.json`; consumed historical approval/evidence cannot be reused.

New `candidate.json` must retain source/generated archives, source/input manifests,
binary/fixture/SDK/runsettings/environment identities and staged SHA from the existing
freeze mechanism, plus `acceptance_contract="NEXUS-GATE-H-OPTION-B-V2"`,
`acceptance_manifest_sha256`, and `test_inventory_sha256`. Put the exact frozen
`test-inventory.json` beside it. Source freeze must include the policy, acceptance,
test and repository tooling changes; external tooling changes must be hashed too.
Execution configuration stores the original unfiltered solution command; preflight
appends only the reviewed suite filter and routes evidence folders.

New freeze `approval.json` must be supplied from the explicit owner approval, with
matching candidate ID/SHA, acceptance-manifest SHA, inventory SHA,
`explicit_user_approval=true`, `authorized_suites=["mandatory","diagnostic"]` and
`authorized_runs_per_suite=1`. The updated preflight does not grant its own Option B
approval. Historical default mode retains its consumed-run guard.

## Reporting and safe diagnostic failure

Report discovered totals, mandatory executed/pass/fail/skip/missing and diagnostic
executed/pass/fail/skip separately. Exact TRX result IDs must match the frozen inventory;
duplicate, unknown, wrong-suite and missing results block certification. Report native
successes, OutputLimit, invalid tool responses, cold/warm/Chat modes, usage tokens and
failure traces from P1 proof and raw responses. Unattempted modes after failure stay
unattempted; no retry/top-up.

The wrapper lets a failed diagnostic finish writing TRX. It never relabels its failed
result PASS. `tools/gate_h_acceptance.py` automatically recognizes a **RELIABILITY FAILURE**
only when proof and actual TRX trace agree on a native-generation `ToolProtocolException`
rejection and record nativeSuccess=false with zero mutation/dispatch/approval. Missing
evidence, skip/abort, unexpected host/transport/lifecycle/assertion failure or any authority
violation remains a mandatory blocker requiring review. Other safely rejected stochastic
failures may be reviewed under this same owner contract; no speculative exemption is
automatically inferred from a generic assertion failure.

Combine both suites explicitly, preserving diagnostic fail counts:

```powershell
python "tools/gate_h_acceptance.py" --manifest "docs/gate-h-acceptance-manifest.json" --inventory "<new-freeze>/test-inventory.json" --mandatory-results "<new-evidence>/mandatory/full" --diagnostic-results "<new-evidence>/diagnostic/full" --diagnostic-evidence "<new-evidence>/diagnostic" --mandatory-invocation "<new-evidence>/mandatory/run-result.json" --diagnostic-invocation "<new-evidence>/diagnostic/run-result.json"
```

Mandatory PASS plus an evidenced safely rejected diagnostic reliability failure can
be reviewed without automatically failing mandatory Gate H. Final Gate H certification
and Gate I progression still require their respective owner reviews; **Gate I HOLD**.

## Implementation boundary

No complete solution certification or long live generation test runs in this task.
Targeted decoder/fail-closed, H and impacted F/G/H regressions plus wrapper/selection
safety checks verify the implementation. Reuse prior evidence only where source/test
identity permits and label it historical. Request a **new Gate H source freeze**, then
STOP; implementation approval is not full certification approval.

**Recommendation: RECOVERY PARTIAL — UNRESOLVED FAILURES**

Three original WPF failures are corrected and verified. Historical Gate B HTTP `ResponseEnded` and BiRefNet `bad allocation` remain unresolved: current targeted reproductions pass, but a verified causal repair of the historical failures has not been established. Gate H remains BLOCKED; Gate I remains HOLD. No full certification run or PASS checkpoint was created.

## 1. Starting source identity

- Verified blocked archive: `nexus-gate-h-certification-blocked-source-20261009-164159.zip`.
- SHA256: `ca96819264ef3e34802db87638e9527ca72e1aa46488884274b1237ca902aa46`.

- Starting working tree: 635 files under the existing checkpoint source definition. Only difference from the blocked source archive: the authorized OCR test-only correction.
- HEAD: `a530067973db2847d8c0028c6e17b0242e0ccabd`.
- All 22 solution projects verified. Fresh read-only MSBuild evaluation verifies 428 active repository source/copy/import/configuration inputs, zero missing. Four copied `None` inputs are included; inert `None` items are excluded. An initial narrower 424-input enumeration omitted those four and was corrected, not interpreted as missing files.
- Tracked generated outputs remain separate: 118 paths. Generated binaries rebuilt during verification are not certified source.

Evidence: `docs/gate-h-recovery-evidence/starting-source-identity.json`, `current-active-input-manifest.json`, project evaluation files and `active-input-scope-reconciliation.json`.

## 2. Concurrent/user work preservation

Starting source hashes and staged bytes were saved before editing. No reset, restore, stash, clean, deletion or Git commit. No unexplained concurrent affected-source differences were found at start or final review. Existing source/UI work is preserved.

Staged patch SHA256 remains `b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`.

The OCR correction remains byte-identical: SHA256 `c0ddc8e77b4c2cd7f78b41823871bb042de86143718e81292a35b6f59f919391`. Clipboard implementation and OCR pipeline/ViewModel source remain untouched.

One build encountered a transient `.NET Host` output lock. The process had exited when inspected; no foreign process was terminated. A subsequent build succeeded. Final source comparison confines changes to the eleven reviewed WPF/diagnostic paths plus this report.

## 3. Root cause of ResponseEnded

**Localized, not causally resolved.** Historical `ResponseEnded` is the .NET `HttpIOException` error category, not a Nexus response-completion event. Historical stack fails inside `HttpConnection.SendAsync`, reached from `GateBToolCapabilityTests.SendRaw` and streamed-round-trip follow-up at old line 149.

That follow-up request explicitly has `stream=false`. The preceding SSE response passed its `[DONE]`, native tool-call identity and argument checks. Therefore this failure is not evidence of a missing Nexus terminal-event emission, double completion event, or content/event ordering bug. The failing transport operation ends before response headers are returned. The historical run completed two nonstream round trips and cleaned up its owned server.

Four fresh original-case runs with transport tracing PASS. PASS timelines show the SSE connection closed/replaced before the subsequent request headers are sent on a new connection. b11057's official `server-http.cpp` uses a chunked provider ending with `sink.done()` and returns `has_next`; this and HTTP pool reuse are relevant investigation leads. They do not prove the historical peer-close race without a matching traced FAIL.

No fresh traced FAIL was obtained. Historical evidence lacks connection IDs/peer-close timeline sufficient to settle the deeper cause. No delay, retry, exception suppression, fabricated event, pool policy change, or runtime rewrite was added.

Audited actual sources: `Nexus.AI.Llama/Pipelines/LlamaConversationPipeline.cs`, `Services/LlamaClientService.cs`, `Services/GenericLlamaEngine.cs`, `Services/LlamaServerManager.cs`, `Nexus.App/Services/ConversationRuntimeWorkflow.cs`, `RuntimeConsumerLease.cs`, `Nexus.Conversation/Manager/ConversationManagerService.cs`, transport parsing and relevant tests. Their production bytes are unchanged.

## 4. Streaming fix and exact files

**No production fix claimed.** `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/GateBToolCapabilityTests.cs` adds diagnostic evidence only:

- System.Net.Http/System.Net.Sockets EventListener events and connection IDs.
- Existing server lifecycle events.
- Per-request start, headers, response lines/SSE frames, EOF, cancellation state, exception and disposal-scope timeline.
- Partial response preservation on failure.
- Timeline serialization deferred until fixture cleanup, reducing observation overhead in the immediate stream/follow-up transition.

Payloads, assertions, timeout, model identity, protocol and shared runtime ownership remain unchanged. The fixture does not borrow an Agent consumer lease; no Agent lease identity is invented in its trace. Fresh Chat/Agent causal event-completion coverage beyond the existing tests remains a prerequisite for any later lifecycle change.

## 5. Gate B regression results

- Original native tool-call/streamed-round-trip case: four separate runs, each 1/1 PASS.
- Existing `Nexus.AI.Llama.Tests`: 177/177 PASS.
- SharedRuntimeAgentTests, ConversationRuntimeWorkflowTests, ToolProtocolTests and AgentOrchestrationTests: 83/83 PASS.
- Zero skips, aborts or failures in these completed runs. Per-live-run server cleanup assertions pass; final process inspection finds no llama-server/testhost/Nexus.App process.

These passes validate current behavior and the diagnostic addition; they do not certify the historical failure as repaired. Historical FAIL and fresh PASS evidence remain separately retained.

## 6. ONNX allocation root cause

**Unresolved.** Exact historical exception: `Microsoft.ML.OnnxRuntime.OnnxRuntimeException : [ErrorCode:Fail] bad allocation`, thrown by `InferenceSession.Run` at `PerfAuditBenchmarkTests.cs:199` (timed inference, not session creation). Historical reported duration: 141.9799315 seconds. Commit/headroom measurements were not captured during that run.

The unchanged benchmark passes isolated, under 4 GiB additional measured commit pressure, and after an explicit relevant cold/warm predecessor followed by two threading-method repetitions in one process. Native peak is substantial, but observed memory returns near baseline after session disposal. A genuine historical leak, unintended concurrent session construction, fixture interference or environment exhaustion cannot be chosen as the cause from the existing evidence.

The benchmark creates one scoped session per thread configuration and disposes each Run result. Benchmark SessionOptions are not explicitly disposed; official ORT guidance identifies this as a lifetime concern, but its contribution to this historical large allocation failure is unmeasured. The manager's GetOrAdd/factory behavior also merits causal concurrent-construction tracing if implicated. Neither concern was relabeled as the proven root cause or repaired speculatively.

## 7. ONNX fix and memory measurements

**No ONNX source/configuration change applied.** Model, resolution, quality, acceptance, CPU arena and thread settings are preserved.

| Probe | Measurements |
|---|---|
| Isolated benchmark | 1/1 PASS; 151.238 s; minimum available commit 18,870,411,264 bytes. Initial process sampler missed dotnet-hosted testhost; its zero private-byte reading is invalid, not a zero-memory claim. |
| Corrected sampler + 4 GiB commit pressure | 1/1 PASS; 153.923 s; peak host commit 17,068,544,000 bytes; minimum available commit 14,377,091,072 bytes. Pressure is privately allocated/released by the external wrapper; benchmark unchanged. |
| Explicit cold/warm predecessor + threading twice | Exact unchanged methods PASS; peak commit 17,107,636,224 bytes; minimum available commit 16,703,225,856 bytes. After method disposal/return: private bytes 103,059,456; 104,075,264; 199,983,104. |

The explicit harness records managed heap/commit, GC counts and process memory before/after each method. Last managed heap approximately 28.8 MB, managed committed approximately 64.2 MB. Input stays `[1,3,1024,1024]`; threading benchmark stays `[1,2,4,6,8,12]`, default sequential ORT execution/default arena and no intentional concurrent benchmark inference.

An external reflection-harness setup initially resolved an incompatible native ORT library and crashed with AccessViolation before inference. Explicit NativeLibrary import resolution to the existing test output's win-x64 ORT library corrected that harness. These setup crashes are not the historical `bad allocation` and are not counted as successful benchmark runs. No model/native runtime replacement was made.

The evidence demonstrates release of large session allocations in these runs, not a universal absence of all native leaks. Arbitrary unmanaged allocations/session internals were not traced; historical native allocation ownership remains unknown.

## 9. Three WPF failure causes

Original three tests reproduced as 0 PASS / 3 FAIL before correction.

1. **BASELINE/INVENTORY DRIFT + INTENTIONAL DESIGN IMPROVEMENT:** Sidebar toggle migrated from literal `&#xE90C;` plus explicit `NexusTypographyIconFontFamily` to `NexusIcon.Glyph="SidebarToggle"`. Exact source comparison proves one retired font consumer, 8 -> 7, total primitive debt 126 -> 125. Preserve the icon improvement.
2. **GENUINE DEFECT, layering:** Photos-style Image Tool navigation introduces seven module-local RGBA literals and a common Button template. The visual improvement is intentional; defining common template/palette in the module view violates existing design-system ownership.
3. **BASELINE/INVENTORY DRIFT:** Frozen primitive allowlist still expects eight Conversation icon-font references. Actual seven; this is the same exact retired consumer, not a reason to restore a redundant font setting.

Impacted tests additionally enforce no hex literals in canonical Buttons.xaml and SystemColors in High Contrast semantic brushes. Those contracts are preserved, not weakened.

Remaining module debt: primitive 125, hardcoded hex 137, module-named style 7, common Button template 1. No new module exception is added. ConversationView.xaml itself remains unchanged.

## Berkas: GATE_H_CLIPBOARD_CONTINUOUS_RECOVERY_REPORT.md
*Lokasi Asli:* `D:\NEXUS\Nexus-CSharp\GATE_H_CLIPBOARD_CONTINUOUS_RECOVERY_REPORT.md`

# Gate H — Clipboard Continuous Recovery

Tanggal: 12 Oktober 2026. Metode: **OBSERVE → HYPOTHESIZE → TEST → FIX → VERIFY**.

## 1. Hasil utama

**CLIPBOARD READER ISOLATION DEFECT RESOLVED — REQUEST NEW GATE H FREEZE**

Kelemahan isolasi pada native **test reader** terbukti dan diperbaiki: reader memakai
`OpenClipboard(NULL)`, sehingga opener NULL lain dapat membuka clipboard yang sama dan
menutup open-state milik reader pertama. Hasilnya `GetClipboardData(13)` gagal dengan
`ERROR_CLIPBOARD_NOT_OPEN=1418`, walaupun data/format dan sequence tidak berubah.

Fix minimal menggunakan **message-only HWND milik test** hanya selama read scope.
Production native writer, owner-selection, CF_UNICODETEXT, movable global memory,
legacy SetText, OCR success-only status dan retry contract tetap unchanged.

Ada tiga lapis bukti: natural reproduction pada original first payload, controlled
interleaving yang menghasilkan signature sama, dan regression test **RED sebelum fix →
GREEN sesudah fix** pada interleaving identik. Post-fix targeted/impacted verification
mencakup **77 unique xUnit cases PASS**, ordered predecessor **12/12 PASS**, dan original
STA cases **3/3 PASS**.

**Batas klaim:** aktor/interleaving persis dalam failed full-suite invocation historis
tidak direkam; tidak diklaim telah mengidentifikasi aplikasi Windows penyebabnya.
Yang terbukti adalah defect reader yang dapat menghasilkan signature sama, serta
natural reproduction baru yang cocok dengan mekanismenya. Historical failure tetap
failure; **Gate H belum certified dan Gate I tetap HOLD**.

## 2. Audit kondisi yang dilanjutkan

Baseline terbaru berisi **650 source/document entries**: previous recovery telemetry
dan laporan sebelumnya dipertahankan. Semua baseline hashes cocok, tanpa unknown
source conflict. Git status/staged/unstaged patches dan before affected source/harness
tersimpan di external evidence. Staged patch tetap:

`b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`.

Saat user meminta melanjutkan dari tool terakhir, pemeriksaan read-only proses tidak
menemukan `dotnet`, `testhost`, atau `llama-server` aktif. Tidak ada foreign process
termination. Semua invocation memakai unchanged verified anonymous Job helper dan
recorded membership kosong sesudah cleanup.

Consumed approval, failed TRX, old diagnostic markers dan frozen archives tidak diubah.
Tidak ada reset/restore/clean/stash/commit/merge, rollback UI/UX atau production patch.

## 3. Validity ordered-predecessor evidence

Dependency closure external harness diperiksa: existing STJ v10 dan Encodings.Web v10.
Dry run melakukan actual JSON serialization/parsing atas snapshot helper tanpa model
generation, test method execution atau clipboard write. Dry PASS.

Ordered attempt pertama menemukan setup defect berbeda: external base directory tidak
memiliki trailing separator, sehingga WPF SiteOfOrigin asset URI mencari
`bin/Debug/Assets/MyImage.jpg` alih-alih `bin/Debug/net8.0-windows/Assets/MyImage.jpg`.
Failure terjadi pada first predecessor setup; bukan native clipboard failure. Artifact
disimpan sebagai `ordered-results.json`/`ordered.log`, tidak direlabel PASS.

Fix eksternal satu baris menambahkan directory separator. Dry berikutnya memverifikasi
WPF startup serta serialization. New identity `clipboard-continuous-ordered-wpf-1`
kemudian menghasilkan valid 12-step record. Setiap method dipanggil secara synchronous/
awaited reflection; ordinal, start/end timestamp dan exception dicatat per step.
**VSTest filter tidak digunakan atau diklaim menjamin urutan.**

Urutan yang benar-benar selesai:

1. `AIWorkspaceRenderingTests.Streaming_OpenFenceKeepsCodeTableAndCopyUsesLatestContent`
2. `AIWorkspaceRenderingTests.CodeBlockHeader_CopyActionCopiesOnlyCodeContent`
3. `ConversationViewModelTests.CopyMessageCommand_CopiesPlainTextWithoutMarkdown`
4. `ConversationViewModelTests.CopyMessageCommand_IsAvailableForUserAndAssistant`
5. `UnifiedFeedbackTests.AiCheck_CopyCorrection_Success_SetsIsCopiedOnly_NoSnackbar`
6. `UnifiedFeedbackTests.AiCheck_CopyCorrection_Failure_ShowsSnackbarError`
7. `FileManagementShellTests.FileManagementView_ReusesOneHostAndBothChildViewsWhilePreservingState`
8. `FileManagementShellTests.SwitchingTabs_PreservesChildViewAndVisualStateIdentity`
9. `FileManagementShellTests.SwitchingTabs_PreservesIndependentChildViewModelState`
10. Original live native test — single-line payload.
11. Original live native test — Unicode/symbol/multiline payload.
12. Original live native test — mixed CRLF/LF payload.

Pre-fix sequence **12/12 PASS** pada 08:18:21.898–08:18:24.051 UTC; post-fix sequence
**12/12 PASS**. Verifier memastikan ordinal1–12 dan setiap previous end≤next start.
Ini representative bounded WPF/copy predecessor evidence, bukan rekonstruksi seluruh
1479 detik full-suite history. Original methods/assembly dipakai; bukan mock-success.

## 4. Hypotheses dan evidence pembeda

### H1 — clipboard berubah setelah write

Previous controlled EmptyClipboard menghasilkan sequence change dan CF13 loss; itu
valid positive control, tidak membuktikan external interference historis. Natural
reproduction terbaru justru menunjukkan sequence1752 stabil dan CF13 tetap tersedia,
sehingga **clear/overwrite bukan penjelasan untuk observed read failure terbaru**.

### H2 — data ada tetapi native read gagal

Follow-up STA probe menjalankan tiga original live payloads sekali. Payload pertama
gagal tanpa injection, dua berikutnya PASS; failed output dipertahankan:

```text
Before write: sequence1747
Native write: true
After write: sequence1752; ownerHWND1379292; ownerPID18228; CF13=true
Before read: sequence1752; owner sama; CF13=true; apartmentSTA
OpenClipboard: true, attempt0
GetClipboardData(13): NULL, immediate error1418
Read-locked snapshot: sequence1752; owner sama; CF13=true
Expected: "Single line native text"; Actual: null
```

Microsoft mendefinisikan1418 sebagai **thread does not have a clipboard open**. GlobalLock
belum dipanggil karena handle NULL. Jadi bukan bukti kehilangan data atau GlobalLock
allocation failure; open-state yang diasumsikan helper tidak bertahan.

Probe held-null-open awal tidak menghasilkan expected open failure: opener kedua juga
sukses. Hypothesis bahwa NULL opener otomatis mengisolasi reader **dibantah**, dan
result `all=false` tersebut dipertahankan (`locked-read-results.json`). Classification
text di artifact itu adalah expected hypothesis, bukan established outcome.

Follow-up terarah memakai interleaving eksplisit, satu invocation:

```text
write=true; first OpenClipboard(NULL)=true
second thread OpenClipboard(NULL)=true; second CloseClipboard=true
first GetClipboardData(13)=NULL/error1418
sequence1887 →1887; CF13=true
```

### H3 — apartment/global-state predecessor

Valid ordered MTA sequence PASS, sedangkan natural STA first-case failure di atas
menunjukkan **sekadar pindah ke STA bukan solusi**. WPF/AI CHECK predecessors tidak
terbukti sebagai penyebab deterministik. Root mechanism dipersempit ke NULL-HWND open
scope, bukan perubahan architecture WPF, dispatcher, OCR atau native writer.

Control pada interleaving identik menggunakan distinct test-owned reader HWND:

```text
first OpenClipboard(readerHWND)=true
second OpenClipboard(NULL)=false; second Close tidak dipanggil
first GetClipboardData(13)=valid handle; error0
sequence1908 →1908; CF13=true
```

Ini bukan delay/retry atau fake success; read scope yang benar mencegah competitor
mengambil bagian dalam open-state yang sama.

## 5. Exact minimal fix

Satu repository executable source file berubah pada task ini:

`src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/NexusClipboardTests.cs`

- `ClipboardReaderWindow` kecil, private dan test-only membuat native message-only
  `STATIC` HWND pada thread pemanggil dengan `HWND_MESSAGE` (-3).
- `ReadClipboardNative` memakai `OpenClipboard(readerWindow.Handle)` dan membuang HWND
  setelah CloseClipboard/read scope selesai; creation/destruction failure tetap error.
- Existing failure-only telemetry mencatat `readerHwnd` pada read-open record.
- Optional private `afterOpen` callback digunakan regression test untuk exact competing
  opener schedule; original live tests tidak memasang callback.
- Satu mandatory `[Fact]` baru:
  `NativeRead_OpenScope_CannotBeClosedByAnotherNullWindowOpener`.

Regression memakai real native write, actual competing thread OpenClipboard(NULL), lalu
assert competitor denied, exact Unicode/newline payload dan CF13. **RED** dengan reader
NULL lama: `Assert.False(competingOpen)` gagal karena actual=true, completed failed TRX.
Setelah one surgical fix, regression **GREEN** dalam all-clipboard run. Fix attempts=1.

Semua **30 original assertions** dipertahankan sebagai ordered subsequence. Native
payloads, format exclusions (`DataObject`, `Ole Private Data`), reader10 attempts/20ms
sleep, writer existing retries dan null-failure criteria tidak diubah. Live test tetap
real Win32; tidak ada mock-only replacement, fallback atau read=null PASS.

Production `NexusClipboard.cs`, OCR native copy path/status, legacy SetText, P6 authority,
all UI/theme/rendering source dan original xUnit serialization settings unchanged.

## 7. Open-source decisions

| Source / pattern | Decision / rationale |
|---|---|
| Existing Nexus native writer, telemetry reader, xUnit, WpfAppHost | **USE / ENHANCE** only test read ownership; existing C#/.NET8/WPF infrastructure |
| Microsoft Win32 Clipboard API guidance | **USE** explicit HWND, CloseClipboard lifetime, movable memory and immediate error contracts |
| Microsoft PowerToys local reference, MIT | **REVIEW / REJECT direct transplantation**; test helper uses FormsClipboard STA and swallowed/default errors/retries inappropriate for strict native mandatory proof |
| PowerToys MeasureTool native clipboard example | **REVIEW / NOT NEEDED**; not a drop-in replacement for Nexus checked memory-transfer contract |
| New clipboard service, OLE path, framework/dependency | **NOT NEEDED** |

Sources reviewed:
- [Microsoft GetClipboardSequenceNumber](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winuser/nf-winuser-getclipboardsequencenumber): sequence tracks changed/emptied content for a window station, not read-open ownership.
- [Microsoft SetClipboardData](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winuser/nf-winuser-setclipboarddata): movable memory/ownership transfer.
- [Microsoft Using the Clipboard](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/dataxchg/using-the-clipboard): explicit HWND open/read/close examples.
- [Microsoft system error1418](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/debug/system-error-codes--1300-1699-).
- `D:\NEXUS\Audit\Referensi\PowerToys-main\src\common\UITestAutomation.Next\ClipboardHelper.cs`,
  `src\modules\MeasureTool\MeasureToolCore\Clipboard.cpp`, repository MIT LICENSE.

PowerToys adalah mature Microsoft project; local snapshot dipakai untuk pattern/license
comparison, bukan klaim audit semua versi/security. Tidak ada copied component atau new
product dependency. Web raw-path404 yang ditemui tidak diperlakukan sebagai source evidence.

## 5. Win32 evidence — apa yang terbukti dan belum

### Native writer

Current unchanged production method hanya mengembalikan true pada native path setelah
`OpenClipboard`, `EmptyClipboard`, dan nonzero `SetClipboardData(CF_UNICODETEXT,hGlobal)`.
Live test memastikan `CustomNativeSetTextAction=null`. Source memakai movable global
memory, UTF-16 LE + null terminator, `GlobalUnlock`, transfer ownership saat SetClipboardData
sukses, dan `GlobalFree` hanya bila ownership belum transferred. Tidak ada OLE fallback.

D1 ketiga original payloads melewati native success, exact content, CF13 dan no-OLE-private-
format assertions. Passing observations sengaja tidak dicetak; ini tidak memberi
sequence/owner proof untuk historical failed write.

### Controlled D3

```text
native write success: true
sequence after write: 1076
sequence after explicit test-owned EmptyClipboard: 1077
sequence before/inside read: 1077
owner HWND/PID: 0/0
CF_UNICODETEXT available: false
OpenClipboard: true (attempt0), error0
GetClipboardData(13): handle0, immediate error1168
read text: null; formats=[]
```

`GlobalLock` tidak dipanggil karena data handle NULL; tidak dilaporkan sebagai GlobalLock
failure. Error1168 adalah observed `ERROR_NOT_FOUND`, bukan stale error dari operasi
yang berhasil. Scenario ini membuktikan diagnostic helper dapat membedakan read-open
sukses dari format/data yang hilang setelah explicit clear. Actor kontrol berasal dari
test harness, **bukan observed aplikasi Windows lain**. Prior Unicode payload dikembalikan
dengan existing native writer jika tersedia; bukan full rich-format snapshot restoration.

### Historical cause

Original failing invocation tidak merekam sequence, owner, CF13 availability, native
handles atau errors. Jadi belum diketahui apakah:

- format/data hilang antara successful write dan read;
- owner berubah oleh external actor;
- `GetClipboardData` gagal sementara format masih ada;
- `GlobalLock` gagal pada valid handle;
- previous WPF/Dispatcher/global-state sequence material terhadap failure.

Assembly integration sudah serial; original live method berjalan tanpa explicit STA
marshalling. Win32 Clipboard APIs tidak disamakan dengan WPF OLE Clipboard STA contract.
MTA sendiri tidak terbukti defect: D1 live native assertions PASS dan D3 berhasil pada
MTA. Tidak mengubah dispatcher/apartment/owner HWND berdasarkan hipotesis.

Relevant WPF copy tests menggunakan existing `WpfAppHost.Run`/STA dan beberapa OLE
clipboard save/restore paths. Mereka merupakan kandidat predecessor yang relevan,
bukan bukti bahwa asynchronous/external overwrite benar-benar terjadi saat failure.

## 12. Runtime/process cleanup and wrapper attribution error

First failure triggered CTRL_BREAK for the approved run process group at **03:54:58.561856 UTC**, approximately 0.71 s after observation. Existing failure fixture had already stopped its runtime in finally. Stop/remaining-output processing is part of the same invocation, not a rerun.

Post-run process inspection found **no llama-server, testhost or Nexus.App process**. Freeze handles were released after identity checks.

**Cleanup mistake:** the external wrapper retained past parent PIDs in its ancestry set. A reused parent PID caused **pre-existing Taskmgr.exe PID 19536** to be misclassified as run-owned. Task Manager was created at **02:52:29.740068 UTC**, before run start **03:32:55.239814 UTC**, and was terminated by cleanup at **03:55:10.241265 UTC**. This violated the intended foreign-process preservation constraint. It occurred after the native OutputLimit failure and did not cause that first failure. The assistant has disclosed the mistake; no further process mutation or automatic restoration was attempted.

Raw ownership labels in run-result.json must not be treated as authoritative for every PID. `process_cleanup_attribution_error` in certification-analysis.json preserves the correction. Source/config/staged hashes are independent and remain intact. The wrapper's process-ownership/cleanup logic requires separate review before reuse; no fix or new run is performed in this certification-only task.

## 3. Diagnosis MissingMethodException

Exception pada eksperimen sebelumnya:

```text
Method not found: 'System.Net.Http.Json.JsonContent System.Net.Http.Json.JsonContent.Create(!!0, System.Net.Http.Headers.MediaTypeHeaderValue, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions)'.
```

`binding-before.json` merekam identitas runtime, bukan hanya nama file:

| Assembly/parameter | Version | ALC | MVID |
|---|---|---|---|
| Test-output `System.Text.Json` | 10.0.0.0 | P1DecisionDiagnostic | d70ba6e6-b1d1-49f5-903e-009e34594ac3 |
| Framework `System.Net.Http.Json` | 8.0.0.0 | Default | 0b994795-847a-469b-b18d-29dcedfe43a4 |
| `JsonContent.Create` serializer parameter assembly | 8.0.0.0 | Default | a44cf72a-f592-42e9-a9f0-49a564199a65 |

Integration test bin menyediakan `System.Text.Json.dll` v10 tetapi tidak menyediakan `System.Net.Http.Json.dll`. Resolver custom sebelumnya membiarkan Http.Json jatuh ke Default ALC. Parameter serializer milik method tersebut bukan tipe serializer milik assembly yang dipakai pipeline: `sameSerializerIdentity=false`.

Perbaikan minimal: preload test-output `System.Text.Json` pada custom ALC, lalu `LoadFromAssemblyPath` **file Http.Json v8 yang sudah terpasang pada runtime aktif** ke ALC yang sama, sebelum memuat/JIT pipeline. Referensi serializer Http.Json kemudian mengarah ke STJ v10 yang telah dimuat. `binding-after.json` merekam `sameSerializerIdentity=true`, MVID file Http.Json tetap sama, dan kedua assembly berada pada `P1DecisionDiagnostic`. BCL lintas boundary seperti `HttpClient`, `Task`, dan primitive types tetap shared seperti harness sebelumnya.

Dasar resmi: [Microsoft — About AssemblyLoadContext](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/core/dependency-loading/understanding-assemblyloadcontext), terutama versioning rules, load into the correct ALC, dan type identity. Ini perbaikan untuk isolated diagnostic host; keberhasilannya dibuktikan lewat production method yang sebenarnya. Tidak ada modifikasi terhadap `LlamaConversationPipeline.cs:71–74` (`JsonContent.Create(payload)`).

Identitas external harness sebelum: `6e65430d15606dd09b75b6e3814daf462de388876913cafc077caad9df58cfb9`. Harness frozen sesudah: `9c78ec6f0254d2925fe373f1367c2f3b97064033283ae9801044481db3eff14c`. External Diagnostic DLL frozen: `50aea8656be3a65a7edf4f02f8db25b86caab36440860b47f225c18af10277f2`.
