# Gate H P1 Reliability Decision Audit

**INCONCLUSIVE — RETAIN GATE H HOLD**

Read-only Nexus audit. No production/test/XAML/configuration, token cap, deadline, runtime/parser/model/dependency or authority change. No full certification. Historical failure is genuine OutputLimit; its trigger remains unresolved. Eight native diagnostics returned valid calls, but all intended concurrent-Chat conditions were invalidated by an external diagnostic assembly-binding error before Chat HTTP. Those conditions are UNVERIFIED, not PASS.

## 1. Baseline and source identities

Both ZIPs verified independently, including entry hashes:

| Reference | SHA256 | Scope |
|---|---|---|
| Historical gate-h-instrumented-20261010-100928 | `2bb26a498de63e3cdae6f8f9d48f35cf3d5ea79e42ccca2ee03953423c36301f` | 636 source/document entries; no external wrapper source manifest |
| Recovery gate-h-p1-wrapper-recovery-20261010-145652 | `41cfdb85b619f9766c91b1b92183b15fa4808562212fc36a7858cf3ebd605e34` | 639 source/document entries plus five external certification tooling files |

Recovery's three additional documents are source-freeze review, instrumented certification report and P1/wrapper recovery report. Manifests are not assumed identical. All 636 historical and 639 recovery working-tree identities match; all five external recovery tooling hashes match.

Staged patch remains `b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`. P6 host-owned repair authority, OCR native clipboard/isolation, WPF corrections and AI Workspace improvements unchanged. This audit report is an output outside those snapshots. No restore/reset/stash/clean/delete/commit or overlapping affected-source edit.

Read the instrumented/recovery/P1 reports, original P1 fixture, adapter, decoder, shared-runtime/Chat lifecycle and verified Job Object helper. Current supporting binaries used directly; external package-free diagnostic harness built separately under the verified job.

## 2. Open-source implementations actually reviewed

Pinned b11057 source, fetched as read-only evidence and hashed:

- `tools/server/server-common.cpp`: string tool_choice parsing, request kwargs, thinking override, grammar/parser admission.
- `common/chat.cpp`: REQUIRED enum conversion, template inputs and Gemma4 selection.
- `common/parsers/gemma4.cpp`: actual native parser/grammar and tool-name traversal.
- `tools/server/server-context.cpp`: available-slot/LCP/LRU selection, cache update and per-task sampler construction.

Verified facts: server maps string `required` to REQUIRED; forwards it to Gemma4; Gemma4 tool-call repeat minimum is 1 for REQUIRED and grammar_lazy=false. It nevertheless allows thought/content and scan-to-toolcall prefix before eventual tool call. A finite 256-token generation can stop before that call; required grammar is not a bounded-time/token completion guarantee. Tool names are taken from exposed functions; its generic Gemma4 dictionary grammar does not apply each function's parameter JSONSchema. Strict host array validation remains necessary.

Server-context source creates/reset sampler from per-task sampling options (around line 1783). This contradicts assuming stale sampler reuse from cache-count similarity alone. Slot selection skips busy slots and selects available LCP/LRU matches; source behavior does not itself prove corruption or cross-session state mutation.

USE existing Nexus adapter/strict decoder/lease and Microsoft HTTP cancellation primitives; ADAPT read-only HTTP/slot observation in an external diagnostic; USE the verified Windows Job Object wrapper unchanged. REJECT runtime/grammar/parser replacement, retries and cap/deadline relaxation. No new framework/package dependency.

Source URLs are under `https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/b11057/` with the paths above. Raw sources and SHA256 are in offline-analysis.json/experiment-plan.json. A requested common/chat-parser.cpp URL was absent; it is not falsely claimed reviewed. Actual specialized Gemma4 parser source was inspected instead.

## 3. Historical failure reconstruction

Test: `GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.CandidateArrays_ProductionAdapterAndDecoder_ReadOnlyColdWarmChat`. Failed GenerateAsync at line 58; NativeToolProtocol.Parse raises OutputLimit at line 38.

Pinned model/runtime fingerprint: Gemma-4-E4B, `b11057-59657a613`; historical model/executable hashes retained by approved freeze. Native options: temperature=0, max_tokens=256, stream=false, enable_thinking=false, required, one source-plan tool.

| Historical mode | Prompt | Cached | Completion | Finish | Native call |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| Cold | 1076 | 0 | 79 | tool_calls | Valid a:2+b:1 |
| Warm | 1076 | 1075 | 79 | tool_calls | Valid a:2+b:1 |
| Chat | 1076 | 1074 | 256 | length | None |

Failed response: 980 content characters, no reasoning_content field. Usage does not expose a reasoning-token breakdown; none invented. Prompt evaluation changes 1→2 tokens between warm and failed Chat, prediction duration 37.43413 s. This is observable cache/timing divergence, not a proven cause. Cancellation/120-second deadline is not the exception; runtime cleanup ran in finally.

Historical fixture starts a Chat task before native generation, then awaits Chat after native completion. Actual server overlap/slot state at native start was not recorded; the name Chat is not proof of simultaneous inference or active SSE. Single-slot runtime can queue requests.

## 4. Canonical request equivalence

Historical cold/warm/failed Chat requests are structurally identical, canonical SHA256:

`e2e1d037480c7615daeb83eb01526d247a394cdc915649c10a084e25b27e5c28`.

Every current native wire request has the same canonical SHA. Same model, messages/context, tool description/schema, required string choice, exposed `nexus_apply_source_plan`, temperature, thinking flag and cap. No cache_prompt/id_slot/response_format or scheduling parameter injected into native request. Context 3288 bytes, successful args 168 bytes.

Adapter builds request-local payload/tools, DeepClone snapshots messages, and independently requires matching native tool identity. Decoder accepts valid native shape and rejects length/no-call. No valid historical native call was discarded. Request mutation/admission defect is not established.

## 5. Offline evidence and first observed divergence

Offline analysis completed before model execution. Immediate failure is proven: generation exhausted 256 tokens without native call, safely terminal.

The first difference available in retained response metadata is cache/timing (warm cached=1075/prompt_n=1 versus failed Chat 1074/2), followed by generated content/finish. Historical request ordering at server/slot is unavailable, so this is not necessarily the first internal divergence. No claim that a single-token difference causes failure.

Three recovery repetitions (nine valid native calls) remain historical support for successful generation, not proof of zero failure probability. Current native grammar permits a prose prefix; it explains how required can coexist with length truncation, not why that prefix was selected in the failed run.

## 6. Fixed experiment matrix and budget

Plan written before live start: **8 native calls + 4 auxiliary Chat calls = at most 12 total model generations**, conservatively counting Chat as well. No extra warmup: each cold native measurement also sets up its following warm repeat.

Fixed order: NoChat pair → Chat pair → Chat pair → NoChat pair (ABBA). Four fresh runtime groups, same source runtime configuration/context8192 and single slot. Native cap256 and per-request120-second CTS unchanged. Auxiliary Chat uses original `Reply READY only.`, stream=true, MaxTokens64 and temperature0.

Cold criterion: cached<=16 and cached/prompt<=5%. Warm criterion: cached/prompt>=95%. Otherwise UNVERIFIED. Intended Chat gate waits for observed streaming response headers, then records live client state and read-only /slots before native send; no response-reader delay. This is an explicitly different controlled scheduling diagnostic, not an identical replay of original fixture timing. No artificial easier scenario or native payload substitution claimed.

External dry binding run had zero model generations. Actual live run attempted the fixed matrix once; no retry/replay/top-up after harness errors.

All prompt counts 1076, completion79, finish=tool_calls, strict decoder/exact P1 arrays accepted, no cancellation, same native hash. Label for all eight: **VALID_NATIVE_TOOL**.

Native reflection path worked, but framework/custom JSON type binding prevented Chat serialization. This is not an observed defect in normal Nexus/VSTest runtime. Raw generation-results.json's chatCount=4/total=12 counts attempted auxiliary invocations, not sent model generations; decision-analysis.json explicitly corrects this using zero Chat HTTP-start events. No silent budget extension.

Each native result includes scenario/start/end, PID/leasePID/state, strict result, cancellation/lease state, slot snapshot, raw request/response and timings. Source writes/tool dispatches/approval requests=0 throughout.

## 8. Chat contention comparison

**No valid concurrent-Chat cell was obtained.** Four attempts failed before HTTP. Trace: ChatHeaders=false, ChatDone=false, Chat task already completed/faulted at native-send start. Read-only /slots reports slot0 idle before native request. Therefore these four native passes are observations under effective no-Chat conditions; they are not concurrent success evidence.

Controlled client overlap/server inference overlap and the cache×Chat interaction remain UNVERIFIED. No inference about Chat contention helping/hurting success is justified from this matrix. Harness problem is reported rather than silently repaired and rerun beyond the fixed schedule.

## 9. Cache comparison

Valid cold/warm no-Chat comparisons show cold cached0/prompt1076 and warm cached1075/prompt1076, all native calls79 tokens and accepted. Intended-Chat rows have observed cold/warm cache but cannot test combined active-Chat factors. The intended full factorial matrix is incomplete despite eight successful native outputs. Small sample cannot establish zero failure probability.

## 10. Model/runtime findings

Runtime PIDs by group: N1 22248, C1 3084, C2 24428, N2 24100. Native lease PID matched server PID and stateReady for all calls; lease/current request cancellation=false. Responses have fingerprint b11057-59657a613. /props captures actual runtime metadata; /slots returns id0 and idle state before send, plus prior taskID/prompt tokens where available.

Verified Job Object encloses build/dry/live roots and descendants; final job-only cleanup left membership empty. No historical PID ancestry cleanup or desktop application targeting.

Upstream required grammar is enabled, not ignored or automatically converted to auto. Required min1 applies to eventual tool-call branch while unbounded thought/content prefix is allowed. This structural observation and historical OutputLimit support a finite-budget mechanism; they do not exclusively distinguish model versus cache/scheduling trigger.

## 11. Confirmed/unproven decision table

| Question / hypothesis | Verified fact / supporting evidence | Contradicting or missing evidence | Confidence / required action |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 Nexus isolation defect | Same native requests; local payload/DeepClone; unchanged lease/runtime identity; prior isolation regressions pass | No valid current Chat overlap; hidden runtime interaction not exhaustively excluded | No causal host defect verified; no production fix authorization requested |
| H2 Cache/KV sensitivity | Warm cache1075 controls pass; failed historical cache1074 | Different historical scheduling unknown; no matched 1074 reproduction | Inconclusive; do not label corruption |
| H3 Concurrent scheduling effect | Fixture attempts coexisting Chat; np1 slot source queues busy requests | Historical overlap unknown; current Chat fails pre-HTTP | UNVERIFIED; repair diagnostic compatibility before any separately reviewed matrix |
| H4 Finite model generation | Historical length256/no call; Gemma4 permits content prefix before required eventual call | Eight measured no-Chat calls valid; causal trigger unobserved | Mechanism established, exclusive reliability attribution not established; OutputLimit stays terminal |
| H5 Fixture instability | Source does not gate/trace actual Chat overlap; synthetic fixed feedback scope documented | No demonstrated fixture mutation/admission bug; mandatory generation assertions remain valid | Clarify request-overlap versus simultaneous inference contract, no unilateral test change |
| Harness artifact | MissingMethodException before Chat HTTP; false header/task state, idle slots | Normal Nexus tests had valid Chat runs; native path still functions | High confidence external compatibility failure, not P1 root cause |
| Safety contract | Existing decoder rejects invalid/no-call; 0 dispatch/approval/write; prior60/71 regressions | No native OutputLimit reproduced in current controls | Preserve fail-closed; never promote invalid generation to success |
| Success contract | Historical failure; cold/warm/current controls can succeed | Intended concurrent cells unverified; small sample | Mandatory success remains open, not certified |

## 12. Safety versus success contract

**Safety:** no non-tool/truncated response authorizes tool dispatch, policy approval or source mutation. Historical OutputLimit rejects before an executable plan. Prior retained OutputLimit safety tests and current read-only observations support this, without adding authority.

**Success:** required P1 native proposals must have valid exact arrays within256/120s in required modes. It is a separate requirement; safety terminal failure is not successful generation.

Authoritative current classification: P1 is read-only supporting protocol evidence in architectural scope, but its enabled GateGLiveFact and strict cold/warm/Chat native-generation assertions were included in explicitly approved full certification with zero-failure/missing-mandatory acceptance. Thus it remains **mandatory functional-success acceptance for that certification** and supports safety evidence. It cannot be waived/reclassified based on the read-only label. Historical P1 report predates registered multi-hunk production implementation; current source/approval takes precedence over its earlier schema-registration description.

No test classification or expectation changed in this audit.

## 13. Minimum recommended change

No Nexus request/session/parser production defect verified, so **no production change recommended or implemented now**.

Minimum diagnostic proposal: bind the temporary harness HTTP-JSON/JSON dependencies consistently to the same verified testhost dependency closure (or use an existing compatible testhost observation path), verify Chat wire request and streaming observation without model generation, then obtain separate review for another fixed matrix. Do not create another provider/parser/framework. This external setup repair is not a P1 causal fix and was not used to add calls to this audit.

For the historical generation itself, no cap/retry/deadline/runtime/model change is justified by the current comparative evidence.

## 14. Acceptance-contract implications

Keep current mandatory native-success requirement and Gate H HOLD until the missing comparison/cause is reviewed. Do not accept OutputLimit as native success.

Separate explicit proposal, only if future evidence establishes unavoidable model success variability: consider classifying concurrent P1 as supporting reliability diagnostics while maintaining mandatory invalid-output safety and genuine approved repair success. Consequence: reduced claimed concurrent proposal-generation capability; intermittent generation failure explicitly supported as terminal, not functional success. This would require architectural acceptance review, separately authorized test-contract edits and a new source freeze/full-run approval. It is not adopted or recommended as a shortcut from this inconclusive matrix.

No full-suite invocation or Gate H PASS checkpoint; old approval unused.

## 16. Next action

Review the harness compatibility proposal and clarify the exact required Chat-overlap observation contract before another separately budgeted diagnostic. Retain native256/120s, required/single-tool and strict decoder; retain P6/default-deny authority and known limits.

Because concurrent conditions were not validly tested, this audit cannot distinguish H1/H2/H3/H4 sufficiently for an exclusive causal or model-reliability verdict. No implementation or full certification follows.

## 4. Verified cause and unresolved hypotheses

**Verified immediate failure:** the model exhausted the existing 256-token generation contract and returned no native call. NativeToolProtocol correctly throws OutputLimit. No valid native tool call is present for the decoder to discard.

**Trigger remains unresolved:** no Nexus request mutation, wrong tool visibility, changed generation options, parser loss or session transition defect is proved. Chat scheduling, cache state and model/template behavior remain hypotheses. Current cache difference alone is insufficient causal evidence.

NativeToolModelAdapter builds tools/payload locally for every request, DeepClone snapshots messages, uses the host-selected single descriptor, required string choice, fixed options and independent validation. Pinned b11057 common/chat.h declares AUTO/REQUIRED/NONE tool-choice values and Gemma4 parsing support; real cold/warm/native samples validate the intended request path. A required request is not host approval and cannot turn an absent call into authority.

Three later PASS repetitions do not prove a historical reliability repair. No further P1 generation repetition or speculative production fix is made. Workstream A stops for scope/reliability review under the existing contract.

## 9. Proven wrapper ownership defect

Old admission condition was `child_pid not in owned and parent_pid in owned`. Past parent PIDs never expired or validated as current parent process identities. Taskmgr.exe (PID 19536) was born before the run; its original parent PID later matched a run-associated reused PID. The wrapper stored Task Manager's own creation time after incorrect admission.

Cleanup's subsequent `creation(handle)==owned[pid]` only confirmed the incorrectly admitted child identity; it did not establish run ownership. Hence it still authorized foreign termination. Root cause is **untrusted historical ancestry admission**, not merely missing a final PID/creation check.

Before source and raw cleanup/first-failure evidence retained. No actual Task Manager/Corel/browser/coding-agent process was targeted during recovery testing.

## 10. Wrapper ownership fix

Strict contract:

1. Create a private unnamed, non-inherited job; wrapper holds the only management handle.
2. Create the root via native CreateProcess **suspended**, retain process/thread handles and creation identity.
3. Assign that directly created root to the job and verify membership **before ResumeThread**. Assignment/identity failure aborts launch; only the exact newly created suspended-root handle is cleaned, never an existing enumerated PID.
4. Descendants created normally by CreateProcess inherit kernel job membership, including after parent exit. No breakaway permission or resource/priority/deadline restriction added.
5. Per-process observation requires current handle membership and available creation identity. Foreign/missing/ambiguous identity is unproven, not cleanup eligible.
6. Forced cleanup uses TerminateJobObject on this directly owned invocation job, not process names or historical PID sets. Unknown external processes are untouched. Aggregate job ownership is kernel-established even if enumeration fails; no PID list becomes termination authority.
7. Closing the owned job cancels only associated processes; cleanup is idempotent and serialized. No arbitrary existing-process assignment API exposed.

Windows 8+ nested jobs are supported; real nested-job test passes on this Windows environment. The root job has no UI constraints and is empty before assignment. If assignment is incompatible, fail launch rather than fall back to unsafe ancestry. Child creation through WMI Win32_Process.Create does not inherit membership; such processes remain unproven/untouched and require review. Nexus's normal Process.Start/CreateProcess paths were exercised under the job in impacted tests.

## 11. Foreign-process safety verification

Final **22/22 safety tests PASS**, zero skips. Coverage:

- Direct root handle/identity and single launch.
- Verified child inheritance and cleanup.
- Pre-existing foreign dummy preserved.
- Foreign dummy born after root also preserved.
- Synthetic PID/creation reuse and historical Task Manager identity rejected.
- Fake enumeration PID substitution does not alter kernel membership/cleanup target.
- Parent exits before child; child remains kernel-owned and cleaned.
- Missing/inaccessible creation or membership metadata fails closed.
- Enumeration failure never creates PID-based cleanup authority.
- Process exits before cleanup.
- Cancellation during cleanup and repeated terminate/close.
- Nested job, pinned dotnet host and assignment-failure-before-resume.
- Wrapper regression check forbids old terminate_owned/ancestry/TerminateProcess selection.

Harmless dummies are test-created; foreign dummies are torn down only through the test's own retained Popen handle. No real desktop apps. Initial helper setup failed because a Python subprocess constant was not exported; no root process ran in those failed cases. That run's log is preserved. Corrected helper uses documented native flag 0x400; subsequent 19/19 and final 22/22 logs retained.

## 12. Impacted regression

**71/71 PASS, zero failure/skip/abort**, under the new actual native job launcher: GateBToolCapabilityTests, NativeToolSelectionTests, ToolProtocolTests, SharedRuntimeAgentTests, ConversationRuntimeWorkflowTests, HostAuthoritativeRepairTests, MultiHunkSourceToolTests and MultiHunkSourceRepairTests.

This includes existing real b11057 Gate B server startup/native round-trip/cleanup plus deterministic Chat/Agent/repair validation. It does not add a fourth P1 repetition. One residual process born in the invocation remained in the kernel job after the test root exited; job-only cleanup removed it, final job member list empty. No foreign name/PID lookup used for termination.

No full solution run; original consumed approval marker retained. Final process inspection shows no llama-server/testhost/Nexus.App. `git diff --check` passes with only pre-existing LF/CRLF normalization warnings.

## A/B/C yang diimplementasikan

- **A:** project selection tetap display-only. Dialog native **Izinkan baca proyek**
  menunjukkan root, sesi,10 menit, types/limits/exclusions; default No. Opaque grant
  in-memory binds root identity dan host session. Cabut/expiry/navigation/project/
  session switch membatalkan akses; expiry melepas directory pins. Read authority
  tidak masuk I1 edit profile/provider atau model-defined allowlist.
- **Discovery:** local fixed NTFS root, pinned directory/ancestor handles; bounded
  direct-child traversal (depth6,128 directories,4096 entries),256 source/32 metadata,
  explicit exclusions. `.csproj/.slnx` XML DTD prohibited/resolver null; `.sln` entry
  text dan literal references statis. Tidak ada imports/targets/conditions/globs/task
  evaluation, builds, restore, shell/Git, downloads atau external project reads.
- **B:** strict UTF-8/BOM dan UTF-16 BOM; unsupported encoding/binary/control chars,
  recognizable secret material, oversized/unauthorized/stale files ditolak. Existing
  final-path/reparse/single-link validation direuse; read handles deny writer/delete
  sharing. Identity/size/mtime + raw-byte SHA; max256 KiB/file,8 MiB cumulative grant,
 32 candidates/2 MiB per question. No persistent repository source index/cache.
- **Context:** explicit catalog IDs + bounded lexical selection;≤6 files/160 lines
  per fragment,≤24 KiB UTF-8 dan3072 conservative estimated tokens termasuk envelope,
  turun menurut existing headroom. Exact path/line/hash/encoding/time/truncation
  provenance. Snapshot dispatch valid≤60 s dan re-hashes metadata/source under leases.
  Repository text adalah passive untrusted data, bukan system instruction/grant.
- **C:** existing Chat receives actual evidence, tanpa old project history/manual
  attachments pada project-aware request. Source payload tidak dipersist sebagai
  ordinary filesystem attachment. Read/project CTS terpisah dari Agent CTS; captured
  revision/session fences mencegah late answer/context/persistence pada scope baru.
  Source reload/history resend meminta pertanyaan baru secara eksplisit agar source
  tidak dire-extract otomatis dari old paths. Citations hanya dinilai lokasi/range
  dalam supplied fragments; bukan semantic correctness guarantee.

## Blocker and limitations

`A_SymbolicFileEscape_IsRejectedByTheSameReparseBoundary` failed at
`File.CreateSymbolicLink`, **before reader dispatch**, with:
`System.IO.IOException: A required privilege is not held by the client`.
Direct symlink escape remains unproven here; junction reparse rejection is proven.
No assertion/test disabled, skipped or rewritten to obtain PASS. Enabling privilege,
elevation or global Developer Mode is outside current approved environment changes.
**Full I2-A acceptance cannot be called PASS until this retained failure is closed
using explicitly authorized test-fixture creation or an approved prepared fixture.**

Static candidate inventory is not exact compiled graph; no semantic symbol/call-graph
parser. Relevance is bounded lexical/manual selection, not whole-repository knowledge.
Secret detection is conservative/recognizable-pattern based, not an all-secret
guarantee. Large/dynamic projects may be refused/partially unsupported. Source reload
requires a fresh explicit question. Only one simple live source question is proved;
complex-question model quality is not certified. Physical IME, OS DPI, screen-reader,
full CF1 keyboard/focus acceptance remain HOLD/backlog.

files. Staged SHA remains

## 2. Satu desain: host-owned ProjectReadContextWorkflow

Grant baru **terpisah dari I1**. Runtime tidak menerima filesystem tool baru.

1. Folder dipilih native UI → hanya display path; belum enumerate/read konten.
2. Tombol **“Izinkan baca proyek”** menunjukkan canonical root, types/limits,
   exclusions dan sesi; explicit UI confirmation membuat opaque in-memory grant.
3. Host binds grant ke canonical root + NTFS volume/directory identity + session
   + context revision + expiry + fixed policy. Grant object tidak dibuat dari model
   text/path/Chat prompt, tidak dipersist, tidak masuk tool catalog atau model payload.
4. Satu active root/session. **“Cabut izin baca”**, project/session/navigation change
   atau expiry membatalkan read/preparation/related generation, membuang snapshots
   dan melepas leases. Tidak ada auto-grant untuk sesi/proyek berikutnya.
5. Current I1 edit readiness tetap berasal dari existing approved mutation profile.
   Arbitrary real root boleh memperoleh read grant, **tidak memperoleh edit grant**.

### Fixed host limits — bukan konfigurasi model

| Boundary | Initial limit |
|---|---|
| Root | Satu explicit local fixed NTFS folder; bukan drive root, UNC/network, device path atau reparse root/ancestor |
| Grant lifetime | Maksimum10 menit; snapshot maksimal60 detik, tidak diperpanjang diam-diam |
| Traversal | Maksimum6 directory levels,128 directories,4096 examined entries; cancellation checks; bukan unrestricted recursive scan |
| Catalog | Maksimum256 candidate `.cs`,32 metadata files total (`.sln`, `.slnx`, `.csproj`) |
| File read | Maksimum256 KiB per source/metadata file;8 MiB cumulative read bytes per grant, counted termasuk search/revalidation |
| Per question | Maksimum32 source candidates/2 MiB inspected; maksimum6 files dalam context, masing-masing≤160 selected lines |
| Model context | Maksimum24 KiB UTF-8 **dan**3072 estimated tokens termasuk provenance/envelope; turun sesuai actual existing attachment budget |
| Time | Discovery/preparation≤10 detik per operation dengan CTS; tidak mengubah Agent120 s/SDK15 s atau model caps |

Hard limit tidak diatasi dengan silent wider scan; kegagalan/partial unsupported
state dicatat. User memilih file tambahan dari catalog yang sama jika informasi
kurang. Scope/limit expansion di luar kontrak ini memerlukan approval baru.

Default exclusions, case-insensitive di setiap segment: `.git`, `bin`, `obj`, `.vs`,
`.idea`, `.vscode`, `node_modules`, `packages`, `TestResults`, caches, `.ssh`, `.aws`,
`.azure`, `secrets`, `credentials`; generated `.g.cs`/`.g.i.cs`/`.generated.cs`/
`.designer.cs`, `AssemblyInfo.cs`, `Secrets.cs`, `Credentials.cs`. Hanya empat
extensions di atas; README, JSON/config, `.env*`, `.user`, keys/certificates, binaries,
archives/logs tidak dimuat otomatis. Recognizable private-key material/hard-coded
credential values menyebabkan file ditahan sebelum payload, dengan alasan tanpa
menampilkan nilainya. Filename filters tidak diklaim mendeteksi semua secrets
yang mungkin ditanam di source; batas ini wajib dilaporkan.

### Safe reads dan identity

- Reuse existing file leases tanpa melemahkan validator. Tambah **read-only directory
  lease helper** dalam EditingLite assembly untuk memakai existing native handle
  validation, pin ancestors/current directory sebelum enumerating direct children.
  Reparse entries tidak di-follow; denial dicatat. Hardlinks dengan link count≠1
  ditolak. Path/model requests tidak menerima rooted path, `..`, ADS atau escapes.
- Metadata relative references dinormalisasi sebagai data, containment dicek dahulu;
  external references dicatat tanpa membuka target. Leases/final-path checks tetap
  wajib untuk setiap actual read; lexical containment saja tidak cukup.
- Record volume/file identity, length/mtime dan raw-byte SHA-256. Bounded handle
  reads dengan writer/delete sharing denied, pre/post identity checks dan safe
  root/catalog revision. Unreadable/access-denied/binary/oversized/invalid encoding
  adalah failure jelas; tidak berubah menjadi empty successful content.
- Strict UTF-8 (BOM/no BOM), UTF-16 LE/BE dengan BOM; unsupported/malformed encoding
  ditolak, bukan guessed Latin-1. Existing general attachment decoder tidak diubah.
- Sebelum dispatch, re-open/re-hash selected snapshots dan metadata lalu check
  grant/session/revision/expiry. Stale data ditolak; tidak auto-refresh lalu kirim
  diam-diam. Provenance selalu menyebut snapshot time, bukan klaim live filesystem.

## 3. Discovery/context: metadata data-only, tanpa workspace engine

`.sln` dibaca sebagai bounded solution-entry text; `.slnx`/`.csproj` memakai BCL
`XmlReader` dengan `DtdProcessing.Prohibit`, `XmlResolver=null`, document-size limits.
Literal in-root project/source references + supported SDK-style candidate `.cs`
inventory dikenali secara statis. Imports, targets, conditions/properties/globs,
analyzers, source generators dan compiled project graph **tidak dievaluasi**.
Unsupported/dynamic cases ditampilkan sebagai batasan; candidate source list tidak
diklaim sebagai exact compiled file set. Tidak ada build/restore/script/process,
network discovery/download atau membaca imported external project.

Relevance awal: explicit selected file IDs dari catalog + bounded lexical ranking
filename/path/question tokens; jika perlu, bounded source-text search tanpa index,
embeddings atau extra model selection calls. Cari “network/HTTP” dapat mengarahkan
ke actual client/transport code; login yang belum ditemukan berarti belum terbukti,
bukan dibuat-buat. Tidak membuat C# parser baru: class/method relationships dijelaskan
model dari inspected lines; tidak mengklaim semantic symbol/call graph.

Source fragments membawa relative path, exact line range, encoding, hash, snapshot
time dan read/truncation state. Line mapping dipertahankan pada context yang benar-
benar dialokasikan; potongan/truncation dicatat. Host membungkus repository text
sebagai untrusted data dan instruksi request-scoped meminta citations `[path:Lx-Ly]`,
menyebut files yang dibaca serta mengakui informasi kurang. Model citations dicek
terhadap supplied ranges; invalid citations tidak ditandai verified. Ini bukan
jaminan bahwa semua penjelasan model benar, dan model reliability dilaporkan terpisah.

Project source context bersifat ephemeral, bukan ordinary persisted attachment
yang bisa di-read ulang lewat raw path. Reuse budget/envelope UI, bukan legacy
attachment filesystem entry point. Project-aware request memakai current validated
evidence, **tidak membawa old project source/answers/attachments dari Chat history**.
History tetap terlihat, ordinary Chat/AI Check behavior dipertahankan. Reload/edit
resend wajib menyiapkan evidence baru, atau menolak jika grant sudah tidak berlaku.
Grant revoke/project/session switching menutup dispatch dan late persistence/UI updates
dengan captured session + generation revision; Agent CTS tidak ikut dicancel oleh
Chat/read CTS, existing Agent drain hanya mengikuti project/session navigation.

## 5. OSS choices, risks dan bounded acceptance

| Candidate | Decision / supply-chain / execution |
|---|---|
| Nexus + .NET8 BCL XML/encoding/crypto + Win32 handle checks | **USE/ENHANCE**; sudah tersedia, tidak menambah package. XML data-only dan read handles; no MSBuild execution. |
| Pinned OpenCode/Cline context/navigation UX audit | **ADAPT concepts** saja: explicit file context/provenance. Tidak mengambil executor/permissions atau mengulang research. |
| Microsoft Roslyn syntax APIs | **NOT NEEDED initial slice**; MIT, mature .NET ecosystem, standalone syntax parse tidak perlu workspace build. References belum ada; dependency/semantic analysis tidak diusulkan sekarang. |
| MSBuildWorkspace/Microsoft.Build evaluation | **REJECT initial loading**: repository imports/targets/tasks, environment/SDK dependencies, unsafe evaluation surface dan unnecessary supply-chain work. |
| Existing file organizer recursive scan / general attachment `.env` support | **REJECT as project authority**; tidak mempunyai grant/pinning/exclusion boundary ini. |

Focused reference checks: [XmlReader DTD API (.NET8)](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/system.xml.xmlreadersettings.dtdprocessing?view=net-8.0),
[Roslyn MIT license](https://github.com/dotnet/roslyn/blob/main/License.txt).
Tidak ada OSS install/run/dependency addition pada Stage1.

Risks: filesystem races/replacement (pinned identity + rehash); secrets/source prompt
injection (restrict types/exclusions, sensitive-material rejection, untrusted envelope,
no tools); limit exhaustion (hard bounds/clear partial failures); stale/scope leak
(ephemeral grant/context and dispatch/persistence fences); incomplete graph/symbol
understanding (explicit static/citation limits); model reliability (separate actual
model evidence, no fabricated comprehension).

- **Slice A RED→GREEN:** grant/revoke/session TTL, empty/single SDK project, static
  `.sln/.slnx/.csproj`, literal/external refs, limits/exclusions, malicious DTD/Import
  no execution; root/ancestor/child junction/symlink swaps, ADS/traversal/hardlinks,
  inaccessible directory/file, cancellation/handle cleanup.
- **Slice B RED→GREEN:** strict encoding/Unicode/newline/line provenance, oversized/
  binary, read-time replacement, stale hash/identity/metadata, revoked permission,
  secret filenames/material, misleading repository instructions; final payload
  matches only actual read+allocated fragments, insufficient context explicit.
- **Slice C RED→GREEN:** existing Chat payload receives evidence; no history/attachment
  cross-project leak including reload, new/temporary session and late completion;
  grant/read CTS vs Agent CTS; unsupported edit remains disabled; ordinary Chat/
  AI Check/single-runtime/CF2 review regression when integration stable.
- Actual WPF pilot picker→explicit grant→genuine source list→real class question→
  actual local Chat response with validated references. Deterministic payload tests
  dan two planned live read-only questions direkonsiliasi terpisah; no mock response
  disebut model execution. Show denial/revoke/stale proof and zero source writes,
  project process launches/network discovery/restore/build.

## Berkas: GATE_I2B_NATURAL_LANGUAGE_EDIT_SCOPE.md
*Lokasi Asli:* `D:\NEXUS\Nexus-CSharp\GATE_I2B_NATURAL_LANGUAGE_EDIT_SCOPE.md`

# Gate I2-B — Natural-Language Source Edit / Stage 1

14 Oktober 2026 · **REVIEW COMPLETE; implementation dan Gate J HOLD.**
Rekomendasi tunggal: **`I2B-NL-P1` — proposal model dibatasi host, satu existing
Agent/source-plan pipeline, satu real-project profile yang direview eksplisit.**

## 1. Actual findings / reuse

- I2-A `ProjectReadContextWorkflow` menyediakan explicit session/root grant, catalog,
  identity/hash/revalidation. **USE**, tetapi read grant bukan write/build authority.
  `PrepareAsync` menghasilkan potongan dan menormalisasi newline; **jangan memakai
  fragment Chat sebagai original exact-edit text**. Perlu full byte-faithful snapshot
  dua target melalui reader yang sama, dalam read grant/byte limits existing.
- I1 `SharedRuntimeAgent` menolak SourcePlan tanpa host `SourceIntent`; system prompt
  meminta model menyalin exact supplied literals. `ApplySourcePlanTool` melakukan
  `SameIntent` terhadap literal host. Missing: validasi untrusted NL proposal menjadi
  host-bound intent; bukan menonaktifkan checks tersebut.
- **USE unchanged execution:** `NativeToolModelAdapter`/strict protocol,
  `SourcePlanArgumentValidator`, `ToolExecutor`, `ApplySourcePlanTool`,
  `ExactSourceEdits`, `EditingWorkflowService` prepare/apply/recovery,
  `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider`, CF2 plan-backed diff/card/hide/reopen.
- `SourceVerificationProfile` mem-pin fixture paths/graph; `SourceVerification`
  mem-pin Product/Tests artifacts dan tiga fixture test names. Missing: additive
  immutable real-project manifest/profile, tanpa melonggarkan fixture constructor.
- **Actual execution risk:** repository ancestor `Directory.Build.targets` mempunyai
  `AutoSignNexusOutputs`/`Exec powershell`. Tidak aman menjalankan build proyek yang
  dipilih begitu saja. Verification profile adalah execution authority, bukan sandbox.

## 2. Flow dan authority — model hanya proposer

```text
UI read consent + separately enabled reviewed edit/verification profile
 → fresh exact snapshots + host SourceProposalScope
 → existing SharedRuntimeAgent / native model (one initial proposal)
 → host validates structured proposal → immutable SourcePlanIntent
 → existing ToolExecutor → ApplySourcePlanTool → immutable SourceEditPlan
 → existing diff + independent exact-plan approval
 → existing SafeWrite → pinned build/tests → hash verification / recovery
```

`SourceProposalScope` host-only binds RunId/session/root identity, active read grant,
profile digest, target slots, full source hashes, allowed original-coordinate ranges,
expiry/cancellation and budgets. Tidak bisa dibuat/diubah dari Chat, source atau model.
User memilih **Edit kode**, mengetik NL, lalu Jalankan; tidak ada Old/New input untuk
mode ini. I1 literal mode tetap tersedia pada profil lamanya.

Model mengisi existing sole dialect `nexus_apply_source_plan`:
`a:[{old,new}], b:[{old,new}]`. Paths, hashes, commands, approvals, profile IDs dan
limits **tidak menjadi argumen model**. Slots dipetakan host ke dua target. Natural
proposal tidak dikirim ulang untuk disalin pada model turn kedua: existing
orchestrator memvalidasi respons sebelum dispatch, mencatat accepted host intent
sebagai run-local fact, lalu memakai executor/tool yang sama. Tidak ada executor,
filesystem writer atau approval provider kedua. Global catalog tetap disabled.

Host wajib memeriksa strict schema/duplicate keys/tool IDs/counts, active scopes,
root/file identity, full raw-byte hash, encoding, unique ordinal match, presented
edit ranges, overlap/surrogate/newline fidelity, no-op dan diff/size limits.
`ExactSourceEdits.Resolve` tetap final deterministic resolution; tanpa fuzzy matching,
Markdown patch execution atau “best effort” fallback. Recheck setelah generation,
sebelum approval/first commit dan verification; stale/unknown/out-of-range ditolak.

**Perluasan nyata:** SourceIntent boleh dibentuk host dari *validated model proposal*,
bukan hanya literal yang diketik user. Structural validation tidak membuktikan model
memahami maksud NL. Semantic consent tetap dari user yang melihat exact immutable
diff. Read permission, profile admission dan source-plan approval adalah tiga hal
berbeda; tidak saling menggantikan.

Proposal/approval/build/test memakai **satu120 s deadline**, native cap256 dan existing
Agent input-context cap; I2-A24 KiB tidak boleh otomatis masuk Agent melebihi capnya.
Target source≤4 KiB UTF-8/file,≤256 chars/literal,≤1024 literal bytes,≤2 edits/file,
≤3 edits total,≤2 KiB diff/≤6 changed lines tetap. Pilot tepat dua changed files;
single-file/large-file/UTF-16 writes belum termasuk. MaxSourcePlans1, repair0/retry0.
Malformed/OutputLimit berhenti tanpa partial execution atau automatic top-up.
Revoke/read-expiry/session/project/model switch membatalkan run, deny pending approval
dan drain recovery sebelum scope rebinding. Recovery hanya untuk approved owned
operations; external edits tetap dipertahankan. Hide bukan Cancel dan tidak refill waktu.

## 3. Pilot minimum dan verification profile

**Real project:** complete reviewed working copy dari
`src/00_Contracts/Nexus.Contracts` (17 existing source files, standalone net8.0,
tanpa ProjectReference/PackageReference). Proposed authorized root:

dan exact TRX inventory tetap wajib; no success berdasarkan prose/exit0 saja.
Graph/source baru adalah executable code setelah exact approval, **bukan sandbox**;
user harus mengetahui verification menjalankan approved compiled code dengan haknya.
Setelah pilot, stale read snapshots direvoke; tidak ada silent manifest rebaseline
atau promotion ke proyek/root lain.

## 5. OSS decision, risks, bounded implementation / acceptance

**USE/ENHANCE** Nexus exact original-coordinate resolver/prepare-first plan, host
scope catalog dan reviewed verification. **ADAPT** pinned OpenCode MIT/Cline Apache2
conversation/proposal/review identity patterns dari approved OSS review; .NET/WPF
presentation tetap native. **REJECT** broad executors/allow-always/shadow-Git,
fuzzy search-replace fallback dan postwrite “Keep” sebagai prewrite approval.
**NOT NEEDED** UI framework, new patch engine, Roslyn/MSBuildWorkspace/dependency.
No OSS install/execution; attempted pinned OpenCode edit-file URL returned404 and
tidak dijadikan klaim bahwa editing engine-nya telah diverifikasi.

Risks: incorrect NL intent (exact review + behavior tests), hostile source/proposal
(untrusted data/strict slots/ranges/no commands), build supply-chain/hooks (pinned
offline harness/profile), stale/scope leak (same-run identity fences/linked cancel),
malicious approved code (not sandbox), model capacity (caps tetap, failure visible).

1. **RED→GREEN proposal admission:** deterministic valid NL-shaped proposal from
   exact snapshots; CRLF/BOM/Unicode, partial context, unknown slot/path, duplicate/
   overlapping edits, changed read grant/profile/root/hash and OutputLimit ⇒ deny
   before write. I1 literal-intent negatives tetap strict; no model self-approval.
2. **RED→GREEN reviewed profile:** malicious ancestor/import/Exec/linked path/asset
   drift denied; only reviewed commands/three TRX facts/fresh artifacts; build/test
   failure/cancel/conflict recovers owned bytes without overwriting external edits.
3. **RED→GREEN Chat integration:** type NL without Old/New, show actual CF2 diff;
   exact approval only, double/stale/Enter/hide/expiry/revoke/session isolation;
   Chat/AI Check/runtime/I1 regressions hanya terdampak setelah integration stabil.
4. Actual WPF: explicit read/profile consent on working copy; zero mutation/process
   starts sebelum exact approval; controlled failing proposal→recovery and successful
   two-file task→build/3 facts/hash PASS. All other sources/graph/original tree unchanged.
   Plan at most one read-only proposal prerequisite plus two Agent runs (failure/
   success), at most4 native generations including final facts; no retry-until-pass,
   no caps enlargement. Reconcile real vs deterministic/replayed evidence separately.

## 6. One approval request / Stage 1 result

**Mohon setujui `I2B-NL-P1` hanya untuk dua perluasan authority:**
**(a)** host may accept a structurally validated model-derived proposal inside the
existing source-plan pipeline, still requiring independent exact-plan approval;
**(b)** one pinned real working-copy write/verification profile and its reviewed
offline execution graph. Scope includes exact files/pilot/preparation/proof budget
di atas; engineering approval tidak menggantikan consent user di actual Nexus UI.
Arbitrary roots/builds, single-file/general edits, repairs dan privilege expansion
tidak diminta. Tidak membuat executor atau mutation authority paralel.

Stage1 hanya dokumen:678 source/report files matched closure baseline, staged SHA
`b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046` tetap;
**0 production edits/tests/model generations**, no pilot copy/build/restore dibuat.
Evidence/hash receipt: `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate-i2b-scope\scope-seal.json`.
**I2-B implementation dan Gate J HOLD sampai keputusan owner.**

## Slice 2 — review modern

- Card di area Chat menampilkan **“2 file akan diubah”**, setiap actual plan target

  per-file, Buka/Ciutkan semua, hide/reopen dan resize mempertahankan file/state.
- Adapter kecil membaca **existing immutable entry.Diff**, tanpa code/diff engine,
  filesystem read, regeneration, atau perubahan digest. Dua metadata lines I1
  dipisahkan dari code preview; raw diff tidak diubah dan tetap selectable.
- Read-only monospace FlowDocument: deletion merah lembut, addition hijau lembut,
  neutral context dan `−/+` markers. Brushes berasal dari semantic resources;
  High Contrast mengikuti system semantic colors serta markers, bukan warna saja.
- Raw whitespace/indentasi/Unicode/newline dipertahankan adapter; preview mengikuti
  native WPF line layout/wrapping dan selection. Raw text asli tersedia untuk
  menyalin exact newline representation. Current I1 tidak punya hunk line numbers;
  **nomor baris tidak direkayasa**.
- “Berhasil diubah dan diverifikasi” pada file summary memerlukan receipt cocok

- Approval tetap exact provider. Double action/stale UI tidak mengulang grant;
  reject/expiry tidak menulis. UI-only fixture tidak menjalankan approved edits.

OpenCode/Cline audit dan pola PR. Tidak ada framework/dependency atau global design
change.

# Gate I — Chat-First CF1 implementation

Tanggal: 14 Oktober 2026. Gate J: **HOLD**.

## Keputusan

**S1/S2/S3 diimplementasikan; affected regression GREEN (219/219), targeted
post-regression mode-independence GREEN. Actual WPF scaled matrix tervalidasi.
Full CF1 UI acceptance masih HOLD untuk native OS-DPI, IME fisik, dan pembuktian
keyboard/screen-reader lengkap.** Ini bukan full solution certification dan bukan
fresh live source-edit/model execution.

## Scope dan baseline

- Scope mengikuti CF1: sepuluh existing files yang disetujui di App/Integration.Tests,
  ditambah `GateIChatFirstWorkspaceTests.cs`. Laporan dan external tooling/evidence
  terpisah dari application ownership.
- Baseline 667 source/report identities di
  `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate-i-cf1\baseline.json`.
  Baseline memakai hash audit sebelum edit; satu known RED admission-test insertion
  sudah ada saat status-before diambil dan dicatat oleh baseline script.
- Staged identity wajib tetap
  `b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`.
  Current before/after reconciliation, HEAD, command/TRX, binary hashes dan archive
  identities tersimpan dalam `cf1-seal.json` di evidence directory di atas.
- Tidak ada reset, restore, clean, stash, commit atau source-baseline refresh.
  Historical Gate H/I/recovery/Phase B/OSS-review evidence tetap dipertahankan.

## Implementasi

### S1 — composer, proyek, mode, draft

- Existing Chat timeline/renderer/composer digunakan kembali. Project selector
  ditempatkan langsung di atas input, dengan capability status yang jujur.
- `Tanya` menuju existing Chat route. `Ubah kode terbatas` menuju Agent hanya dengan
  current explicit request dan literal edits Alpha/Beta. Teks `setujui` di Chat
  tidak berubah menjadi source-edit approval.
- Literal old/new hanya terlihat pada mode edit dengan admitted workspace.
  Project selection tidak sama dengan authority membaca atau menulis file.
- Draft input, attachment context dan literal intent disimpan bounded in-memory
  untuk kombinasi project + conversation/session + mode, maksimum 20 draft scopes.
- Project/session switching cancel/drain Agent sebelum rebinding; activity/review
  dibersihkan saat ganti scope. Context-read completion memakai revision fence.
  Tanya dapat dipilih ketika Agent masih menunggu approval tanpa membatalkan plan.

### S2 — contextual review dan host-owned activity

- Opaque conversation overlay diganti contextual review column. Default closed
  menggunakan seluruh available conversation width. Available width <760 DIP
  memakai review surface dengan Chat/composer disembunyikan sementara; close
  mengembalikan existing surface dan draft, tanpa membuat composer kedua.
- Latest activity berada di host conversation area, tidak masuk `Messages` atau
  model-authored history. Status utama menggunakan bahasa Indonesia dan fakta
  typed verification/recovery; success memerlukan source/build/3 required tests.
- `Tutup tinjauan` hanya mengubah presentation. `Batalkan perubahan` terpisah.
  Hide/reopen mempertahankan pending instance, RunId/PlanId/digest/deadline dan
  tidak memulai run baru. Existing exact provider tetap one-shot/default-deny.
- Full selectable diff dan pinned approve/reject footer dipertahankan. Hash/IDs
  dipindahkan ke expandable Detail teknis plan; hasil teknis penuh tetap tersedia.
- Welcome glow yang menutupi compact review ditemukan melalui screenshot inspection
  dan disembunyikan ketika review aktif.

### S3 — konteks file yang benar-benar dikirim

- `Jelaskan kode` menggunakan actual authorized Alpha/Beta snapshot. Production
  workflow revalidates pinned manifest/root/graph/source/SDK lalu membandingkan
  current selection digest dan snapshot hash. Target lain/traversal/drift ditolak.
- Internal pinned helper dipakai hanya oleh fixture tests; public production route
  tetap menggunakan reviewed root dan manifest hash constants.
- Snapshot content masuk existing explicit attachment/context-budget pipeline dan
  Chat payload, bukan sekadar path label. Snapshot chip memiliki empty filesystem
  path sehingga tidak menjadi arbitrary reader/re-extraction authority.
- Interface default file-context implementation menolak. Tidak ada file indexer,
  shell/Git/MCP, implicit Agent attachment/history, atau tambahan runtime.

## Open-source dan keamanan

USE/ENHANCE existing Nexus renderer/composer, attachment budget, scoped editing,
exact approval, SafeWrite, verification/recovery. ADAPT konsep project/composer
proximity, scoped drafts, contextual panel dan request identity dari pinned OSS
audit `GATE_I_CHAT_FIRST_OSS_UX_ARCHITECTURE_REVIEW.md`. Tidak ada framework,
dependency/runtime transplant atau GPL source copying.

Agent hard deadline 120 s termasuk approval, native256/Chat64 certified limits,
SDK build/test15 s, independent CTS, one initial I1 plan/no automatic repair,
private LocalLow verification temp dan verified Job lifecycle tidak diubah.

# Gate I — chat-first OSS UX architecture review

13 Oktober 2026. **DESIGN READY FOR ONE OWNER SCOPE REVIEW.**
Rekomendasi: pertahankan percakapan/composer Nexus sebagai pengalaman utama;
letakkan **Pilih proyek tepat di atas composer**, ungkapkan literal intent I1 hanya
ketika aksi ubah kode dipilih, dan gunakan panel review kontekstual yang tertutup
secara default. Reuse pipeline/renderer existing; bukan Agent/editor/runtime baru.
Gate H certified dan Gate I1 functional PASS tetap baseline; **Phase B belum PASS**.

## 1. Recovery interrupted work dan screenshot-based defects

AGENTS/policy dan laporan I1 dibaca; current tree direkonsiliasi dengan recovery
checkpoint SHA `30481818f87008f6b6e3d1f76e9df59b1d2a2224b820d5ab8caa47525f014405`.
Hanya empat file Phase B yang berbeda dari666-file baseline; patch dipertahankan.
Staged SHA tetap `b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`;
certified Gate H ZIP tetap `2039be89ee34e82cfa38c20471ea68bf742fb09dd08ccdbb27ef79665737f822`.

| Interrupted Phase B | Actual evidence / disposition |
|---|---|
| View XAML/code-behind | Dua pane, splitter, compact tabs <760 DIP, single control instances, full selectable diff, empty states sudah ada. Footer approval kemudian dipindah keluar review scroll; tab underscore diperbaiki. Preserve, tidak restart. |
| Agent VM | Presentation state/DiffText/PlanDetails, allowlisted files, summaries, late-UI guard dan existing pending approval binding ada. Preserve execution contract. |
| UI tests | RED6 layout cases; GREEN13 awal, GREEN15 setelah pending/expiry tests dan immediate action-disable fix. Final affected suite **169 PASS/1 FAIL**. |
| Final failure | `GateIAgentWorkspaceTests.Admission_RequiresHostPinnedManifest_NotJustFolderSelection`: Alpha hash berubah setelah successful I1 task. Host **correctly denies** original baseline. Test depends on mutable real engineering workspace and needs isolation; bukan alasan reset files atau weaken admission. |
| Visual evidence | External visual host menjalankan actual Nexus App/MainWindow/production DI,9 width/theme combinations ×4 display states, fixture pending provider dan historical receipt replay, zero generation/mutation. Matrix mendahului final pinned-footer patch; final visuals belum lengkap. **Tidak klaim Phase B PASS.** |

Screenshot yang benar-benar diperiksa: `gate-i-phase-b/actual-visual-matrix-1/`
`empty-Light-1280.png`, `pending-ui-only-Light-1280.png`,
`pending-ui-only-Dark-1440.png`, `pending-ui-only-HighContrast-900.png`,
`terminal-receipt-replay-Light-1280.png`; actual functional screenshots juga dibaca
dari `gate-i-i1-recovery/actual-approved-source-success/`.

**UX defects:** empty review menghabiskan ±64% layar, mandatory four literal boxes
muncul sebelum user menyatakan ingin edit, pilihan proyek jauh dari Chat composer,
overlay menutup percakapan, jargon mixed-language/profile/receipts dominan.
**Missing integration:** project/draft binding lintas conversation, explicit routing
composer ke bounded edit, file context yang benar-benar disertakan, activity/result
di conversation, close/reopen panel tanpa implicit cancellation.
**Certified constraints:** reviewed graph/two files/exact literals/one approval,
120 s including review, source hash drift default-deny. Ini bukan UX defects yang
boleh dihilangkan lewat polesan UI.

## 2. Actual ownership / user-journey map

Abbreviation: `App/`=`src/03_App/Nexus.App/`,
`IT/`=`src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/`.

| Existing owner / code path | Finding / reuse |
|---|---|
| `App/ViewModels/MainViewModel.cs:ExecuteNavigationAsync/TransitionLifecycleAsync`; `MainWindow.xaml` | AIWorkspace routes to singleton Conversation VM and module lifecycle. Keep navigation/runtime owner unchanged. |
| `App/Views/ConversationView.xaml`: `AIChatItems`, `AIWorkspaceMain`, composer `TxtAIInput`, header Chat/AI Check/model | Familiar chat, streamed renderer and one existing composer already exist. Current AgentWorkspaceView is opaque `Panel.ZIndex=850`, Grid.Row1/ColumnSpan2 overlay; this explains the separate-form experience. |
| `ConversationView.xaml.cs:TxtAIInput_PreviewKeyDown`, drag/drop/focus/follow-tail | Enter→existing SendCommand, Shift+Enter newline; preserve these contracts and route explicit task kind, not natural-language heuristics. |
| `ConversationViewModel.cs:InputText/Attachments/SendCommand/StartGeneration/RunGenerationAsync` | Chat owns input/history/payload/rendering. Agent has a different Prompt and RunCommand. Selecting a project currently does not enrich Chat with file content. |
| `ConversationViewModel.ExecuteNewConversationAsync/OpenConversationAsync` | Chat history opens/rebuilds; no project-keyed drafts or Agent invalidation here. Navigation-away does cancel/drain Agent. Integration must cover session change as well as navigation without importing history into Agent. |
| `AgentWorkspaceViewModel.SelectWorkspaceAsync/RunAsync/Decide/CloseCommand` | Selection/snapshots, fresh Agent session, literal intents and exact prepared plan already available. Close currently cancel+drains; contextual **hide** needs a distinct presentation action. |
| `App/Services/AgentWorkspaceWorkflow.Select/RunAsync` | Pinned manifest+SDK+graph/source hashes, authorized snapshots and run-local existing Agent graph. Context sent to edit Agent is identities + exact supplied literals, not a general repository reader. |
| `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider.BindRun/RequestPlanAsync/Decide`; existing editing services | Actual plan/profile/run/digest one-shot approval and guarded SafeWrite/verification/recovery. Reuse intact. Never infer grants from card text, selected tab or a model's response. |
| `ConversationRuntimeWorkflow` / `SharedRuntimeAgent` | Same runtime; Agent/Chat CTS isolated; model/navigation drain lease. Keep existing120 s Agent/15 s SDK limits and owned Job cleanup. |

Renderer/design-system files, SDK verification fix, policy, SafeWrite and source-plan
contracts are preserved user/baseline work. Current audit changes only this document;
no tests/model/full certification rerun, no application edits.

## 3. Deep OSS UX comparison — actual source and interaction evidence

All source snapshots are pinned, hashed and stored externally. Published images/demos
were viewed and source/state/interaction tests read; these applications were **not
installed/executed** and their tests were not rerun. Images show their documented
interaction, not new Nexus execution proof. Links below point to audited commits.

### OpenCode — project/session/composer proximity, scoped drafts, contextual review

Snapshot [`4ffede4`](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/tree/4ffede49416c18302bbccf8517c68db427d71844), MIT, active dev branch/CI.
Actual desktop/web UI is **Solid**, with separate desktop host; not a native WPF control.

- `packages/app/src/new-session/view.tsx:NewSessionView` composes Composer and
  project/workspace selectors in the same centered region. At this snapshot project
  controls are **below** Composer; adapt proximity, while Nexus requirement places
  selector **above**. `new-session/project/selector.tsx` handles selection/dialog/focus.
- `composer/composer.tsx:Composer/ComposerModelControl` keeps editor/model choices

shadow-Git restore, post-edit acceptance as a substitute for Nexus prewrite approval.

## 4. Recommended native architecture — one composer, distinct authority channels

Keep `ConversationView` the AI Workspace shell. Its existing Chat/AI Check module
selection and model picker remain; add separate **composer task kind** (Tanya /
Ubah kode terbatas), not a third value encoded as `!IsChatModeActive`.

```text
ConversationView / ConversationViewModel
  existing Chat messages, renderer, history and streaming
  selected-project display + capability status + session/project draft key
  existing composer: explicit task routing
    Tanya → existing Chat SendCommand/payload/runtime
    Jelaskan kode → explicit authorized snapshot/attachment → Chat, no mutation
    Ubah kode → progressive literal intent → existing AgentWorkspace VM/workflow
  latest host-owned Agent activity/result card (not model text, not Chat history)
  contextual AgentWorkspace review view: files/diff/approval/technical receipt
```

No parallel composer or new timeline engine. First bounded slice shows one latest
run/activity card in the conversation area; keep existing Chat message collection/
renderer and persistence unchanged. Agent lifecycle events project onto that card.
General persistent multi-run task timelines are later scope, not assumed available.

Project selection has **two separate states**: chosen display location and host
admission result. Unreviewed folder gets a readable limited status without directory
scan/read/write. “Jelaskan kode” requires actual explicit attachment or revalidated
authorized Alpha/Beta snapshot; selected project/path alone is never file content.

## 5. User journey / responsive specification and examples

**Initial:** existing conversation welcome, no Alpha/Beta form and no empty half-width
panel. Project row directly above composer: `[Pilih proyek ▾]`, capability hint;
placeholder **“Tanyakan sesuatu...”**. Model picker stays familiar. Normal Chat and
AI Check continue their existing routes.

**Select:** show leaf project name, full path on accessible tooltip/technical details,
and honest status: “Chat tersedia” / “Perubahan kode terbatas tersedia” /
“Proyek berubah; perlu ditinjau ulang sebelum mengubah kode”. Ordinary questions
remain usable even when admission fails; do not show fake Agent activity.

**Explain example:** select authorized Alpha → “Jelaskan kode” → explicit context
chip shows file/hash/source provenance and validated current snapshot actually enters
the Chat payload. Missing/unreviewed context: “Pilih atau lampirkan file yang boleh
dibaca”. Never answer as if repository content was read when only path is known.

**Edit example:** user selects **Ubah kode** and types “Ubah nilai hasil menjadi2”.
Admission must succeed. Only then reveal compact “Teks yang dicari” / “Teks pengganti”
for actual Alpha/Beta targets. Explain “Versi I1 memerlukan perubahan teks eksplisit
pada dua file”. Do not infer executable edits from Chat output/free-form request.
User confirms literals and starts the existing bounded workflow; no plan/write yet
from merely choosing a mode or project.

**Pending:** card “2 file siap ditinjau — belum ada file diubah” + **Tinjau perubahan**.
Open contextual panel with all affected files, exact full selectable diffs, concise
summary, **Setujui perubahan / Tolak perubahan**, readable expiration. IDs/digests/
hashes remain complete inside **Detail teknis**. Closing/minimizing panel only hides
presentation, not cancellation/decision; reopen shows the same run/plan/deadline.
Expiry disables decisions while hidden too; a pending badge/countdown stays reachable.

**After:** card uses actual typed facts: “Perubahan berhasil diverifikasi” only after
existing source/build/3-test verification; “Perubahan dibatalkan dan file dipulihkan”
only when recovery/baseline proof says restored. RecoveryConflict instead says file
changed externally and was preserved; OutputLimit says response was incomplete and
task failed. “Lihat hasil” opens real build/test/recovery summary + technical receipt.

```text
Wide: conversation (dominant)                 Review (only when relevant/open)
  chat / latest Agent activity                 [File] [Perubahan] [Hasil]
  …                                            exact diff + expiration/actions
  [Pilih proyek ▾] [capability hint]            [Detail teknis ▾]
  [Tanya] [Ubah kode]  ← explicit task choice
  existing composer / send / task-specific cancel
```

Wide default conversation100%, optional right review around35–40%, native splitter.
Use available content width, not raw window width. Below760 DIP: Chat primary,
**Tinjau perubahan (2)** tab/drawer opens the same review instance; composer project
row remains associated with input. No blank review pane, no duplicate controls or
loss of plan/draft/focus on resize. Verify900/1280/1440 windows, compact640/759 content,
and125/150% DPI. Escape hides panel and returns focus; it never implicitly grants.

## 6. Supported I1 vs future general-project functionality

|---|---|
| Ordinary Chat; explicit supported attachments; display selected location | Recursive project reads/indexing/repository understanding from a selected folder |
| Revalidated content from the same existing two authorized snapshots | Reading arbitrary paths or model-generated file lists |
| Explicit Alpha/Beta literals, one source plan/approval, fixed SDK/tests/recovery | Natural-language autonomous multi-project coding, arbitrary `.sln` verification |
| Host-fact latest activity/review/result in Chat-first shell | Shell/terminal/Git/MCP/auto-approval, general checkpoint restore or new editor |

The engineering workspace now has approved Alpha/Beta final hashes from the earlier
success. Original manifest still pins pre-task baseline; next admission correctly
denies drift. **Do not refresh manifest, reset source or broaden profile silently**.
New live edit baseline needs owner review separately. Chat-first UI can still be
tested without generation using prepared fixture plans and read-only receipt replay.

## 8. Security impact / state transitions

- Keep immutable binding tuple: Chat session/draft identity, canonical project,
  selection version, Agent SessionId/RunId, profile/workspace identity and actual
  plan/digest. Display labels and cached receipts never become grants.
- Ordinary Chat payload follows existing contract. Agent receives only the current
  explicit request/validated literals/scoped data; never Chat history/attachments.
- Switching project/session saves drafts, invalidates pending actions, and cancels/
  awaits existing Agent/recovery before rebinding. Ordinary Chat CTS is distinct;
  a late response/card remains tagged to its original session, not the newly selected
  project. Closing only a review panel does not revoke/recreate a valid run.
- No pause/refill of120 s when panel hides; SDK15 s and Job ownership remain unchanged.
  Approve routes only to existing exact pending provider; Enter-to-send cannot grant.
- Context snapshot content is explicit, bounded and revalidated; reject missing,
  stale/reparse/outside-root requests before read/send. File labels without content
  produce no claim of file-specific knowledge. Gamma remains protected/verified,
  not an extra browsable authorized target.
- Host outcome/build/TRX/recovery facts determine success text; model prose never
  changes task state. Full raw error/evidence stays in technical details; recovered
  baseline and RecoveryConflict are different user messages.

## 9. Focused acceptance / test plan

1. Fresh AI Workspace: Chat/composer visible, no mandatory literals/empty half panel,
   project selector immediately above input; Chat/AI Check/model behavior unchanged.
2. ProjectA/B switching preserves keyed text/literals/context and caret; no draft/
   file data/history leak across normal/temporary conversations. Unsupported project
   permits ordinary Chat but clearly denies edit/read-specific capabilities.
3. File explanation asserts actual allowed snapshot/explicit attachment enters
   payload; stale/missing/outside/reparse/root-drift cases fail closed. No fabricated
   explanation from project name; no directory scan/general read tool.
4. Explicit Ubah kode progressively reveals literals; request reaches existing Agent;
   all diffs shown before write, same RunId/plan/digest, one actual approval only.
5. Hide/reopen, narrow/wide switch, user tabs, theme changes and focus do not change
   plan/expiry. Expired, rejected, cancelled and stale UI cannot approve; user typing
   “setujui” in Chat and pressing Enter never authorizes a write.
6. Friendly conversation status exactly matches real build/3 tests/recovery facts;
   OutputLimit stays failure, recovered vs conflict differentiated, technical data
   complete/selectable. Header badge accessible while panel hidden.
7. Keyboard tab order, IME/Shift+Enter, focus restoration, announcements via WPF UIA
   (`AutomationProperties.LiveSetting`/existing accessibility resources), complete
   diff selection, splitter,900/1280/1440 and compact widths/three themes/DPI.
8. Preserve Agent/Chat cancellation isolation, navigation/model drain and default
   deny. Use existing successful I1 backend proof for unchanged execution; rerun only
   affected UI/router/context tests, not all350 or full Gate H certification.

Actual visual proof may use production App/MainWindow with clearly labelled
prepared UI-only plans/receipt replay and zero generation/mutation, as before.
Do not label replay as fresh functional execution. A fresh live source-edit proof,
if needed after context/routing changes, requires a valid reviewed baseline and
separate identity reconciliation, not a hidden reset/retry-until-pass.

## 10. Owner approval boundary / evidence and decisions

Requested **one CF1 approval**: chat-first composer/project restructuring, progressive
I1 intent, contextual panel/latest activity, Indonesian primary labels, session/project
draft/focus preservation and bounded explicit source-context bridge, on the ten
existing files + one test file in §7. This exceeds previous four layout files.

No application implementation in this audit. No test/generation/full certification
rerun; original interrupted patch and FAIL/PASS/visual evidence preserved. Remaining
Phase B admission test isolation and final footer visual verification are tracked,
not dismissed or converted to PASS. Gate H/I1 reports remain historical evidence.

External evidence folder:
`C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate-i-chat-first-review\` contains

# Gate I I1 — implementation report

13 Oktober 2026. **INTEGRATION IMPLEMENTED; UI ACCEPTANCE BLOCKED.**
Scope I1 disetujui owner; bukan arbitrary-project autonomous coding.
**Gate I belum dinyatakan selesai/PASS. Gate J HOLD.**

## 1. Delivered integration

AI Workspace sekarang memiliki entry **Agent (.cs)** dan child native WPF panel:
reviewed workspace picker, coding request, explicit Alpha/Beta Old/New literals,
Run/Cancel, state/countdown, read-only exact-plan diff, Approve/Reject, serta bounded
build/test/recovery diagnostics dan result receipt. Chat/AI Check renderer tetap sama.

Execution path:

```text
ConversationView → AgentWorkspaceView / AgentWorkspaceViewModel
→ AgentWorkspaceWorkflow (admission/session/run-local composition)
→ existing SharedRuntimeAgent / AgentOrchestrator / ToolExecutor
→ existing ApplySourcePlanTool
→ existing EditingWorkflowService / SafeWrite / SourceVerification / recovery
```

Tidak ada executor, writer, verifier, diff engine, runtime owner atau tool mutation
baru. Run-local instances memakai existing implementation dan singleton runtime,
server, HttpClient, native capability serta editing service. Global default catalog
tetap disabled; global approval provider tetap deny-write.

Reviewed project `D:\NEXUS\GateI-ReviewedWorkspace` dipersiapkan secara eksternal
dengan graph Product/Tests, SDK8.0.424 dan existing package versions. Manifest
15 graph/source inputs + SDK hash dipin host dalam `AgentWorkspaceWorkflow`:
`d0bf32ab5c0d6486e38302813661266e44426fb3d970e6fe962f3c7510189dbd`.
Lokasi/root saja tidak memberi authority; manifest drift, source drift atau project
lain ditolak. Setelah source berhasil berubah, baseline ini membutuhkan review baru,
bukan otomatis menerima hash baru.

## 2. Security and implementation changes

- Snapshot profile/root/targets/hashes, fresh Agent SessionId dan immutable user
  literals ditangkap sebelum run. RelevantContext hanya selected identities, tidak
  mengambil Chat history, attachments atau session lain.
- `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider` bind ke RunId dari existing RunStarted event dan
  exact profile instance. Grant harus cocok RunId/PlanId/digest; one-shot. Pending
  approval dibatalkan saat close/cancel/expiry; late/stale grant tidak berlaku.
- UI review berasal dari **actual prepared SourceEditPlan**, bukan diff buatan UI.
  Existing precommit source/graph/identity checks, backup/TxF atomic replace dan
  reverse hash-guarded recovery dipertahankan.
- Hard120 s termasuk human approval; native256, MaxRetries=0, satu source plan,
  automatic repair=0. Tidak ada paused/refilled deadline atau token escalation.
- Agent CTS terpisah dari Chat. Navigation cancel+await Agent/recovery; existing
  runtime switch/Stop revokes dan drains lease sebelum mengganti runtime. Fresh
  session/revision mengabaikan stale UI updates.
- UI success membutuhkan Completed + passed verification + final source facts,
  build PASS dan tiga required tests. OutputLimit/denied/failed/cancelled/timed-out
  tetap failure/status; bounded diagnostics menggunakan enum names yang terbaca.

**Existing files surgically modified** (repo-relative):

1. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/App.xaml.cs` — registrations dan explicit Conversation VM
   factory binding ke child Agent VM.
2. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/ViewModels/ConversationViewModel.cs` — child/open command
   dan navigation cancellation/drain.
3. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/ConversationView.xaml` — entry/child host saja.
4. `src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/ArchitectureGovernanceTests.cs` — runtime
   alias/default-deny/child binding regression.

**New files** under `src/03_App/`:

- `Nexus.App/Services/AgentWorkspaceWorkflow.cs`
- `Nexus.App/Services/AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider.cs`

Existing Agent/editing/profile/policy/runtime contracts dan application dependencies
tidak diubah. Existing staged work dan unrelated UI/UX tetap dipertahankan.
Enhanced Open Source: **USE** Nexus pipeline/diff/recovery/Job launcher;
**ADAPT** existing WPF semantic controls, DI, Dispatcher/CTS/TCS dan Windows UI
Automation untuk external proof; **ENHANCE** admission/approval/status binding.

Rekomendasi: satu workflow source-edit nyata melalui WPF pada **satu project
reviewed yang kompatibel dengan profile Gate H**, dua existing `.cs` files,
literal edits dari pengguna, exact review/approval, build/tests dan recovery.

coding pada arbitrary project belum feasible dalam authority tersertifikasi.

source dan menulis dokumen ini; belum ada integrasi Agent UI atau test/model run.
