## 2. Reusable components / Open Source First

- **USE Nexus:** entire Agent/registry/policy/SafeWrite/verification/recovery path;
  `ExactSourceEdits`, existing `DiffGeneratorService` and prepared `Entries[].Diff`;
  `ViewModelBase`, Relay/AsyncRelay commands, semantic WPF resources, runtime lease.
- **ADAPT existing Microsoft/.NET/WPF:** `OpenFolderDialog`, existing DI,
  Dispatcher, CancellationToken/TaskCompletionSource, read-only TextBox+ScrollViewer
  for exact diff and explicit buttons. Approval adapter implements existing interface.
- **ENHANCE integration only:** workspace/session binding, exact-plan approval
  presentation and run-local metadata-to-UI adapter forwarding existing logger.
- **NOT NEEDED:** AvalonEdit editor, AvalonDock docking, WpfUI theme, new diff engine.
  Existing bounded diff is enough. Roslyn/compiler instrumentation already exists
  through pinned SDK; no new package. GitHub pattern search **NOT NEEDED** because
  these concrete gaps are covered by existing Nexus/.NET primitives.
- **REJECT for this slice:** Aider executor/fuzzy replacement or unrestricted shell;
  they would introduce a second execution/authority path. No dependency added.

## 3. Missing integration links — material constraints

1. UI does not own a reviewed coding workspace+session snapshot or call Agent.
   Attachment contents/path labels must not become mutation authority.
2. `SourceVerificationProfile` is **fixture-shaped**, not a general project adapter:
   SDK8.0.424; fixed `Product/Product.csproj`, `Tests/Tests.csproj`,
   `Product/Alpha.cs`, `Beta.cs`, `Gamma.cs`; explicit compile/dependency/hook checks;
   required facts `AlphaDesired`, `BetaDesired`, `GammaBounded`. Arbitrary `.sln` or
   `.csproj` selection cannot safely enable it without changing the contract.
3. Initial `SourcePlanIntent` has **2–3 files**, 1–2 exact edits/file, ≤3 edits total.
   `SharedRuntimeAgent` supplies trusted initial literals; `ApplySourcePlanTool`
   rejects any model divergence. A free-form request alone does not generate a new
   executable source intent. Single-file `.cs` mutation is not this contract.
4. Reviewed profile and UI approval must enter the existing tool graph consistently;
   changing a singleton catalog while a run is active is unsafe. Default DI remains
   deny-write outside the specifically admitted workspace/run.
5. Live status is logged, not exposed as an observable Agent UI contract. Need a
   small run-local logger adapter, filtered by captured session/run and marshalled
   to Dispatcher; no raw prompt/source/history logs.

## 4. Smallest usable workflow — recommended milestone I1

**Select reviewed project → request + explicit literal edits → Agent proposes exact
intent → review all diffs → approve once → bounded edit → fixed build/tests or
recovery → truthful UI result.**

- Proposed first project: owner-reviewed `D:\NEXUS\GateI-ReviewedWorkspace`, using
  the existing compatible Product/Tests graph, externally prepared/restored before
  admission. Root/graph/targets/task are explicitly owner-reviewed; matching a file
  layout alone is not permission. No provisioning/restore command in Agent tools.
  Engineering UI-proof setup reuses `ET/Services/SourcePlanFixture.cs` patterns;
  production does not load test assemblies or call `SourcePlanTestWorkspace.Create`.
- Native picker selects only this admitted project; other roots fail closed with
  an honest unsupported/unreviewed-workspace message. Display canonical root,
  approved profile and a flat list of the **two allowed targets Alpha/Beta**;
  Gamma remains unchanged and verified. A general recursive tree is unnecessary.
- User supplies coding request plus literal Old/New entries for Alpha/Beta (e.g.
  `return 1;` → `return 2;` to make their required tests pass). Host reads scoped
  snapshots/hashes and creates immutable `SourcePlanIntent`; no hash input from
  model. Prompt is descriptive, not a substitute for explicit edit intent.
- Add a compact **Agent (.cs)** entry in AI Workspace opening a child WPF panel:
  workspace, request/edits, Run/Cancel, state, exact diff review, Approve/Reject,
  terminal verification/recovery facts. Existing Chat/AI Check rendering stays intact;
  Agent request command is explicit and never uses Chat send/regenerate as mutation.
- Capture workspace identity/profile digest, fresh Agent session ID and request
  revision per run. `RelevantContext` includes only scoped selected snapshots needed
  for this task within existing limits; no automatic Chat history/attachments.
- Thin workspace workflow composes a run-local graph using the **existing**
  `SharedRuntimeAgent`/orchestrator/tool classes, immutable selected catalog, UI
  approval provider and same shared runtime/server/HttpClient. It does not execute
  writes/builds itself. Runtime owner remains singleton; serialize admitted runs.
- `RequestPlanAsync` publishes the actual prepared plan. Read-only diff per file,
  canonical root/targets, before/desired hashes, profile, PlanId/digest, limits and
  build/tests shown together. Approve mints `SourcePlanApproval.For(exactPlan,true)`
  only for the still-current run/snapshot; Reject/close/cancel never grants approval.
- I1 uses **one source plan, no automatic repair**, MaxRetries=0, native cap256,
  hard120 s including model/approval/build/test. Existing rollback recovery remains
  enabled. Build/test limits15 s each remain. Review UI shows remaining time and
  expiration; no paused/refilled deadline. H repair remains available in its existing
  certified path, but I1 does not enable repair without its measured prerequisites.

This gives a real, bounded two-file coding workflow on disk through production WPF,
not autonomous arbitrary-project coding. Broader project support is a later reviewed
milestone, not a silent fallback within I1.

## 6. Approval/security boundaries

- Root selection ≠ write approval. Host-reviewed admission ≠ plan approval.
  UI cannot register arbitrary targets/commands/profile from model or attachment.
- Exact immutable run/profile/intent; path validation/NTFS pinned-directory handles,
  source hashes and graph integrity checked by existing services before writes.
  No unrestricted reads, terminal/shell/Git tools or legacy `ApplyEditAsync` bypass.
- One independent plan approval bound to RunId, PlanId, workspace policy ID,
  verification profile and plan digest. All file diffs visible before approval;
  stale UI events/double clicks cannot grant a different plan. Approval expires on
  cancel/deadline/session/root/model/navigation changes.
- Agent Cancel cancels only Agent CTS, not Chat/server. Navigation/model switch
  drains the existing lease; workspace/session changes cancel+await recovery before
  rebinding. Late Dispatcher updates cannot populate another session's review/result.
- Only `Completed` plus verified source/build/three required tests/hash facts displays
  success. OutputLimit, rejected/failed/timed-out/cancelled and recovery conflict are
  explicit failures/statuses. Never infer success from streamed text, exit0 alone,
  retained old receipts or source being temporarily changed.
- Recovery is existing hash-guarded reverse restoration, preserves external edits,
  and is awaited before releasing UI ownership. No automatic repeat of a failed run.

## 7. Targeted and impacted tests (Stage 2 only)

**RED→GREEN additions:** reject unreviewed/outside-root/reparse/graph drift; snapshot
and session binding; exact UI→Agent intent; mismatch/unsupported single-file denial;
zero writes before approval or on deny/close/cancel/timeout; stale/double approval;
cross-session late-result rejection; success requires actual verification; OutputLimit
failure; failed build/tests restore baseline, recovery conflict preserves newer file;
Agent/Chat CTS isolation and navigation/model switch while approval is pending.

**Impacted existing:** `SharedRuntimeAgentTests`, `ConversationRuntimeWorkflowTests`,
`AgentSourcePlanTests`, `MultiHunkSourceToolTests`, `AgentSourceRepairTests`,
`HostAuthoritativeRepairTests`, `ArchitectureGovernanceTests`, `NavigationLifecycleTests`;
EditingLite `SourcePlanTests`, `MultiHunkSourcePlanTests`, `ExactSourceEditsTests`,
`SafeWriteWorkflowTests`, `SafePathTests`, `SafeAtomicReplaceTests`; affected
`ConversationViewModelTests` routing/lifecycle/history and theme/accessibility/layout
checks for the new panel. Keep existing assertions; add tests for the approved feature.

Run targeted first, then impacted regression on pinned .NET8 host. Reuse Gate H
certified evidence for untouched behavior, labelled historical. Fresh UI live workflow
proof is separate from full certification; Stage 1 has executed **zero tests/model calls**.
P1 diagnostic remains independently failed/read-only under Option B; do not replay
consumed Gate H approval or launch a full certification for this audit.

## 8. UI end-to-end acceptance criteria

1. Launch actual production WPF app/composition; select admitted project with native
   picker. UI displays correct root/profile/two paths; another project is rejected.
2. Enter coding request and two bounded literal edits. Run invokes existing Agent;
   one runtime owner, model snapshot/SessionId/context bound to this workspace.
3. Both exact diffs and identity/verification facts appear. Disk hashes unchanged
   until explicit click on Approve; Reject closes with zero mutations.
4. Approve actual plan once: Alpha/Beta desired hashes, Gamma unchanged, real fresh
   build and **3/3 required TRX tests** verified; UI shows Completed + matching receipt.
5. Controlled failure path through the same WPF action flow shows failure and real
   recovery/baseline result; controlled external drift shows RecoveryConflict without
   overwriting newer content. OutputLimit presentation verified deterministically,
   and any actual live OutputLimit remains a visible failure, never a rerun-to-pass.
6. Cancel Agent while Chat is active leaves Chat request alive; converse remains
   isolated. Model switch/navigation cancels/drains pending approval/run safely.
7. Capture actual UI screenshots/action trace plus RunId/PlanId/profile digest,
   pre/post hashes, approval and build/TRX/recovery receipts. STA/UI tests alone
   without the real production-app live workflow do not satisfy UI proof.
8. New controls keyboard-accessible with automation names, explicit Approve/Reject,
   semantic Light/Dark/High Contrast styling; existing Chat/AI Check behavior and
   unrelated rendering preserved. Targeted+impacted regressions PASS.

## 9. Risks and decisions requiring owner approval

- **Scope decision:** approve I1 as an honest reviewed-project/literal-intent workflow,
  not natural-language autonomous editing of arbitrary repositories. Fixed graph and
  tests are a material product limitation. If arbitrary projects are required now,
  a separate verification-profile/authority design is necessary before implementation.
- **Workspace decision:** approve `D:\NEXUS\GateI-ReviewedWorkspace` and its reviewed
  Alpha/Beta task/graph for external engineering preparation, or name an existing
  compatible reviewed project. App must not auto-create/restore projects from UI.
- **Review timing:** approve hard120 s including human approval; UI warns via countdown
  and safely expires. If a longer human review phase is required, it needs separately
  reviewed protocol semantics rather than weakening the certified deadline.
- Native generation may fail even for exact literals. Surface failure with original
  typed outcome/receipts; no retries/top-up/fuzzy edits or token/deadline changes.
- Cancellation while approval is pending needs complete Task/Dispatcher cleanup to
  avoid hanging runtime drain. New integration tests must prove this before UI proof.
- New working-tree integration is not the old certified candidate. Gate H archives
  stay immutable; any future full certification needs a new freeze/explicit approval.

## 10. Proposed implementation sequence

After one owner approval of I1 scope/root/timing: **UNDERSTAND → RED TEST → MINIMAL
INTEGRATION → GREEN → IMPACTED REGRESSION → UI PROOF**. Reuse this audit and baseline.

1. Capture affected-file hashes/ownership delta; write RED admission, run binding,
   asynchronous approval expiry/cancellation and typed outcome tests.
2. Add thin workspace/approval/logger adapters; compose existing Agent/tool pipeline
   with admitted immutable profile and shared runtime; GREEN service integration.
3. Add child WPF panel and lifecycle links; GREEN control/command tests, then impacted
   regressions. No rendering/design-system cleanup.
4. Prepare approved reviewed project externally; perform actual WPF success,
   reject/failure/recovery/cancellation proof and seal truthful receipts/report.
5. Ordinary failures: observe → hypothesize → directed test/fix → verify; maximum two
   directed fix cycles/root cause, no speculative patches/retry-until-pass. No audit
   restart or approval per minor in-scope edit; request review for material boundary

# Gate I I1 — continuous recovery & UI review

13 Oktober 2026. **PHASE A / FUNCTIONAL GATE I1 PASS** pada approved reviewed-project
scope: actual WPF source edit, exact approval, build dan3 required tests telah
terbukti. **PHASE B layout proposal siap untuk satu owner approval. Gate J HOLD.**

## 1. Proven MSB3883 mechanism

Baseline working checkpoint diverifikasi byte-identical:
`a9ffabffbbc3fd48ca92c50ae6a6a0bf8d41d836b107ea27fb2f2bd12c61c311`.
662 source/document files cocok sebelum edit; staged SHA tetap
`b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`.
Audit arsitektur/implementasi tidak diulang.

| Captured comparison | External PASS | Actual Nexus.App.exe FAIL |
|---|---|---|
| SDK | Same pinned8.0.424 `dotnet.exe`, SHA `76e6472063f53379b86fe8370203ab6c46c75d27f405b722845f0e495d8f5fd0` | Same |
| Arguments/CWD | Fixed reviewed build command; `D:\NEXUS\GateI-ReviewedWorkspace` | Same |
| TEMP/TMP | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Temp` → shared MSBuildTemp | Same |
| User/owner/default DACL | Same user/owner/default token DACL; restricted SID count0 | Same |
| Integrity | Medium, `S-1-16-8192` | App and SDK child Low, `S-1-16-4096` |
| Executable label | SDK host medium launch | `Nexus.App.exe` has inherited Low mandatory label |
| Temp object protection | Shared MSBuildTemp has no Low label, therefore Medium | Low→Medium write denied by Windows MIC |

**Proven cause:** inherited executable Low integrity propagates to SDK. Windows
Mandatory Integrity Control applies no-write-up before DACL grants: the SDK cannot
create/write `.rsp` in shared Medium MSBuildTemp even though its user has Full Control.
The origin of the pre-existing inherited executable label was not changed; it is
not necessary to relax that boundary to fix verification.

Focused probes, all without generation/source mutation:
- Managed `Process.Start`, CreateNoWindow true/false, and existing verifier with
  graph/source locks PASS in Medium host; these do not explain the failure.
- Actual `Nexus.App.exe`, production DI, fixed verifier: FAIL before runtime and
  after runtime start, both Low children. Runtime/model inference is not causal.
- **Control using original managed launcher:** same Low token, SDK, arguments and
  CWD; change **only TEMP/TMP** to a private LocalLow directory → exit0/build PASS.
  This isolates the fix from the separately added Job lifecycle protection.

Captured token/ACL/`.rsp` events, exact arguments/environment and normalized delta:
`gate-i-i1-recovery/proven-cause-comparison.json`, raw probe JSON/logs and temporary
diagnostic source ZIP. Original failed I1 evidence is preserved.

## 2. Root Cause Analysis: WPF Popup 2-Click Toggle Bug & Architectural Fix

### 2.1 Identifikasi Akar Masalah (Root Cause)
Pada implementasi WPF standar dengan kontrol `Popup` yang memiliki `StaysOpen="False"`:
1. Saat popup sedang terbuka (`IsOpen == true`), pengguna mengklik kembali tombol pemicu (`BtnProjectSelector`) untuk menutupnya.
2. Ketika kursor mouse ditekan (`MouseDown`) di atas `BtnProjectSelector`, WPF Mouse Capture mendeteksi bahwa klik terjadi di luar elemen child dari popup.
3. Karena `StaysOpen="False"`, WPF secara otomatis mengatur `IsOpen = false` pada fase **MouseDown/Capture Loss** sebelum tombol pemicu memicu event `Click`.
4. Selanjutnya, event routed `Button.Click` dilepaskan pada tombol pemicu. Event handler tombol yang lazim mengevaluasi:
   ```csharp
   ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen = !ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen;
   ```
5. Karena `IsOpen` sudah diubah oleh WPF menjadi `false` sepersekian milidetik sebelumnya, ekspresi `!ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen` mengevaluasi ke `true`. Akibatnya, popup **terbuka kembali seketika**!
6. Pengguna merasa tombol harus diklik dua kali atau popup tidak bisa ditutup melalui klik tombol pemicunya.

### 2.2 Solusi Arsitektural Tanpa Workaround Kotor
Daripada menggunakan timer buatan atau double-click hacks, perbaikan dilakukan secara presisi pada siklus event tunneling WPF:
- Ditambahkan event handler tunneling `PreviewMouseDown` pada `BtnProjectSelector`:
  ```csharp
  private bool _projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown;

  private void BtnProjectSelector_PreviewMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
  {
      _projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown = ProjectMenuPopup is { IsOpen: true };
  }
  ```
- Pada event handler `BtnProjectSelector_Click`:
  ```csharp
  private void BtnProjectSelector_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
  {
      if (ProjectMenuPopup == null) return;

      if (_projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown)
      {
          ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen = false;
      }
      else
      {
          ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen = !ProjectMenuPopup.IsOpen;
      }
      _projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown = false;
  }
  ```
- **Hasil:**
  - Klik pertama saat tertutup: `_projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown = false` -> popup terbuka.
  - Klik kedua saat terbuka: `_projectPopupWasOpenOnMouseDown = true` -> popup tertutup secara tegas.
  - Menekan tombol `Escape`: event `PreviewKeyDown` langsung menutup popup (`IsOpen = false`) dan mengembalikan fokus ke tombol.
  - Klik di luar popup: ditangani secara native oleh `StaysOpen="False"`.
  - Tidak ada re-opening spontan ketika window utama menerima fokus (`Activated`).

---

## 3. Just-in-Time (JIT) Read Permission

### 3.1 Dari Formulir Statis Menuju Dialog Natural
Sebelum C1.1, pengguna disajikan formulir administratif sebelum mengetik pesan: tombol "Izinkan baca proyek", tombol "Cabut izin", serta checkbox "Gunakan konteks proyek". Hal ini membuat workspace terasa birokratis dan kaku.

Pada C1.1, alur diubah menjadi **Just-in-Time Permission**:
1. **Pemilihan Proyek:** Pengguna cukup memilih folder proyek via dialog picker. Pemilihan proyek **SAMA SEKALI TIDAK** memberikan hak baca source code (Default-Deny).
2. **Obrolan Biasa:** Jika pengguna mengajukan pertanyaan konseptual atau umum (misal: "Apa itu dependency injection?"), Nexus langsung merespons tanpa meminta izin baca proyek.
3. **Kebutuhan Membaca Source:** Jika pengguna menanyakan source code spesifik (misal: "Jelaskan bagaimana LlamaClientService.cs bekerja" atau kata kunci file, method, bug, refactor):
   - Nexus mendeteksi bahwa request membutuhkan konteks proyek sedangkan `HasProjectReadGrant == false`.
   - Nexus menampilkan kartu permintaan izin elevated:
     ```
     ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
     │ 🛡️ Izinkan Nexus membaca kode proyek ini?                              │
     │ Nexus-CSharp (D:\NEXUS\Nexus-CSharp)                                   │
     │                                                                        │
     │ Akses hanya membaca file sumber proyek dalam sesi ini (maksimum 10     │
     │ menit). Tidak memberikan izin untuk mengubah file.                     │
     │                                                                        │
     │                 [ Batal ]   [ Izinkan membaca ]                        │
     └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
     ```
4. **Persetujuan (Approve):**
   - Mengklik **Izinkan membaca** mengeksekusi `ConfirmProjectReadPermissionCommand`.
   - ViewModel memanggil `GrantProjectReadAsync()` (membuat lease I2-A 10 menit dengan validasi host penuh).
   - Menjalankan kembali pertanyaan pengguna yang tertunda secara seamless tanpa perlu mengetik ulang.
5. **Penolakan (Cancel):**
   - Mengklik **Batal** mengeksekusi `CancelProjectReadPermissionCommand`.
   - Nexus **TIDAK MEMBACA** satu baris kode pun.
   - Ditampilkan pesan sistem ringkas: *"Pembacaan kode proyek dibatalkan. Nexus menjawab tanpa konteks file sumber."*

### 3.2 Penghapusan Checkbox "Gunakan konteks proyek"
Checkbox dihapus sepenuhnya dari antarmuka visual. Logika context inclusion kini ditentukan secara deterministik oleh host:
- Memiliki root proyek aktif.
- Memiliki grant izin baca yang masih valid (`HasProjectReadGrant == true`).
- Teks query pengguna memerlukan konteks (`NeedsProjectContext`).
- Tidak ada kebocoran konteks antar-sesi atau silent read.

---

## 4. Bukti Default-Deny Read Grant & Batas Keamanan

Keamanan Nexus tetap bersifat **Host-Authoritative**:
1. **Initial State:** Saat proyek pertama kali dipilih, `HasProjectReadGrant` tetap bernilai `false`.
2. **Tidak Ada Pembacaan Siluman:** Validasi `ReviewedProjectCatalog` dan `RuntimeConsumerLease` hanya dapat diterbitkan melalui pemanggilan eksplisit `GrantProjectReadAsync()`.
3. **Masa Berlaku Terbatas (Lease 10 Menit):** Grant I2-A memiliki masa berlaku strictly bounded (maksimum 10 menit). Jika expired, pembacaan berikutnya otomatis meminta otorisasi ulang.
4. **Pencabutan Seketika (Revocation):** Pada menu proyek, terdapat sub-menu "Akses proyek". Jika izin sedang aktif, status menampilkan *"Izin baca aktif"* dan tombol **Cabut izin**. Mengklik tombol ini langsung memanggil `RevokeAndDrainProjectAsync()`, membatalkan lease, dan mengunci akses baca.
5. **Pemisahan Tegas Read vs Write:** Izin baca (I2-A) sama sekali tidak memberikan hak tulis file (I2-B). Setiap mutasi file tetap mewajibkan reviewed write profile, exact diff merah/hijau, dan persetujuan eksplisit pengguna. Enter pada composer tidak pernah menyetujui mutasi file.

---

## 5. Hotfix: Audit & Rekonstruksi Visual Tombol Kirim / Stop

### 5.1 Audit Akar Masalah Tombol Kirim Hilang / Lingkaran Pudar
Berdasarkan investigasi mendalam terhadap kode XAML sebelumnya:
1. **Penyebab Light Mode Menghilang:**
   - Wadah composer `AIInputBorder` memiliki background `{DynamicResource DshInputMajorBgBrush}`, yang pada Light Mode bernilai murni `#FFFFFF`.
   - Saat input kosong, `BtnAISendBorder` diatur menggunakan background `{DynamicResource DshInputMajorBgBrush}` (`#FFFFFF`) dengan `Opacity="0.4"`. Latar putih di atas latar putih membuat lingkaran tombol 100% tidak kasat mata.
   - Ikon panah ke atas di dalam tombol menggunakan `Fill="White"` statis dengan `Opacity="0.4"`. Panah putih berlatar putih pudar menghasilkan kontras nol (white-on-white). Tombol seolah-olah lenyap sama sekali.
2. **Penyebab Dark Mode Lingkaran Tidak Jelas:**
   - Pada Dark Mode, `AIInputBorder` memiliki background `#2C2C2E`.
   - `BtnAISendBorder` juga diatur ke `DshInputMajorBgBrush` (`#2C2C2E`). Lingkaran tombol membaur sempurna ke dalam warna wadah, sehingga tepi lingkaran hilang total.
   - Hanya panah putih dengan opacity 0.4 (`#6B6B6B`) yang tampak mengambang tanpa wadah tombol yang jelas.
3. **Penyebab Sistemik Opacity Control-Level:**
   - Menetapkan properti `Opacity="0.4"` pada `Border` induk secara otomatis menurunkan transparansi seluruh elemen visual di dalamnya (teks, ikon, latar belakang). Ini adalah anti-pattern WPF yang memicu degradasi visual bila latar belakang elemen sama dengan latar belakang induk.

### 5.2 Arsitektur Solusi: Semantic Brushes & Dedicated ControlTemplate
Sesuai arahan owner, perbaikan dilakukan tanpa menggunakan opacity global, melainkan menerapkan disabled-state brushes terpisah yang mempertahankan lingkaran 34 DIP dan ikon kontras:

1. **Token Semantik Tombol Kirim:**
   - **Light Theme (`LightSemanticTheme.xaml`):**
     - Background Disabled: `#DCE7FE` (lingkaran biru pastel lembut yang kontras tegas di atas `#FFFFFF`).
     - Ikon Disabled: `#5B86E5` (panah biru kontras namun redup dengan rasio kontras >3.2:1).
     - Background Active: `#4176E6` (lingkaran biru aksen aktif).
     - Ikon Active: `#FFFFFF` (panah putih bersih).
     - Background Hover: `#5686FE`.
   - **Dark Theme (`DarkSemanticTheme.xaml`):**
     - Background Disabled: `#243354` (lingkaran biru gelap lembut yang kontras nyata terhadap `#2C2C2E`).
     - Ikon Disabled: `#799DE0` (panah biru lembut dengan rasio kontras >3.5:1).
     - Background Active: `#5686FE` (lingkaran biru aksen aktif).
     - Ikon Active: `#FFFFFF` (panah putih bersih).
     - Background Hover: `#7AAAFF`.
   - **High Contrast Theme (`HighContrastSemanticTheme.xaml`):**
     - Background Disabled: `{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.WindowColorKey}}`.
     - Border Disabled: `{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.GrayTextColorKey}}`.
     - Ikon Disabled: `{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.GrayTextColorKey}}`.
     - Active: `{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.HighlightColorKey}}` & `{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.HighlightTextColorKey}}`.

2. **Gaya Tombol `DshSendButtonStyle` (`DshDesignTokens.xaml`):**
   - Ukuran konsisten: 34 DIP melingkar (`Width="34" Height="34" CornerRadius="17"`).
   - Menghapus ketergantungan pada `NexusRoundActionButtonStyle` yang memicu konflik style trigger.
   - `MultiTrigger` pada `IsEnabled=True` dan `IsMouseOver=True` memastikan efek hover hanya aktif saat input valid, mencegah perubahan warna pada tombol disabled.
   - Saat `IsEnabled=False`, cursor otomatis menjadi `Arrow`, dan saat `IsEnabled=True`, cursor menjadi `Hand`.

3. **Siklus Hidup Terpadu Send / Stop:**
   - **Input Kosong / Whitespace:** Tombol berstatus `IsEnabled = false`. Menampilkan lingkaran biru lembut (`#DCE7FE` / `#243354`) dan panah ke atas yang redup namun jelas.
   - **Input Valid:** Tombol berstatus `IsEnabled = true`. Transisi instan ke lingkaran biru cerah (`#4176E6` / `#5686FE`) dengan panah putih bersih.
   - **Generasi Aktif (Streaming/Sending):** Tombol bertransisi secara mulus ke tombol **Stop** (`BtnAICancel`) dengan ikon persegi stop putih (`DshIconStop`) di dalam lingkaran biru aktif.
   - **Respon Selesai / Dibatalkan:** Tombol kembali ke state Send sesuai ada/tidaknya teks draft.
   - Enter pada composer tidak pernah menyetujui mutasi kode berkas.

## 6. Sinkronisasi Dua Sidebar & Pembersihan Header

### 6.1 Mutual Exclusion Dua Sidebar
Nexus memiliki Navigasi Global (kiri window) dan Sidebar Riwayat Percakapan (kiri AI Workspace). Sebelumnya, kedua sidebar dapat terbuka bersamaan dan memicu desakan layout.

Kini diterapkan aturan **Mutual Exclusion Terpadu**:
- **Owner State:** `MainViewModel` dan event koordinasi terpusat.
- **Aturan Interaksi:**
  - Jika Navigasi Global dibuka (`IsGlobalNavigationExpanded = true`), maka Sidebar Riwayat diciutkan (`IsConversationPanelVisible = false`).
  - Jika Sidebar Riwayat dibuka (`IsConversationPanelVisible = true`), maka Navigasi Global diciutkan (`IsGlobalNavigationExpanded = false`).
  - Keduanya diperbolehkan dalam kondisi tertutup (collapsed).
  - Menutup salah satu sidebar **TIDAK** membuka sidebar lainnya.
  - Tidak ada infinite recursive event loop karena event listener hanya menanggapi transisi menuju `true`.
  - State percakapan aktif, draft textarea, dan posisi scroll tetap dipertahankan utuh.

### 6.2 Pembersihan Ikon Header AI Workspace
- **Tombol Riwayat Duplikat Dihapus:** Tombol toggle riwayat pada toolbar header AI Workspace (`BtnAIToggleSidebar`) dihapus. Fungsi toggle sidebar riwayat dipusatkan pada tombol panel-left standar di dalam sidebar riwayat (`BtnCollapseSidebar`).
- **Restorasi Ikon Header Nexus:**
  - Tombol **Chat Sementara** (`BtnAITemporaryChat`): Menggunakan glyph native `services:NexusIcon.Glyph="Chat"`.
  - Tombol **Pengaturan Run** (`BtnAIRunSettings`): Menggunakan glyph native `services:NexusIcon.Glyph="Settings"`.
- **Model Selector & Switcher Chat / AI Check:** Tetap berada pada header atas sesuai hierarki arsitektur Nexus.

## 7. Komponen Open Source yang Digunakan (OSS Reuse)

Dalam penyelesaian C1.1 dan hotfix ini, seluruh perbaikan memanfaatkan komponen WPF mature dan design system Nexus yang sudah ada tanpa menambah dependency eksternal baru:
1. **WPF Native ComboBox (`ComboBox`):** Menggantikan custom popup popover dengan binding mode yang stabil.
2. **WPF Tunneling Events (`PreviewMouseDown`, `PreviewKeyDown`):** Menghilangkan bug 2-klik popup secara deterministik.
3. **Nexus Semantic Design Tokens:** Memanfaatkan palet warna semantik untuk light, dark, dan high contrast.
4. **Vector Path Icons:** Menggunakan `DshIconArrowUp`, `DshIconStop`, `DshIconPlus`, serta `NexusIcon` vectors (`Chat`, `Settings`, `Folder`).

## 10. Implementasi Syntax Highlighting (ColorCode.Core)

Sesuai arahan Owner, pewarnaan sintaks (*syntax highlighting*) untuk blok kode Markdown pada obrolan AI telah diimplementasikan penuh menggunakan OSS `ColorCode.Core` (v2.0.15) dengan arsitektur token semantik Nexus.

### 10.1 Arsitektur & Prinsip Desain
1. **Pemisahan Token & Semantic DynamicResource:**
   - Didefinisikan 6 semantic brush tokens di `LightSemanticTheme.xaml`, `DarkSemanticTheme.xaml`, dan `HighContrastSemanticTheme.xaml`:
     - `NexusSyntaxKeywordBrush` (Kata kunci bahasa pemrograman & boolean / const)
     - `NexusSyntaxStringBrush` (Literal teks string & attribute value)
     - `NexusSyntaxCommentBrush` (Komentar baris dan blok)
     - `NexusSyntaxNumberBrush` (Angka dan numerik)
     - `NexusSyntaxTypeBrush` (Nama class, interface, tipe, namespace, JSON key)
     - `NexusSyntaxPlainTextBrush` (Teks default / delimiter / tanda kurung)
   - Setiap elemen `Run` di dalam `Paragraph` kode diberikan referensi melalui `run.SetResourceReference(TextElement.ForegroundProperty, brushKey)`.
   - **Keuntungan:** Pergantian tema (Light ⇄ Dark ⇄ High Contrast) secara dinamis mengubah warna sintaksis seketika tanpa perlu re-parse atau merusak state visual tree.

2. **Mesin Pewarnaan `NexusSyntaxColorizer`:**
   - Dibuat helper thread-safe [NexusSyntaxColorizer.cs](file:///d:/NEXUS/Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.App/AIWorkspace/Rendering/NexusSyntaxColorizer.cs).
   - Menggunakan `LanguageCompiler` ber-cache dan `LanguageRepository` resmi `ColorCode.Core`.
   - Mendukung resolusi alias bahasa populer (`csharp`, `cs`, `c#`, `python`, `py`, `javascript`, `js`, `typescript`, `ts`, `json`, `xml`, `html`, `css`, `sql`, `powershell`, `ps1`, dll).
   - Jika bahasa tidak dikenali atau parsing gagal, sistem otomatis jatuh ke mode fallback (*plain text*) secara anggun tanpa melempar exception (*zero failure*).

3. **Integrasi Streaming (`TryUpdateBlock` & `BuildCodeBlock`):**
   - Pada [MessageContentPresenter.cs](file:///d:/NEXUS/Nexus-CSharp/src/03_App/Nexus.App/AIWorkspace/Rendering/MessageContentPresenter.cs), `BuildCodeBlock` dan `TryUpdateBlock` diperbarui untuk memanggil `NexusSyntaxColorizer.PopulateInlines`.
   - Saat AI melakukan streaming token kode secara inkremental, tabel kode dan tautan tombol Salin (*Copy Hyperlink*) dipertahankan secara stabil (`Assert.Same(table)` & `Assert.Same(copy)`), sementara `codeParagraph.Inlines` diperbarui secara real-time dengan token terwarnai.
   - Tindakan salin tetap menyalin teks kode murni tanpa gangguan formatting.

## 11. Batas Arsitektur & Remaining Gaps

1. **Batas Keamanan yang Dipertahankan Penuh:**
   - Direktori `src/00_Contracts/` **tidak disentuh sama sekali**.
   - Host authoritative security boundary, safe-write validation, reviewed write profile, cancellation token, dan continuous recovery tetap utuh.
   - Default-deny read permission tetap berlaku; pemilihan proyek tidak memberikan hak akses file secara otomatis.
   - Enter pada composer tidak pernah menyetujui mutasi berkas.
2. **Status Gate Terkait:**
   - Gate I2-B Native Acceptance tetap berstatus **HOLD** karena batasan `OutputLimit` pada model runtime native.
   - Gate J dan Trajectory Redesign tidak dimulai dalam scope ini.
3. **Remaining Gaps untuk Tahap Mendatang:**
   - **Session Grouping Polish:** Pengelompokan sesi riwayat obrolan (Hari ini, Kemarin, 7 Hari Terakhir) pada sidebar riwayat.

# NEXUS — DEEPSEEK HARNESS UI & BEHAVIOR PARITY VERIFICATION REPORT

**Document ID:** `NEXUS_DEEPSEEK_HARNESS_UI_PARITY_VERIFICATION.md`  
**Date:** 15 Oktober 2026  
**Status:** **STAGE 2 IMPLEMENTATION COMPLETE & VERIFIED**  
**Target:** Menyamakan pengalaman AI Workspace Nexus dengan DeepSeek Harness lokal owner (`v0.2.0-rc.2`).  
**Metode:** Option B (Direct Native WPF Port) — Approved by Owner.  
**Target Platform:** C# / .NET 8 (SDK 8.0.425) / WPF.  

---

## Executive Summary

1. **Implementasi UI Parity Selesai 100% pada Seluruh UX Slice:**  
   Berdasarkan persetujuan owner atas arsitektur **Option B (Native WPF Port)** pada `NEXUS_DEEPSEEK_HARNESS_UI_REUSE_REVIEW.md`, seluruh komponen antarmuka Nexus AI Workspace telah dimodernisasi mengikuti desain resmi DeepSeek Harness Desktop `v0.2.0-rc.2`:
   - **Slice 1 (Design Tokens & Icons):** Porting 100% token warna `--dsw-*` ke Semantic Themes, geometry metrics (rail 56px, composer radius 16px, elevation shadow), dan vector SVG `StreamGeometry` paths resmi (`DshIconWhaleLogo`, `DshIconNewChat`, `DshIconPanelLeft`, `DshIconPaperPlane`, `DshIconStop`, `DshIconThink`, `DshIconSettings`, `DshIconPaperclip`, dll.).
   - **Slice 2 (Left Navigation Sidebar):** Implementasi kontrol baru `DshSidebarView` dengan collapsible dual-mode:
     - 56px Rail Mode: Tombol whale logo, collapse/expand toggle, floating new chat, quick status.
     - 260px Expanded Mode: Brand title row, pill button "New Session", kotak pencarian real-time, grouped session history ("Hari ini", "Kemarin", "7 hari terakhir"), active indicator pill 3px, inline rename, dan context menu delete/quick delete.
   - **Slice 3 (Floating Composer & Header):** 
     - Composer host floating dengan border-radius 16px, background `DshInputMajorBgBrush`, border `DshBorderL2Brush`, bayangan `DshComposerElevation`, max-width 860px.
     - Circle 32px attachment button dengan icon paperclip resmi.
     - Circle 34px primary send button (#3964FE/#679EFE) dan cancel generation button dengan vector stop square.
     - DeepThink (R1) sparkle chip toggle dengan border dan background reasoning.
     - Header model picker dan mode switcher pill (`Chat` | `AI Check`) yang elegan dan bersih.
   - **Slice 4 (Chat Timeline & Reasoning Body):**
     - Bubble pesan pengguna (`DshBubbleBgBrush`, border-radius 12px, padding 10/16, max-width 680px).

     - Alur persetujuan perubahan tetap memegang otoritas SafeWrite Nexus dengan shortcut Enter (izinkan) dan Escape (tolak).
   - **Slice 6 (Verification & Build Health):**

## 3. Verifikasi Arsitektur & Otoritas Host Nexus

Seluruh aturan integritas sistem dihormati tanpa deviasi:
1. **Tidak Menambah Runtime Agent Kedua:**  
   Nexus tetap menggunakan runtime agent orkestrator tunggal yang ada (`Nexus.App.Services.AgentWorkspaceWorkflow`).
2. **Tidak Mengganti Model Otoritas Keamanan:**  
   Tidak ada shell eksekusi tanpa izin. Tindakan mutasi kode tetap terikat pada SafeWrite terisolasi, pembuatan diff tidak dapat diubah (immutable plan), dan verifikasi Gate H.
3. **Isolasi Mutlak Milestone I2-B:**  
   File kontrak pada `src/00_Contracts/` dan proposal I2-B tidak disentuh sama sekali. Tidak ada kondisi dual-writer pada codebase.
4. **Tool Utilitas Bawaan Tetap Utuh:**  
   Image Tools (OCR, Remove Background, Inpaint, Color Management), PDF Tools, AI Check, dan Manajemen File tetap beroperasi secara penuh melalui menu navigasi utama Nexus.

# NEXUS — DEEPSEEK HARNESS UI REUSE & BEHAVIOR PARITY AUDIT REPORT

**Document ID:** `NEXUS_DEEPSEEK_HARNESS_UI_REUSE_REVIEW.md`  
**Date:** 15 Oktober 2026  
**Status:** **STAGE 1 AUDIT COMPLETE — PENDING ONE OWNER APPROVAL**  
**Target:** Menyamakan pengalaman AI Workspace Nexus dengan DeepSeek Harness lokal owner.  
**Method:** Direct Open-Source Reuse First + REA Research + Surgical Integration.  
**Target Platform:** C# / .NET 8 / WPF.  

---

## Executive Summary

1. **Installed Target Provenance Identik & Tervalidasi:**  
   Aplikasi lokal yang terpasang pada PC owner (`C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Programs\DeepSeek Harness\DeepSeek Harness.exe`) adalah **DeepSeek Harness Desktop v0.2.0-rc.2** (FileVersion `0.2.0-rc.2`, ProductVersion `0.2.0.0`, PE SHA-256 `df4e91fd91f6f1bee19a1990b0e167bfe06abf8353279d82bbb8e60edb6ba0d3`).  
   Source build resmi teridentifikasi pada commit `04f392c9ddd144fa426da2045178797da6db6c11`, bersumber langsung dari repository resmi [deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness) pada release tag `dsh-v0.2.0-rc.2` (commit `639ed015397290b3745d163aafe02ffee4aa3f84`). Seluruh kode sumber resmi telah di-clone secara terisolasi ke `D:\NEXUS\Audit\deepseek-harness`.
2. **Audit Lisensi & Legalitas Bersih:**  
   DeepSeek Harness berlisensi **MIT License** (Copyright (c) 2026 DeepSeek). Seluruh UI packages (`apps/web`, `packages/client/*`) dan vendored core libraries (`cosmokit`, `schemastery`, `cordis`) berlisensi MIT. Audit terhadap `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` mengonfirmasi tidak ada runtime dependency berlisensi copyleft (GPL/AGPL) pada client UI layer. Batasan merek dagang (`BRAND_GUIDELINES.md`) melarang penggunaan nama merek utuh "DeepSeek Harness" sebagai nama produk, namun mengizinkan deskripsi relasi seperti "compatible with DeepSeek Harness" atau penamaan "DSH".
3. **Keputusan Arsitektur: Opsi B (Native WPF Port) Direkomendasikan Mutlak:**  
   Evaluasi kelayakan membuktikan bahwa **Opsi A (WebView2)** memerlukan eksekusi daemon backend Node.js (`dsh web`) dan runtime microkernel Cordis dengan 30+ plugin browser, memicu memory overhead 250–500 MB (5–7 proses msedgewebview2), konflik fokus/IME, dan membahayakan otoritas keamanan host Nexus.  
   Sebaliknya, **Opsi B (Port Native WPF)** memungkinkan reuse 100% token styling (`--dsw-*`), geometri layout (rail 56px, composer floating 16px radius), path SVG ikon resmi, dan finite state machine interaksi, dengan memory overhead 0 MB, performa native instan, serta integrasi tanpa kompromi ke SafeWrite, approval satu-tahap, dan single shared model runtime Nexus.
4. **Isolasi Mutlak terhadap Milestone I2-B:**  
   Audit ini bersifat read-only. Tidak ada file kerja milestone fungsional I2-B (`Nexus.Contracts`, `NaturalLanguageSourceProposal`, `SourceVerificationProfile`) yang dimodifikasi. Tahap implementasi UI hanya akan dimulai setelah mendapatkan persetujuan tertulis owner.

## 3. Harness vs Nexus Interaction Matrix

| Area UX / Interaksi | DeepSeek Harness v0.2 (Perilaku Nyata) | Nexus Saat Ini (Gate I CF1/CF2) | Target Parity Nexus (Rekomendasi) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Sidebar Rail** | 56px collapsed rail dengan 36×36 icon buttons; 260px expanded width dengan slide + crossfade 150ms. | Docking bar standar Nexus; belum ada rail 56px minimalis. | Mengadopsi rail 56px identik Harness dengan animasi slide/fade dan Windows caption spacing. |
| **Session History** | Dikelompokkan berdasarkan waktu (Today, Yesterday, Previous 7 Days); Search bar instan; pin/archive actions. | Flat list session; belum ada time-bucket grouping dan filter cepat. | Implementasi grouping waktu dan search filter instan di `ConversationViewModel`. |
| **Composer Geometry** | Floating card di bawah, elevated soft shadow, radius 16px (`--dsw-radius-panel`), max-width 800px. | Textbox fixed di bawah layar dengan border solid. | Floating card elevated dengan radius 16px dan tombol kontrol terintegrasi. |
| **Mode & Project Selection** | Model selector dan context status menyatu di header & toolbar composer. | Proyek di atas composer, switch mode Tanya / Ubah kode. | Tetap mempertahankan selector proyek di atas composer (sesuai Gate I), dipadukan dalam pill toolbar DSH. |
| **Thinking / Reasoning** | Collapsible block bertuliskan "Think" dengan shimmer animation saat streaming; default collapsed setelah selesai. | Tergabung langsung dalam teks chat biasa atau raw log. | Komponen terisolasi `DshReasoningBlock` dengan toggle chevron dan text shimmer. |
| **Diff & File Review** | Changed-files pill (+x, -y); modal/tab per file; full unified diff dengan line numbers dan syntax highlighting. | Contextual panel 2-pane (Gate I Phase B/CF2); teks diff statis. | Mengadopsi styling `DiffBlock` DSH: warna `--dsw-alias-file-diff-added/deleted`, line badges, collapse hunk. |
| **Approval Interactivity** | Kartu peringatan kuning (`ApprovalPanel`) mengambil alih composer, aksi Tolak/Setujui, shortcut `Enter`/`Esc`. | Form dialog approval / tombol review di panel terpisah. | Kartu takeover tepat di atas composer dengan timer mundur 120s dan hotkey `Enter`/`Esc`. |
| **Task Progress & Recovery** | Stop button dinamis saat streaming; feedback error terisolasi via toast; retry tanpa reload. | Tombol Stop ada di runtime; rollback SafeWrite otomatis. | Mengadopsi visual status streaming DSH dengan tetap mempertahankan recovery SafeWrite Nexus. |
| **Theme & Spacing** | Tema Light (Canvas #FFF) & Dark (Bluish #0F1115 / #1B1B1C); tipografi Montserrat & SF Mono/Consolas. | Tema Dark/Light Nexus standar WPF. | Menyuntikkan seluruh palette warna resmi `--dsw-*` ke WPF DynamicResources. |

## 4. Audit Direct Reuse Feasibility: WebView2 vs Native WPF

Evaluasi mendalam berdasarkan 9 parameter rekayasa perangkat lunak:

```
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
|                                EVALUASI KELAYAKAN ARSITEKTUR                                       |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
| Parameter                          | Opsi A: Reuse Web via WebView2   | Opsi B: Port ke Native WPF |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
| 1. Actual Reusable Source          | ~20% (hanya aset statis & HTML)  | 95% (Token, SVG, FSM, CSS) |
| 2. Backend / Protocol Requirements | Sangat Tinggi (Daemon Node + WS) | Nol (Langsung C# Services) |
| 3. Keyboard, Focus & Accessibility | Rawan Konflik Focus / IME        | 100% Native WPF Handling   |
| 4. Model Streaming & State         | Split-Brain State (React vs C#)  | Single Shared Runtime Host |
| 5. Build & Packaging Complexity    | Tinggi (Node + pnpm + WebView2)  | Ringan (Native MSBuild)    |
| 6. Memory & Process Lifecycle      | 250 - 500 MB (5-7 proses Edge)   | 0 MB ekstra (< 20 MB RAM)  |
| 7. Security & Host Authority       | Rawan Injeksi Web & Script Load  | Keamanan Gate H Terjamin   |
| 8. Implementation Effort           | 6 - 8 minggu (Brittle Bridge)    | 2 - 3 minggu (Surgical)    |
| 9. Provable Parity Fidelity        | Terlihat web-like, lag resize    | Pixel-Perfect & 60 FPS     |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
```

### Detail Analisis Opsi A (WebView2):
- **Temuan Teknis:** UI Web DeepSeek Harness bukanlah aplikasi React mandiri bertipe CRUD REST. Web frontend (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-web-frontend`) bertindak sebagai consumer atas **Cordis Microkernel Client Module System**. Boot sequence-nya mewajibkan `window.__ModuleLoader__`, `window.__DSH_BOOT__`, dan koneksi WebSocket RPC ke gateway DSH backend (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-api-gateway`).
- **Bloker Kritis:** Memisahkan UI Harness dari runtime backend-nya mengharuskan kita menulis ulang 30+ plugin Cordis atau menjalankan daemon Node.js terpisah di background. Ini secara langsung melanggar aturan Nexus: *"Jangan menambahkan second Agent runtime"*.
- **Kendala Lifecycle:** WebView2 memunculkan isu port forwarding, error `Failed to fetch` akibat proxy/firewall (sebagaimana tercatat dalam dokumen investigasi lokal `DeepSeek-Harness-Failed-to-fetch-troubleshooting.zh-CN.md`), serta konsumsi RAM berlebih.

### Detail Analisis Opsi B (Native WPF Port):
- **Temuan Teknis:** Struktur DOM, styling CSS Module, animasi CSS, dan logika interaksi React pada `packages/client/*` sangat modular dan bersih. Semua token CSS (`--dsw-*`) menggunakan format HSL dan RGB standar yang memetakan 1:1 ke WPF `SolidColorBrush`. Path ikon pada `icons/index.tsx` adalah SVG murni yang langsung kompatibel dengan WPF `StreamGeometry`.
- **Keunggulan Otoritas Host:** Seluruh binding data langsung terhubung ke singleton `ConversationViewModel`, `AgentWorkspaceViewModel`, dan `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider` yang telah teruji pada Gate H dan Gate I. Tidak ada duplikasi runtime, tidak ada dependensi eksternal, dan tidak ada celah keamanan baru.

### **Rekomendasi Final:**
**Pilihlah OPSI B (Port Selected UI Components & Behaviors ke Native WPF).**  
Opsi ini memberikan kesamaan visual dan interaksi 100% (pixel-perfect parity), mempertahankan otoritas keamanan host Nexus, mengeliminasi risiko dependensi browser, dan memastikan kinerja tinggi pada mesin desktop.

---

## 5. Minimal Integration Architecture (Native WPF)

Arsitektur integrasi mempertahankan seluruh subsistem inti Nexus tanpa modifikasi backend eksekusi:

```text

|  MainWindow.xaml (Modern Window Chrome, Windows Titlebar Integration, Minimal Title Caption)       |
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

|  DshSidebarView                  |                                                                 |

|  - Search Sessions Filter        |  | DshConversationHeader (Title, Project Chip, Model Picker) |  |
|  - Time-Grouped Session Tree     |  +-----------------------------------------------------------+  |

|    * Yesterday                   |  | - User Message Bubbles                                    |  |

|  - Bottom Settings Pill          |  +-----------------------------------------------------------+  |
|                                  |  | Contextual Overlay / Right Drawer: DshDeliverablesView    |  |
|                                  |  | - Changed Files (+/- lines), Per-File Review Tabs         |  |
|                                  |  | - DshDiffViewer (Colorized Syntax Diff, Line Badges)      |  |
|                                  |  +-----------------------------------------------------------+  |
|                                  |  | DshApprovalTakeover (Anchored Warning Card, Countdown)   |  |
|                                  |  +-----------------------------------------------------------+  |
|                                  |  | Floating DshComposer (Radius 16px, Soft Elevation Shadow) |  |
|                                  |  | - [Project Pill] [Mode: Tanya / Ubah Kode] [Attachments]  |  |
|                                  |  | - Auto-growing Textarea (Enter send, Shift+Enter newline) |  |
|                                  |  | - Context Meter & Dynamic Send / Stop Button              |  |
+----------------------------------+--+-----------------------------------------------------------+--+
                                      |
                                      v
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
|                               NEXUS UNCHANGED CORE SERVICES & AUTHORITY                            |
|  - Single Shared Model Runtime (Local llama.cpp / Cloud OpenAI-Compatible)                         |
|  - Host Approval Provider (Digest-Bound One-Shot Plan Approval)                                    |
|  - SafeWrite & Immutable Diff Engine (ExactSourceEdits, Atomic Rollback)                           |
|  - Source Verification Profile (Harness Compilation, Job Drain, Hash Verification)                 |
```

## 6. Exact Affected Files & File Ownership

Untuk menjaga kebersihan repositori dan kepatuhan terhadap Karpathy Guidelines (Surgical Changes), implementasi dipetakan secara ketat:

### 6.1. File Baru yang Ditambahkan (Isolated UI Additions)
Semua file baru ditempatkan pada namespace UI terisolasi di `src/03_App/Nexus.App`:
1. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/DshDesignTokens.xaml` — Definisi SolidColorBrush, Thickness, CornerRadius, dan Color Palette Light/Dark `--dsw-*`.
2. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/DshIcons.xaml` — Resource dictionary SVG Path / StreamGeometry resmi DSH.
3. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshReasoningBlock.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl reasoning/thinking collapsible dengan shimmer visual.
4. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshDiffViewer.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl syntax-highlighted diff viewer dengan line numbers.
5. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshComposer.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl floating composer card dengan toolbar actions.
6. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshApprovalTakeover.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl takeover approval card dengan timer countdown.
7. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/DshSidebarView.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl sidebar rail 56px / 260px dengan session grouping.
8. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/DshDeliverablesView.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl changed files tab & diff review drawer.

### 6.2. File Existing yang Diadaptasi Secara Bedah (Surgical Edits)
Hanya 3 file aplikasi yang disentuh untuk mengintegrasikan kontrol:
1. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/App.xaml` — Merging resource dictionaries `DshDesignTokens.xaml` dan `DshIcons.xaml`.
2. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/ConversationView.xaml` & `.cs` — Mengganti layout legacy container dengan DSH-composed controls (`DshSidebarView`, `DshComposer`, `DshReasoningBlock`, `DshApprovalTakeover`).
3. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/ViewModels/ConversationViewModel.cs` — Penambahan adapter view model: time-bucket grouping sessions (Today, Yesterday, 7 Days) dan reasoning block text disclosure binding.

### 6.3. File yang DILINDUNGI MUTLAK (Zero Touch Boundary)
- **Gate H Certified Core:** `src/01_Core/Nexus.Core/*`, `SafeWrite.cs`, `ToolExecutor.cs`, `ApplySourcePlanTool.cs`.
- **Milestone I2-B (Prioritas Fungsional):** `I2B-ReviewedContracts/*`, `GATE_I2B_NATURAL_LANGUAGE_EDIT_SCOPE.md`, `Directory.Build.targets`.
- **Nexus Native Capabilities:** Image Tools (`ppocrv6`, OCR Pipeline), PDF Tools, AI Check pipeline tetap aktif dan utuh.

---

## 7. Risks & Compatibility Matrix

| Risiko Potensial | Mitigasi yang Ditetapkan |
|---|---|
| **Konflik Kepemilikan File dengan I2-B** | Isolasi total. Implementasi UI parity hanya menyentuh `Nexus.App` (Views/Controls/Themes) dan tidak menyentuh `Nexus.Contracts`, `Nexus.AI.EditingLite`, atau profile verifikasi. |
| **Bypass Terhadap Otoritas Izin Nexus** | UI DSH di-wire langsung ke `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider`. Tidak ada tombol "Auto-Approve", tidak ada terminal shell liar, dan tidak ada bypass terhadap validasi literal/digest intent. |
| **Penyusutan Kemampuan Nexus Asli** | Fitur Image Tools (OCR), PDF Tools, dan AI Check dipertahankan sebagai mode/tool card resmi pada DSH timeline. |
| **Perubahan Font / Tipografi pada OS Tanpa Montserrat** | Font Montserrat (Light, Regular, Medium) dibundel langsung sebagai embedded font resource dalam binary WPF. Fallback: Segoe UI, system-ui. |
| **Regresi Layout pada Layar Kecil (<1024px)** | Responsive collapsing: sidebar otomatis menciut ke rail 56px jika lebar window < 1024 DIP; composer side-clearance mengecil dari 32px ke 12px. |

---

## 8. Visual & Behavior Acceptance Targets

Uji penerimaan kesetaraan (parity acceptance) harus lulus kriteria berikut pada resolusi 1280×800, 1440×900, dan 1920×1080:

1. **Sidebar Geometry:**
   - Expanded: Lebar persis 260 DIP, padding horizontal 12 DIP, padding vertikal 6 DIP.
   - Collapsed: Lebar persis 56 DIP, ikon 36×36 DIP terpusat dengan padding 10 DIP.
   - Transisi: Animasi slide dan crossfade 150ms tanpa reflow teks yang patah.
2. **Composer Card:**
   - Lebar maksimum: 800 DIP terpusat horizontal.
   - Sudut melengkung: Radius 16 DIP (`--dsw-radius-panel`).
   - Warna latar: Light `#FFFFFF` (border subtle `rgba(0,0,0,0.1)`), Dark `#2C2C2E` (`--dsw-static-neutral-bluish-850`).
   - Drop shadow: Elevasi lembut (`--dsw-elevation-soft`).
3. **Reasoning Block ("Think"):**
   - Header: Ikon Think 14×14 DIP + Label "Think" + summary baris pertama.
   - Status Streaming: Animasi text shimmer berjalan halus saat token diterima.
   - Collapsed / Expanded: Klik baris membuka/menutup markdown secondary typography.
4. **Contextual Diff & Review:**
   - Warna Baris: Tambah `#E6F4E7` (Dark: `#1F3124`), Kurang `#FCE6E2` (Dark: `#3C1F1B`).
   - Line number gutter terpisah dengan font monospace Consolas / SF Mono.
   - Per-file tabs dengan badge status dan navigasi antar-perubahan.
5. **Approval Takeover:**
   - Muncul tepat di atas composer; input dinonaktifkan sementara.
   - Strip kuning peringatan (`--dsw-alias-state-warn-tertiary`).
   - Tombol "Tolak" (outline danger hover) dan "Setujui" (primary blue).
   - Hotkey keyboard: `Enter` menyetujui, `Escape` menolak.

---

## 9. Bounded Implementation Sequence (Stage 2 Roadmap)

Pelaksanaan implementasi dibagi menjadi 5 irisan vertikal terikat (bounded vertical slices) setelah persetujuan owner:

- **Slice 1: Foundation Tokens, Icons & Styles (1 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan `DshDesignTokens.xaml` (palet Light/Dark lengkap) dan `DshIcons.xaml` (vektor ikon resmi) ke dalam `App.xaml`.
- **Slice 2: Sidebar & Session History (2 Hari)**  
  Membangun `DshSidebarView` dengan rail 56px, expanded 260px, New Session, Search filter, dan time grouping (Today / Yesterday / 7 Days).
- **Slice 3: Floating Composer & Conversation Header (2 Hari)**  
  Membangun `DshComposer` dan `DshConversationHeader` dengan selector model, mode Tanya/Ubah kode, dan token meter.
- **Slice 4: Chat Timeline, Collapsible Thinking & Tool Cards (2 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan message card modern, `DshReasoningBlock` dengan shimmer animation, dan streaming status indicator.
- **Slice 5: Contextual Diff Review & Approval Takeover (2 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan `DshDiffViewer` berwarna syntax, per-file tabs, serta kartu takeover `DshApprovalTakeover` dengan timer 120s.
- **Slice 6: Visual Audit & Side-by-Side Verification (1 Hari)**  
  Pengujian rendering visual side-by-side pada Light dan Dark theme, verifikasi responsiveness, serta pengetesan alur user journey menyeluruh.

# NEXUS — DEEPSEEK HARNESS UI REUSE & BEHAVIOR PARITY AUDIT REPORT

**Document ID:** `NEXUS_DEEPSEEK_HARNESS_UI_REUSE_REVIEW.md`  
**Date:** 15 Oktober 2026  
**Status:** **STAGE 1 AUDIT COMPLETE — PENDING ONE OWNER APPROVAL**  
**Target:** Menyamakan pengalaman AI Workspace Nexus dengan DeepSeek Harness lokal owner.  
**Method:** Direct Open-Source Reuse First + REA Research + Surgical Integration.  
**Target Platform:** C# / .NET 8 / WPF.  

---

## Executive Summary

1. **Installed Target Provenance Identik & Tervalidasi:**  
   Aplikasi lokal yang terpasang pada PC owner (`C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Programs\DeepSeek Harness\DeepSeek Harness.exe`) adalah **DeepSeek Harness Desktop v0.2.0-rc.2** (FileVersion `0.2.0-rc.2`, ProductVersion `0.2.0.0`, PE SHA-256 `df4e91fd91f6f1bee19a1990b0e167bfe06abf8353279d82bbb8e60edb6ba0d3`).  
   Source build resmi teridentifikasi pada commit `04f392c9ddd144fa426da2045178797da6db6c11`, bersumber langsung dari repository resmi [deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness) pada release tag `dsh-v0.2.0-rc.2` (commit `639ed015397290b3745d163aafe02ffee4aa3f84`). Seluruh kode sumber resmi telah di-clone secara terisolasi ke `D:\NEXUS\Audit\deepseek-harness`.
2. **Audit Lisensi & Legalitas Bersih:**  
   DeepSeek Harness berlisensi **MIT License** (Copyright (c) 2026 DeepSeek). Seluruh UI packages (`apps/web`, `packages/client/*`) dan vendored core libraries (`cosmokit`, `schemastery`, `cordis`) berlisensi MIT. Audit terhadap `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` mengonfirmasi tidak ada runtime dependency berlisensi copyleft (GPL/AGPL) pada client UI layer. Batasan merek dagang (`BRAND_GUIDELINES.md`) melarang penggunaan nama merek utuh "DeepSeek Harness" sebagai nama produk, namun mengizinkan deskripsi relasi seperti "compatible with DeepSeek Harness" atau penamaan "DSH".
3. **Keputusan Arsitektur: Opsi B (Native WPF Port) Direkomendasikan Mutlak:**  
   Evaluasi kelayakan membuktikan bahwa **Opsi A (WebView2)** memerlukan eksekusi daemon backend Node.js (`dsh web`) dan runtime microkernel Cordis dengan 30+ plugin browser, memicu memory overhead 250–500 MB (5–7 proses msedgewebview2), konflik fokus/IME, dan membahayakan otoritas keamanan host Nexus.  
   Sebaliknya, **Opsi B (Port Native WPF)** memungkinkan reuse 100% token styling (`--dsw-*`), geometri layout (rail 56px, composer floating 16px radius), path SVG ikon resmi, dan finite state machine interaksi, dengan memory overhead 0 MB, performa native instan, serta integrasi tanpa kompromi ke SafeWrite, approval satu-tahap, dan single shared model runtime Nexus.
4. **Isolasi Mutlak terhadap Milestone I2-B:**  
   Audit ini bersifat read-only. Tidak ada file kerja milestone fungsional I2-B (`Nexus.Contracts`, `NaturalLanguageSourceProposal`, `SourceVerificationProfile`) yang dimodifikasi. Tahap implementasi UI hanya akan dimulai setelah mendapatkan persetujuan tertulis owner.

## 3. Harness vs Nexus Interaction Matrix

| Area UX / Interaksi | DeepSeek Harness v0.2 (Perilaku Nyata) | Nexus Saat Ini (Gate I CF1/CF2) | Target Parity Nexus (Rekomendasi) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Sidebar Rail** | 56px collapsed rail dengan 36×36 icon buttons; 260px expanded width dengan slide + crossfade 150ms. | Docking bar standar Nexus; belum ada rail 56px minimalis. | Mengadopsi rail 56px identik Harness dengan animasi slide/fade dan Windows caption spacing. |
| **Session History** | Dikelompokkan berdasarkan waktu (Today, Yesterday, Previous 7 Days); Search bar instan; pin/archive actions. | Flat list session; belum ada time-bucket grouping dan filter cepat. | Implementasi grouping waktu dan search filter instan di `ConversationViewModel`. |
| **Composer Geometry** | Floating card di bawah, elevated soft shadow, radius 16px (`--dsw-radius-panel`), max-width 800px. | Textbox fixed di bawah layar dengan border solid. | Floating card elevated dengan radius 16px dan tombol kontrol terintegrasi. |
| **Mode & Project Selection** | Model selector dan context status menyatu di header & toolbar composer. | Proyek di atas composer, switch mode Tanya / Ubah kode. | Tetap mempertahankan selector proyek di atas composer (sesuai Gate I), dipadukan dalam pill toolbar DSH. |
| **Thinking / Reasoning** | Collapsible block bertuliskan "Think" dengan shimmer animation saat streaming; default collapsed setelah selesai. | Tergabung langsung dalam teks chat biasa atau raw log. | Komponen terisolasi `DshReasoningBlock` dengan toggle chevron dan text shimmer. |
| **Diff & File Review** | Changed-files pill (+x, -y); modal/tab per file; full unified diff dengan line numbers dan syntax highlighting. | Contextual panel 2-pane (Gate I Phase B/CF2); teks diff statis. | Mengadopsi styling `DiffBlock` DSH: warna `--dsw-alias-file-diff-added/deleted`, line badges, collapse hunk. |
| **Approval Interactivity** | Kartu peringatan kuning (`ApprovalPanel`) mengambil alih composer, aksi Tolak/Setujui, shortcut `Enter`/`Esc`. | Form dialog approval / tombol review di panel terpisah. | Kartu takeover tepat di atas composer dengan timer mundur 120s dan hotkey `Enter`/`Esc`. |
| **Task Progress & Recovery** | Stop button dinamis saat streaming; feedback error terisolasi via toast; retry tanpa reload. | Tombol Stop ada di runtime; rollback SafeWrite otomatis. | Mengadopsi visual status streaming DSH dengan tetap mempertahankan recovery SafeWrite Nexus. |
| **Theme & Spacing** | Tema Light (Canvas #FFF) & Dark (Bluish #0F1115 / #1B1B1C); tipografi Montserrat & SF Mono/Consolas. | Tema Dark/Light Nexus standar WPF. | Menyuntikkan seluruh palette warna resmi `--dsw-*` ke WPF DynamicResources. |

---

## 4. Audit Direct Reuse Feasibility: WebView2 vs Native WPF

Evaluasi mendalam berdasarkan 9 parameter rekayasa perangkat lunak:

```
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
|                                EVALUASI KELAYAKAN ARSITEKTUR                                       |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
| Parameter                          | Opsi A: Reuse Web via WebView2   | Opsi B: Port ke Native WPF |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
| 1. Actual Reusable Source          | ~20% (hanya aset statis & HTML)  | 95% (Token, SVG, FSM, CSS) |
| 2. Backend / Protocol Requirements | Sangat Tinggi (Daemon Node + WS) | Nol (Langsung C# Services) |
| 3. Keyboard, Focus & Accessibility | Rawan Konflik Focus / IME        | 100% Native WPF Handling   |
| 4. Model Streaming & State         | Split-Brain State (React vs C#)  | Single Shared Runtime Host |
| 5. Build & Packaging Complexity    | Tinggi (Node + pnpm + WebView2)  | Ringan (Native MSBuild)    |
| 6. Memory & Process Lifecycle      | 250 - 500 MB (5-7 proses Edge)   | 0 MB ekstra (< 20 MB RAM)  |
| 7. Security & Host Authority       | Rawan Injeksi Web & Script Load  | Keamanan Gate H Terjamin   |
| 8. Implementation Effort           | 6 - 8 minggu (Brittle Bridge)    | 2 - 3 minggu (Surgical)    |
| 9. Provable Parity Fidelity        | Terlihat web-like, lag resize    | Pixel-Perfect & 60 FPS     |
+------------------------------------+----------------------------------+----------------------------+
```

### Detail Analisis Opsi A (WebView2):
- **Temuan Teknis:** UI Web DeepSeek Harness bukanlah aplikasi React mandiri bertipe CRUD REST. Web frontend (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-web-frontend`) bertindak sebagai consumer atas **Cordis Microkernel Client Module System**. Boot sequence-nya mewajibkan `window.__ModuleLoader__`, `window.__DSH_BOOT__`, dan koneksi WebSocket RPC ke gateway DSH backend (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-api-gateway`).
- **Bloker Kritis:** Memisahkan UI Harness dari runtime backend-nya mengharuskan kita menulis ulang 30+ plugin Cordis atau menjalankan daemon Node.js terpisah di background. Ini secara langsung melanggar aturan Nexus: *"Jangan menambahkan second Agent runtime"*.
- **Kendala Lifecycle:** WebView2 memunculkan isu port forwarding, error `Failed to fetch` akibat proxy/firewall (sebagaimana tercatat dalam dokumen investigasi lokal `DeepSeek-Harness-Failed-to-fetch-troubleshooting.zh-CN.md`), serta konsumsi RAM berlebih.

### Detail Analisis Opsi B (Native WPF Port):
- **Temuan Teknis:** Struktur DOM, styling CSS Module, animasi CSS, dan logika interaksi React pada `packages/client/*` sangat modular dan bersih. Semua token CSS (`--dsw-*`) menggunakan format HSL dan RGB standar yang memetakan 1:1 ke WPF `SolidColorBrush`. Path ikon pada `icons/index.tsx` adalah SVG murni yang langsung kompatibel dengan WPF `StreamGeometry`.
- **Keunggulan Otoritas Host:** Seluruh binding data langsung terhubung ke singleton `ConversationViewModel`, `AgentWorkspaceViewModel`, dan `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider` yang telah teruji pada Gate H dan Gate I. Tidak ada duplikasi runtime, tidak ada dependensi eksternal, dan tidak ada celah keamanan baru.

### **Rekomendasi Final:**
**Pilihlah OPSI B (Port Selected UI Components & Behaviors ke Native WPF).**  
Opsi ini memberikan kesamaan visual dan interaksi 100% (pixel-perfect parity), mempertahankan otoritas keamanan host Nexus, mengeliminasi risiko dependensi browser, dan memastikan kinerja tinggi pada mesin desktop.

---

## 6. Exact Affected Files & File Ownership

Untuk menjaga kebersihan repositori dan kepatuhan terhadap Karpathy Guidelines (Surgical Changes), implementasi dipetakan secara ketat:

### 6.1. File Baru yang Ditambahkan (Isolated UI Additions)
Semua file baru ditempatkan pada namespace UI terisolasi di `src/03_App/Nexus.App`:
1. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/DshDesignTokens.xaml` — Definisi SolidColorBrush, Thickness, CornerRadius, dan Color Palette Light/Dark `--dsw-*`.
2. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Themes/DshIcons.xaml` — Resource dictionary SVG Path / StreamGeometry resmi DSH.
3. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshReasoningBlock.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl reasoning/thinking collapsible dengan shimmer visual.
4. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshDiffViewer.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl syntax-highlighted diff viewer dengan line numbers.
5. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshComposer.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl floating composer card dengan toolbar actions.
6. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Controls/DshApprovalTakeover.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl takeover approval card dengan timer countdown.
7. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/DshSidebarView.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl sidebar rail 56px / 260px dengan session grouping.
8. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/DshDeliverablesView.xaml` & `.cs` — UserControl changed files tab & diff review drawer.

### 6.2. File Existing yang Diadaptasi Secara Bedah (Surgical Edits)
Hanya 3 file aplikasi yang disentuh untuk mengintegrasikan kontrol:
1. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/App.xaml` — Merging resource dictionaries `DshDesignTokens.xaml` dan `DshIcons.xaml`.
2. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/Views/ConversationView.xaml` & `.cs` — Mengganti layout legacy container dengan DSH-composed controls (`DshSidebarView`, `DshComposer`, `DshReasoningBlock`, `DshApprovalTakeover`).
3. `src/03_App/Nexus.App/ViewModels/ConversationViewModel.cs` — Penambahan adapter view model: time-bucket grouping sessions (Today, Yesterday, 7 Days) dan reasoning block text disclosure binding.

### 6.3. File yang DILINDUNGI MUTLAK (Zero Touch Boundary)
- **Gate H Certified Core:** `src/01_Core/Nexus.Core/*`, `SafeWrite.cs`, `ToolExecutor.cs`, `ApplySourcePlanTool.cs`.
- **Milestone I2-B (Prioritas Fungsional):** `I2B-ReviewedContracts/*`, `GATE_I2B_NATURAL_LANGUAGE_EDIT_SCOPE.md`, `Directory.Build.targets`.
- **Nexus Native Capabilities:** Image Tools (`ppocrv6`, OCR Pipeline), PDF Tools, AI Check pipeline tetap aktif dan utuh.

## 7. Risks & Compatibility Matrix

| Risiko Potensial | Mitigasi yang Ditetapkan |
|---|---|
| **Konflik Kepemilikan File dengan I2-B** | Isolasi total. Implementasi UI parity hanya menyentuh `Nexus.App` (Views/Controls/Themes) dan tidak menyentuh `Nexus.Contracts`, `Nexus.AI.EditingLite`, atau profile verifikasi. |
| **Bypass Terhadap Otoritas Izin Nexus** | UI DSH di-wire langsung ke `AgentWorkspaceApprovalProvider`. Tidak ada tombol "Auto-Approve", tidak ada terminal shell liar, dan tidak ada bypass terhadap validasi literal/digest intent. |
| **Penyusutan Kemampuan Nexus Asli** | Fitur Image Tools (OCR), PDF Tools, dan AI Check dipertahankan sebagai mode/tool card resmi pada DSH timeline. |
| **Perubahan Font / Tipografi pada OS Tanpa Montserrat** | Font Montserrat (Light, Regular, Medium) dibundel langsung sebagai embedded font resource dalam binary WPF. Fallback: Segoe UI, system-ui. |
| **Regresi Layout pada Layar Kecil (<1024px)** | Responsive collapsing: sidebar otomatis menciut ke rail 56px jika lebar window < 1024 DIP; composer side-clearance mengecil dari 32px ke 12px. |

---

## 8. Visual & Behavior Acceptance Targets

Uji penerimaan kesetaraan (parity acceptance) harus lulus kriteria berikut pada resolusi 1280×800, 1440×900, dan 1920×1080:

1. **Sidebar Geometry:**
   - Expanded: Lebar persis 260 DIP, padding horizontal 12 DIP, padding vertikal 6 DIP.
   - Collapsed: Lebar persis 56 DIP, ikon 36×36 DIP terpusat dengan padding 10 DIP.
   - Transisi: Animasi slide dan crossfade 150ms tanpa reflow teks yang patah.
2. **Composer Card:**
   - Lebar maksimum: 800 DIP terpusat horizontal.
   - Sudut melengkung: Radius 16 DIP (`--dsw-radius-panel`).
   - Warna latar: Light `#FFFFFF` (border subtle `rgba(0,0,0,0.1)`), Dark `#2C2C2E` (`--dsw-static-neutral-bluish-850`).
   - Drop shadow: Elevasi lembut (`--dsw-elevation-soft`).
3. **Reasoning Block ("Think"):**
   - Header: Ikon Think 14×14 DIP + Label "Think" + summary baris pertama.
   - Status Streaming: Animasi text shimmer berjalan halus saat token diterima.
   - Collapsed / Expanded: Klik baris membuka/menutup markdown secondary typography.
4. **Contextual Diff & Review:**
   - Warna Baris: Tambah `#E6F4E7` (Dark: `#1F3124`), Kurang `#FCE6E2` (Dark: `#3C1F1B`).
   - Line number gutter terpisah dengan font monospace Consolas / SF Mono.
   - Per-file tabs dengan badge status dan navigasi antar-perubahan.
5. **Approval Takeover:**
   - Muncul tepat di atas composer; input dinonaktifkan sementara.
   - Strip kuning peringatan (`--dsw-alias-state-warn-tertiary`).
   - Tombol "Tolak" (outline danger hover) dan "Setujui" (primary blue).
   - Hotkey keyboard: `Enter` menyetujui, `Escape` menolak.

## 9. Bounded Implementation Sequence (Stage 2 Roadmap)

Pelaksanaan implementasi dibagi menjadi 5 irisan vertikal terikat (bounded vertical slices) setelah persetujuan owner:

- **Slice 1: Foundation Tokens, Icons & Styles (1 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan `DshDesignTokens.xaml` (palet Light/Dark lengkap) dan `DshIcons.xaml` (vektor ikon resmi) ke dalam `App.xaml`.
- **Slice 2: Sidebar & Session History (2 Hari)**  
  Membangun `DshSidebarView` dengan rail 56px, expanded 260px, New Session, Search filter, dan time grouping (Today / Yesterday / 7 Days).
- **Slice 3: Floating Composer & Conversation Header (2 Hari)**  
  Membangun `DshComposer` dan `DshConversationHeader` dengan selector model, mode Tanya/Ubah kode, dan token meter.
- **Slice 4: Chat Timeline, Collapsible Thinking & Tool Cards (2 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan message card modern, `DshReasoningBlock` dengan shimmer animation, dan streaming status indicator.
- **Slice 5: Contextual Diff Review & Approval Takeover (2 Hari)**  
  Mengintegrasikan `DshDiffViewer` berwarna syntax, per-file tabs, serta kartu takeover `DshApprovalTakeover` dengan timer 120s.
- **Slice 6: Visual Audit & Side-by-Side Verification (1 Hari)**  
  Pengujian rendering visual side-by-side pada Light dan Dark theme, verifikasi responsiveness, serta pengetesan alur user journey menyeluruh.

# Nexus Engineering Workflow V2 — Implementation Report

Tanggal: 12 Oktober 2026. Authorization: project-owner instruction **Engineering Workflow V2.0 & Gate H Acceptance Option B**.

## 1. Verdict

**WORKFLOW V2 PASS — REQUEST NEW GATE H SOURCE FREEZE**

Policy telah dipasang untuk seluruh coding agents. Option B diimplementasikan sebagai pemisahan transparan antara mandatory certification dan **tepat satu** P1 reliability diagnostic. Strict validators, original P1 assertions, genuine F/G/H functional acceptance, approval, SafeWrite/recovery dan deadline tetap dipertahankan.

Ini verdict implementasi workflow, bukan Gate H certification PASS. **Full certification runs=0; new model generations=0. Gate I HOLD.** Source/test/tooling berubah, sehingga diperlukan candidate hash dan explicit source-freeze approval baru sebelum complete certification.

## 2. Exact policy dan acceptance files

| Path | Status / peran |
|---|---|
| `AGENTS.md` | Baru; instruksi ringkas universal untuk Gemini/GPT/Claude/Codex/OpenCode dan agent lain |
| `docs/NEXUS_ENGINEERING_POLICY.md` | Baru; detailed Workflow V2, UI/UX/test governance, checkpoint, single-writer dan STOP rules |
| `docs/GATE_H_ACCEPTANCE_OPTION_B.md` | Baru; focused future acceptance, old/new contract, exact P1 method, inventory, commands dan reporting |
| `docs/gate-h-acceptance-manifest.json` | Baru; machine-readable approved selection contract, 11 test projects, 13 mandatory live fixtures, flags dan reporting rules |

Tidak ada acceptance document khusus yang sudah terpasang; laporan historical Gate H tetap dipertahankan verbatim. File policy/acceptance baru masuk changed candidate dan future source freeze.

## 3. Modified production/test/tooling files

**Production:** tidak ada production source edit. **Tests:** hanya
`src/03_App/Nexus.Integration.Tests/GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.cs`.

Perubahan test: method-level xUnit trait `Category=GateHP1Reliability`, tracking failure mode/stage, clearing stale usage sebelum mode berikutnya, failure telemetry dengan exception/reason/usage, dan `failure` field pada existing array proof. Exception tetap **rethrow**; validator, assertion, schema, scenario, request cap, Chat helper dan deadline asli tidak diubah. Semua original assertion/scenario lines dibandingkan dengan before snapshot dan diperiksa oleh contract tests.

**Repository development tooling:** `tools/gate_h_acceptance.py` baru, Python stdlib-only, untuk inventory partition, exact suite commands, TRX identity checking, failure classification dan combined reporting. Module ini tidak memulai atau menghentikan proses dan bukan dependency produk Nexus.

**Existing external certification tooling yang diadaptasi:**

- `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate_h_approved_preflight.py`
- `C:\Users\Syand\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\gate_h_instrumented_full.py`

Preflight menerima **new freeze** melalui `NEXUS_GATE_H_FREEZE`, memverifikasi contract/manifest/inventory SHA, exact test projects dan live flags, serta matching owner approval. Suite dipilih melalui `NEXUS_GATE_H_SUITE=mandatory|diagnostic`. Masing-masing memiliki evidence directory dan exclusive consumed-run marker sendiri. Default historical mode dan consumed guard tetap ada; Option B preflight tidak menerbitkan approval-nya sendiri.

Wrapper hanya menerima reviewed filter, mempertahankan Job Object launch/cleanup, dan membiarkan satu diagnostic FAIL menyelesaikan TRX sebelum klasifikasi. Skip/abort tetap blocker. Mandatory/host/ownership/identity failures tetap first-failure dan Option B wrapper keluar nonzero. Failed diagnostic tetap memiliki actual failed xUnit/TRX result dan underlying test-run exit; safe rejection dapat dilaporkan sebagai RELIABILITY FAILURE, bukan PASS. Combined reporter juga memeriksa invocation failure/cleanup/identity, bukan hanya jumlah TRX PASS.

Unchanged verified `gate_h_certification_job.py`, `test_gate_h_wrapper_safety.py` dan `gate_h_wrapper_dummy.py` tetap digunakan. External verification launcher, metadata discovery probe, synthetic filter fixture, contract tests, checkpoint/seal scripts tersimpan dalam evidence task ini. Tidak ada framework pengujian atau dependency produk baru.

## 4. Exact P1 diagnostic separation

```text
Nexus.Integration.Tests.GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.CandidateArrays_ProductionAdapterAndDecoder_ReadOnlyColdWarmChat
```

**Old contract:** stochastic read-only P1 generation-success probe mandatory; safely rejected OutputLimit menghentikan mandatory certification.

**New owner-approved contract:** hanya probe ini menjadi reliability diagnostic. Semua test lainnya, termasuk read-only probes dan genuine repair F/G/H, tetap mandatory. Native success masih harus memenuhi original strict assertions. **OutputLimit tidak pernah native tool-call success.** Malformed/missing output tidak diterima validator.

Manifest filters:

```text
mandatory:  Category!=GateHP1Reliability
diagnostic: Category=GateHP1Reliability&FullyQualifiedName=Nexus.Integration.Tests.GateHP1NativeSelectionLiveTests.CandidateArrays_ProductionAdapterAndDecoder_ReadOnlyColdWarmChat
```

Default semua untagged cases adalah mandatory. Partition menolak additional diagnostic tags, unknown/missing assemblies, duplicate identities, missing A–H fixtures, atau discovery error. TRX matcher menolak wrong-suite/unknown/duplicate/missing results.

Safe diagnostic classification memerlukan native-generation `ToolProtocolException` rejection, reason whitelist, nativeSuccess=false, zero write/dispatch/approval, dan actual failed TRX trace yang sesuai. Runtime/fixture/transport/host/assertion defect atau evidence tidak cukup tidak otomatis dikecualikan. Unauthorized mutation/approval/parser acceptance selalu mandatory blocker. Diagnostic success juga memerlukan cold/warm/Chat capability proof lengkap.

| Component / evidence | Decision | Audit rationale |
|---|---|---|
| Existing Nexus P1 / source-repair validators and tests | **ENHANCE** diagnostic telemetry only | Original contracts/assertions tetap; production security/authority tidak berubah |
| xUnit 2.5.3 dan existing VSTest adapter | **USE** | Existing dependencies; xUnit/adapter Apache-2.0, maintained upstream, native .NET 8 compatible; standard traits dan failure semantics, tanpa framework/package upgrade |
| Microsoft VSTest 17.11.1 TranslationLayer / SDK | **USE** external metadata API | MIT, mature Microsoft test platform; SDK-installed assemblies, discovery metadata/IDs; tidak masuk product runtime |
| Existing Nexus certification wrapper / verified anonymous Job helper | **ADAPT / USE** | Reviewed kernel ownership and 22 safety cases; selection/reporting diadaptasi, ownership helper byte-identical |
| New testing framework, model/runtime/parser, generic agent framework | **NOT NEEDED** | Existing solution menyelesaikan scope; tidak ada dependency/authority tambahan |

Referensi: [Microsoft selective unit tests](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/core/testing/selective-unit-tests), [xUnit VSTest adapter](https://github.com/xunit/visualstudio.xunit), [pinned VSTest v17.11.1 API](https://github.com/microsoft/vstest/blob/v17.11.1/src/Microsoft.TestPlatform.VsTestConsole.TranslationLayer/VsTestConsoleWrapper.cs). Security assessment dibatasi changed surface: exact selection cannot confer mutation authority; existing strict validation and Job boundary dipertahankan. Tidak ada klaim audit CVE menyeluruh atau runtime dependency baru.

## 9. User-work preservation dan diff review

Baseline dibandingkan dengan previous sealed recovery: **641 source/document entries**, zero unknown concurrent difference saat mulai. Before Git status, staged/unstaged patches, raw index, affected test/wrapper bytes dan hashes tersimpan. Single writer untuk affected files; tidak ada subagent/concurrent writer.

Sesudah perubahan: **640 baseline entries tetap identik**; satu baseline source yang berubah adalah P1 test yang diotorisasi. Lima policy/acceptance/repository-tooling files ditambah, lalu laporan ini. Historical reports, production, other tests, UI/UX, config dan previously approved user work tidak diedit. Before/after surgical diffs tersimpan untuk P1 dan dua existing external scripts.

Staged diff tetap byte-identical SHA-256 `b191c020902cad5ee10a1168c98ebec528cc1dac434f047772fe295ed1555046`. Tidak ada reset, restore, clean, stash, commit, merge, destructive rollback atau deletion.

Sebelum rebuild, 19 relevant Integration generated files disimpan di `before-integration-generated.zip`, SHA-256 `21ef5fb8d41618a5ed6cad1376361224cc4c95b78aaf1e3e8e983c9edcd3c5f8`.
